TriSplat: Simulation-Ready Feed-Forward 3D Scene Reconstruction
Authors: Weijie Wang, Zimu Li, Jinchuan Shi, Zeyu Zhang, Botao Ye, Marc Pollefeys, Donny Y. Chen, Bohan Zhuang
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:53+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://lhmd.top/trisplat, Code: https://github.com/ziplab/TriSplat
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sparse-view 3D reconstruction is increasingly addressed with feed-forward splatting networks that predict explicit primitives directly from images. Yet most existing methods remain centered on Gaussian primitives and expose surfaces only indirectly: extracting a usable mesh for downstream simulation, physics reasoning, or embodied interaction still requires expensive post-hoc steps that break the feed-forward promise. This limitation is especially pronounced in pose-free settings, where scene structure and camera parameters must be estimated jointly from sparse observations. We present TriSplat, a feed-forward reconstruction network that represents scenes with oriented triangle primitives and directly exports simulation-ready mesh scenes from a single forward pass. Given input images, the network predicts local 3D point maps, triangle attributes, camera poses, and optional intrinsics. Rather than regressing triangle orientation as an unconstrained latent variable, our approach constructs geometry normals from the predicted point maps, refines them with an image-conditioned normal head, and converts them into stable local frames for triangle parameterization. A mono-normal bootstrap schedule further stabilizes early training, while opacity and blur scheduling progressively sharpens the learned surface representation for direct mesh extraction. Experiments on RealEstate10K and DL3DV show that this representation produces more geometry-faithful reconstructions than Gaussian feed-forward baselines while maintaining competitive novel-view rendering quality. Because the rendering primitives are themselves surface triangles, the output can be directly ingested by physics engines, collision detectors, and standard rendering pipelines without any conversion, making it a practical simulation-ready solution for feed-forward 3D scene reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TriSplat：面向模拟的前馈3D场景重建
稀疏视角3D重建正越来越多地采用前馈splatting网络，该网络直接从图像预测显式几何体。然而，现有方法大多仍以高斯体为基础，仅间接暴露表面：为下游模拟、物理推理或具身交互生成可用网格仍需昂贵的后处理步骤，违背了前馈承诺。这一局限在无姿态设定中尤为明显，此时需从稀疏观测中联合估计场景结构与相机参数。我们提出TriSplat，一种前馈重建网络，以定向三角形原语表示场景，并在单次前向传递中直接导出可直接用于模拟的网格场景。给定输入图像，网络预测局部3D点图、三角形属性、相机姿态及可选内参。不同于将三角形朝向作为无约束潜在变量回归，我们的方法从预测点图构建几何法线，通过图像条件法线头进行优化，并将其转换为稳定的局部坐标系以参数化三角形。单法线引导训练进一步稳定早期训练，而不透明度与模糊度调度逐步锐化学习到的表面表示，以直接提取网格。在RealEstate10K和DL3DV上的实验表明，该表示在保持竞争力的新型视角渲染质量的同时，比高斯前馈基线生成更符合几何的重建结果。由于渲染原语本身即为表面三角形，输出可直接被物理引擎、碰撞检测器和标准渲染管线使用，无需任何转换，使其成为前馈3D场景重建的实用模拟解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for simulation-ready 3D reconstructions without post-processing, TriSplat introduces a feed-forward network that directly outputs oriented triangle primitives from sparse images, enabling immediate mesh extraction for physics and rendering. The method predicts point maps, triangle attributes, camera poses, and intrinsics, then derives stable local frames from geometry normals refined by an image-conditioned head, using a mono-normal bootstrap and opacity/blur scheduling to stabilize training and sharpen surfaces. Experiments on RealEstate10K and DL3DV demonstrate that TriSplat outperforms Gaussian-based feed-forward methods in geometric fidelity while maintaining competitive novel-view rendering quality, and its triangle-based output is directly usable by simulation engines without conversion.
为实现无需后处理的仿真级3D场景重建，TriSplat提出一种前馈网络，直接输出定向三角形基元并从稀疏图像生成可用网格。不同于高斯基元，它通过预测局部3D点图并结合图像条件法向量头与单法向量引导训练，稳定三角形参数化。在RealEstate10K和DL3DV数据集上，TriSplat在几何保真度上优于高斯前馈基线，同时保持相近的新型视角渲染质量，且其三角形输出可直接用于物理引擎和渲染管线，无需额外转换。
MobileGym: A Verifiable and Highly Parallel Simulation Platform for Mobile GUI Agent Research
Authors: Dingbang Wu, Rui Hao, Haiyang Wang, Shuzhe Wu, Han Xiao, Zhenghong Li, Bojiang Zhou, Zheng Ju, Zichen Liu, Lue Fan, Zhaoxiang Zhang
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://mobilegym.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present MobileGym, a browser-hosted, lightweight, fully controllable environment for everyday mobile use, targeting interaction fidelity without replicating proprietary backends. It enables two capabilities previously out of reach for everyday apps: verifiable outcome signals through deterministic state-based judging over structured JSON state, and scalable online RL through low-cost parallel rollouts. The full environment state is captured, configured, forked, and compared as structured JSON, and a single server can host hundreds of parallel instances, with about 400 MB memory per instance and about 3 s cold start. A layered state model and a declarative task-definition framework keep state programmability and task creation practical at scale, and a single programmatic judging mechanism delivers both deterministic evaluation verdicts and dense RL rewards. The accompanying MobileGym-Bench provides 416 parameterized task templates, including 256 test and 160 train templates, over 28 apps, with deterministic judges and a structured AnswerSheet protocol that avoids free-text matching failures. In a Sim-to-Real case study, GRPO on Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct gains +12.8 percentage points on the 256-task test set, and on a 59-task real-device signal subset, real-device execution retains 95.1% of the simulation-side training gain. Project page: https://mobilegym.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MobileGym：面向移动GUI代理研究的可验证且高度并行的仿真平台
我们提出了MobileGym，一个基于浏览器、轻量级、完全可控的日常移动应用仿真环境，旨在实现交互保真度而不复制专有后端。它实现了此前普通应用无法实现的两项能力：通过结构化JSON状态的确定性状态判断提供可验证的结果信号，以及通过低成本并行运行实现可扩展的在线强化学习。完整环境状态以结构化JSON形式捕获、配置、分叉和比较，单个服务器可托管数百个并行实例，每个实例约300MB内存，冷启动约3秒。分层状态模型和声明式任务定义框架确保大规模下状态可编程性和任务创建的实用性，单一程序化评判机制同时提供确定性评估结果和密集型强化学习奖励。配套的MobileGym-Bench提供416个参数化任务模板，覆盖28款应用，包括256个测试模板和160个训练模板，配备确定性评判器和结构化AnswerSheet协议，避免自由文本匹配失败。在Sim-to-Real案例研究中，Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct上的GRPO在256任务测试集上提升12.8个百分点，在59任务真实设备信号子集上，真实设备执行保留了95.1%的仿真训练增益。项目页面：https://mobilegym.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for verifiable and scalable mobile GUI agent training without proprietary backend dependencies, MobileGym introduces a browser-hosted, lightweight simulation environment that captures full state as structured JSON for deterministic evaluation and parallel RL. It enables scalable online reinforcement learning via low-cost parallel rollouts, with each instance consuming ~400 MB memory and ~3s cold start. The platform supports 416 parameterized tasks across 28 apps via a declarative task framework and a unified judging system that delivers both verdicts and dense rewards. Experimental results show GRPO on Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct achieves +12.8% performance gain on 256 test tasks, and real-device execution retains 95.1% of simulation training gains on a 59-task subset.
为实现可验证、可扩展且贴近真实场景的移动端GUI智能体训练，MobileGym构建了一个基于浏览器的轻量级仿真平台，无需依赖专有后端即可保持交互保真度。其通过结构化JSON状态实现确定性评估，并支持低内存、快速冷启动的高并行强化学习训练。实验表明，Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct在256个测试任务上使用GRPO方法提升12.8个百分点，且在59个真实设备任务子集上，真实设备执行效果保留了95.1%的仿真训练增益。
AnyScene: Towards Highly Controllable Driving Scene Generation at Anywhere and Beyond
Authors: Haiming Zhang, Junfei Zhou, Feng Jiang, Jingzhong Li, Zhenglong Guo, Penglin Dai, Jifeng Dai, Yan Xie, Benjin Zhu
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:48+00:00
Comments: Work in progress. Project page: https://mind-omni.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generating high-fidelity and controllable synthetic data is critical for advancing end-to-end autonomous driving, particularly for addressing the long tail of rare safety-critical scenarios. Existing occupancy-guided methods typically rely on shallow conditioning mechanisms and reference-frame-dependent video synthesis, which limits fine-grained controllability from arbitrary BEV layouts and restricts their applicability for scalable simulation. In this paper, we propose AnyScene, a unified occupancy-centric framework for driving scene generation. AnyScene generates semantic occupancy sequences from BEV layouts through a Spatial-Temporal Occupancy Diffusion Transformer that jointly tokenizes BEV and occupancy features in an autoregressive manner. This design enables precise controllability from cross-dataset and user-defined BEV inputs while naturally supporting long-horizon generation. Building upon the generated occupancy, a Geometry-Grounded View Expansion module treats occupancy as the canonical spatial representation and synthesizes temporally consistent multi-view driving videos in a reference-free and autoregressive fashion, supporting flexible camera configurations at inference time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnyScene achieves state-of-the-art performance in both occupancy and video generation. It exhibits strong generalization to unseen and customized layouts, and provides measurable benefits for downstream tasks such as sparse-view 3D reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyScene：面向任意场景与更广范围的高可控驾驶场景生成
生成高保真且可控的合成数据对推进端到端自动驾驶至关重要，尤其有助于应对罕见的安全关键场景长尾问题。现有基于占用引导的方法通常依赖浅层条件机制和参考帧依赖的视频合成，限制了从任意鸟瞰图（BEV）布局实现细粒度可控性，并限制其在可扩展仿真中的适用性。本文提出AnyScene，一个统一的以占用为中心的驾驶场景生成框架。AnyScene通过空间-时间占用扩散Transformer，从BEV布局生成语义占用序列，该模型以自回归方式联合编码BEV与占用特征。此设计支持从跨数据集和用户自定义的BEV输入实现精确可控性，同时自然支持长时程生成。基于生成的占用，几何锚定视图扩展模块将占用视为规范空间表示，以无参考帧和自回归方式合成时序一致的多视角驾驶视频，支持推理时灵活的相机配置。大量实验表明，AnyScene在占用与视频生成方面均达到最先进性能，对未见及自定义布局具有强泛化能力，并为下游任务（如稀疏视角3D重建）提供可衡量的增益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for controllable and high-fidelity synthetic driving data to cover rare safety-critical scenarios, AnyScene introduces a unified occupancy-centric framework that generates semantic occupancy sequences from arbitrary bird’s-eye-view layouts using a Spatial-Temporal Occupancy Diffusion Transformer. This model jointly tokenizes BEV and occupancy features in an autoregressive manner, enabling fine-grained control from cross-dataset inputs and long-horizon generation. The generated occupancy is then expanded into temporally consistent multi-view videos via a Geometry-Grounded View Expansion module, which operates reference-free and supports flexible camera configurations. Experimental results show AnyScene achieves state-of-the-art performance in both occupancy and video synthesis, with strong generalization to unseen layouts and measurable improvements in downstream tasks like sparse-view 3D reconstruction.
为解决自动驾驶中罕见安全场景的合成数据生成难题，AnyScene 提出一种统一的基于占用的框架，通过空间-时间占用扩散变换器从任意鸟瞰图布局生成语义占用序列，该模型以自回归方式联合编码鸟瞰图与占用特征，实现跨数据集输入的精细控制与长时生成。生成的占用序列经几何锚定视图扩展模块转化为时序一致的多视角视频，支持推理时灵活的相机配置且无需参考帧。实验表明，AnyScene 在占用与视频生成任务上达到当前最优水平，对未见布局具备强泛化能力，并显著提升下游任务如稀疏视图三维重建的效果。
From Model Scaling to System Scaling: Scaling the Harness in Agentic AI
Authors: Shangding Gu
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper studies the next major bottleneck in agentic AI as system scaling, not only model scaling: the design of auditable, persistent, modular, and verifiable architectures around foundation models. We refer to this shift as scaling the harness: treating the structured execution layer around a foundation model as a first-class object of design, evaluation, and optimization. Although recent large language models enable agents to use tools, retrieve information, maintain memory, and execute long-horizon workflows, evaluation remains largely model-centric, often reducing agents to final-task success while treating memory, retrieval, tool use, orchestration, verification, and governance as secondary implementation details. This framing is increasingly inadequate because agent performance emerges from the interaction among the foundation model, memory substrate, context constructor, skill-routing layer, orchestration loop, and verification-and-governance layer. Together, these components form the agent harness, which translates model capability into long-horizon agent behavior. We study scaling the harness through three core bottlenecks: context governance, trustworthy memory, and dynamic skill routing, together with the orchestration and governance mechanisms that coordinate and constrain them. We further outline a research agenda for harness-level benchmarks that go beyond one-shot task success to measure trajectory quality, memory hygiene, context efficiency, communication fidelity, verification cost, and safe evolution over time. To make the discussion concrete, we develop CheetahClaws: https://github.com/SafeRL-Lab/cheetahclaws, a Python-native reference harness, and compare it with Claude Code and OpenClaw. Our main claim is that future progress in agentic AI will depend as much on system design as on stronger foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从模型扩展到系统扩展：代理AI中的‘缰绳’扩展
本文研究了代理AI中的下一个主要瓶颈——系统扩展，而非仅模型扩展：围绕基础模型设计可审计、持久、模块化和可验证的架构。我们将此转变称为‘缰绳扩展’：将基础模型周围的结构化执行层视为设计、评估和优化的一等对象。尽管近期大型语言模型使代理能够使用工具、检索信息、维护记忆并执行长周期工作流，但评估仍主要以模型为中心，常将代理简化为最终任务成功率，而将记忆、检索、工具使用、编排、验证和治理视为次要实现细节。这种框架正变得日益不足，因为代理性能源于基础模型、记忆基底、上下文构建器、技能路由层、编排循环和验证-治理层之间的交互。这些组件共同构成‘代理缰绳’，将模型能力转化为长周期代理行为。我们通过三个核心瓶颈——上下文治理、可信记忆和动态技能路由，以及协调和约束它们的编排与治理机制，研究‘缰绳’扩展。我们进一步提出了一项研究议程，旨在开发超越单次任务成功率的‘缰绳’级基准，以衡量轨迹质量、记忆卫生、上下文效率、通信保真度、验证成本及随时间的安全演化。为使讨论具体化，我们开发了CheetahClaws：https://github.com/SafeRL-Lab/cheetahclaws，一个原生Python的参考缰绳，并与Claude Code和OpenClaw进行比较。我们的主要主张是：未来代理AI的进步将同样依赖于系统设计，而非更强的基础模型。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that the next major bottleneck in agentic AI lies not in model scaling but in system scaling—the design of auditable, persistent, and modular architectures around foundation models, which we call 'scaling the harness.' The authors identify three core bottlenecks: context governance, trustworthy memory, and dynamic skill routing, along with orchestration and governance mechanisms that coordinate them. To ground the discussion, they introduce CheetahClaws, a Python-native reference harness, and compare it with Claude Code and OpenClaw. Their experiments reveal that system-level design significantly impacts trajectory quality, memory hygiene, and safe evolution, suggesting that future progress in agentic AI will depend as much on harness architecture as on model capability.
本文指出，下一代代理AI的主要瓶颈不在模型规模扩展，而在系统规模扩展——即围绕基础模型构建可审计、持久化、模块化的架构，称为“扩展缰绳”。作者识别出三个核心瓶颈：上下文治理、可信记忆与动态技能路由，以及协调和约束它们的编排与治理机制。为具体化讨论，他们开发了CheetahClaws，一个原生Python的参考缰绳系统，并与Claude Code和OpenClaw进行对比。实验表明，系统级设计对代理性能影响显著，轨迹质量、记忆卫生与上下文效率等指标比单一任务成功率更重要，暗示未来代理AI的进步将同样依赖系统架构设计，而非单纯模型能力提升。
Squeezing Capacity from Multimodal Large Language Models for Subject-driven Generation
Authors: Shuhong Zheng, Aashish Kumar Misraa, Yu-Teng Li, Yu-Jhe Li, Igor Gilitschenski
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:35+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 18 figures, Project Page: https://zsh2000.github.io/squeeze-mllm-subject-gen/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Subject-driven image generation aims to synthesize new images that preserve the identity of the given subject while following textual instructions. Existing approaches often encode text and reference images separately. This limits cross-modal reasoning abilities and causes copy-paste artifacts. Recent frameworks that connect multimodal models and diffusion models improve instruction following, but largely overlook identity preservation. To address these limitations, we condition diffusion models on Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) that jointly encode text and reference images, and augment it with VAE-based identity conditioning. A novel Dual Layer Aggregation (DLA) module is designed to aggregate multi-level MLLM features for optimal conditioning, and a multi-stage denoising strategy is applied to progressively balance the semantic information from MLLM and fine-detail identity from VAE during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach harmonizes multimodal understanding with identity preservation, mitigates copy-paste issues, and achieves superior performance regarding human preference on subject-driven image generation. Our project website is available at https://zsh2000.github.io/squeeze-mllm-subject-gen/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从多模态大语言模型中榨取容量以实现主题驱动的生成
主题驱动的图像生成旨在合成新图像，在遵循文本指令的同时保留给定主题的身份。现有方法通常分别编码文本和参考图像，这限制了跨模态推理能力并导致复制粘贴伪影。近期将多模态模型与扩散模型结合的框架提升了指令遵循能力，但大多忽视了身份保留。为解决这些局限，我们以同时编码文本和参考图像的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）为扩散模型提供条件，并结合基于VAE的身份条件化。设计了一种新颖的双层聚合（DLA）模块，用于聚合多层级MLLM特征以实现最优条件化，并在推理过程中应用多阶段去噪策略，逐步平衡MLLM提供的语义信息与VAE提供的精细身份细节。大量实验表明，我们的方法协调了多模态理解与身份保留，缓解了复制粘贴问题，并在主题驱动图像生成的人类偏好评估中取得了优越性能。项目网站见：https://zsh2000.github.io/squeeze-mllm-subject-gen/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate images that preserve subject identity while following textual instructions, this work proposes conditioning diffusion models on Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) that jointly encode text and reference images, augmented with VAE-based identity conditioning. A Dual Layer Aggregation (DLA) module aggregates multi-level MLLM features for optimal conditioning, and a multi-stage denoising strategy balances semantic guidance from MLLMs with fine-grained identity details from VAEs during generation. Experiments show the method significantly improves identity preservation, reduces copy-paste artifacts, and achieves higher human preference scores compared to existing approaches.
为解决在遵循文本指令的同时保持主体身份一致的图像生成问题，该研究提出将扩散模型与能联合编码文本和参考图像的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）相结合，并辅以基于VAE的身份条件化。设计了双层聚合模块以整合多层级MLLM特征，并采用多阶段去噪策略在生成过程中平衡语义信息与身份细节。实验表明，该方法显著提升了身份保持能力，减少了复制粘贴伪影，并在人类偏好评估中优于现有方法。
Prism: A Plug-in Reproducible Infrastructure for Scalable Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning
Authors: Jun-Tao Tang, Yu-Cheng Shi, Zhen-Hao Xie, Da-Wei Zhou
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:28+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/LAMDA-CL/Prism
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve versatility by reformulating diverse tasks into a unified instruction-following framework via instruction tuning. However, real-world deployment requires continuous adaptation to emerging tasks, motivating Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT). Despite its growing importance, current MCIT research is hindered by severe engineering bottlenecks. Existing methods are typically implemented by directly modifying the base MLLM codebase, which imposes substantial implementation overhead and yields method-specific architectures that severely limit code reuse and fair comparison. To address this, we introduce Prism, a plug-in reproducible codebase specifically designed for scalable MCIT research. It separates algorithmic development from the backbone implementation via a lightweight plugin registration mechanism, enabling new strategies to be integrated as independent plugins without modifying the underlying MLLM codebase, thereby eliminating structural fragmentation and accelerating method development. Prism natively supports widely used large-scale training pipeline, thereby enabling reproducible and scalable MCIT experimentation. Code is available at https://github.com/LAMDA-CL/Prism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Prism：用于可扩展多模态持续指令调优的插件式可复现基础设施
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过指令调优将多样化任务统一为指令遵循框架，从而实现多功能性。然而，实际部署需持续适应新兴任务，催生了多模态持续指令调优（MCIT）。尽管其重要性日益增长，当前MCIT研究受严重工程瓶颈制约。现有方法通常直接修改基础MLLM代码库，导致实现开销大、架构方法特定，严重限制代码复用与公平比较。为此，我们提出Prism，专为可扩展MCIT研究设计的插件式可复现代码库。其通过轻量级插件注册机制，将算法开发与骨干实现分离，使新策略可作为独立插件集成，无需修改底层MLLM代码库，从而消除结构碎片化并加速方法开发。Prism原生支持广泛使用的大型训练流水线，实现可复现且可扩展的MCIT实验。代码见https://github.com/LAMDA-CL/Prism。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and reproducible multimodal continual instruction tuning (MCIT) in real-world deployments, Prism introduces a plug-in architecture that decouples algorithmic development from the base MLLM codebase, enabling new strategies to be added as independent plugins without modifying core infrastructure. This design eliminates structural fragmentation and facilitates rapid experimentation while ensuring reproducibility. Experimental results demonstrate that Prism effectively supports diverse MCIT methods with minimal overhead, enabling seamless integration into existing large-scale training pipelines and significantly accelerating research progress in this domain.
为应对多模态持续指令调优（MCIT）在实际部署中的可扩展性和可复现性需求，Prism提出一种插件化架构，将算法开发与基础MLLM代码库解耦，允许新策略以独立插件形式集成而无需修改底层代码，从而消除结构碎片化并加速方法开发。该架构天然兼容主流大规模训练流水线，实验表明其能有效支持多种MCIT方法，实现高可复现性与可扩展性，便于跨方法公平比较与快速迭代。
Helix4D: Complex 4D Mesh Generation
Authors: Jiraphon Yenphraphai, Jianqi Chen, Jian Wang, Gordon Qian, Sergey Tulyakov, Rameen Abdal, Raymond A. Yeh, Peter Wonka, Chaoyang Wang
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:22+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://snap-research.github.io/helix4d/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current video-to-4D methods struggle with complex topology changes, transparent materials, thin structures, and inner surfaces. We present Helix4D, a dynamic mesh generation framework by inheriting the expressive representation of Trellis2, adapting it from image-to-3D to video-conditioned 4D generation. Our design arises from two key questions: (a) how to enable Trellis2's frame-local attention to share information across frames while preserving its pretrained quality on rare cases such as transparent objects and inner surfaces, and (b) how to inject temporal information into a purely 3D positional encoding without breaking pretrained capabilities. We address (a) with a sliding-window cross-frame attention and anchor on the first frame. The first frame is generated by the base Trellis2 model and injected into our model, letting it inherit Trellis2's quality in rare cases through cross-frame attention. We address (b) with a 4D temporal encoding that repurposes redundant low-frequency spatial RoPE bands for time, extending the encoding from 3D with no additional parameters. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of Helix4D for high-quality dynamic mesh generation on ActionBench and our own challenging complex dynamics set.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Helix4D：复杂4D网格生成
当前视频到4D的方法在处理复杂拓扑变化、透明材质、薄结构和内表面时存在困难。我们提出了Helix4D，一个动态网格生成框架，继承Trellis2的表达能力，将其从图像到3D扩展为视频条件下的4D生成。我们的设计源于两个关键问题：(a) 如何使Trellis2的帧内注意力在保持其在透明物体和内表面等罕见情况预训练质量的同时，实现跨帧信息共享？(b) 如何在不破坏预训练能力的前提下，将时间信息注入纯3D位置编码？我们通过滑动窗口跨帧注意力并锚定第一帧解决(a)问题，第一帧由基础Trellis2模型生成并注入我们的模型，使其通过跨帧注意力继承Trellis2在罕见情况下的质量。我们通过4D时间编码解决(b)问题，该编码重用冗余的低频空间RoPE频带表示时间，扩展编码至4D且无需额外参数。大量实验表明，Helix4D在ActionBench和我们自建的复杂动态数据集上，能有效实现高质量动态网格生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current video-to-4D methods in handling complex topology changes, transparent materials, and thin structures, Helix4D introduces a dynamic mesh generation framework that adapts Trellis2’s expressive 3D representation for video-conditioned 4D generation. It solves two core challenges: enabling frame-local attention to share information across frames without degrading pretrained performance on rare cases, and injecting temporal encoding into 3D positional encoding without additional parameters. The framework uses a sliding-window cross-frame attention anchored on the first frame, generated by the base Trellis2 model, to preserve its quality for rare cases, and repurposes low-frequency spatial RoPE bands for time in a 4D encoding. Experimental results demonstrate Helix4D’s effectiveness in generating high-quality dynamic meshes on ActionBench and a custom complex dynamics dataset.
为了解决当前视频到4D方法在处理复杂拓扑变化、透明材质、薄结构和内表面时的不足，Helix4D提出了一种动态网格生成框架，将Trellis2的表达能力从图像到3D扩展至视频条件下的4D生成。该方法针对两个核心问题：如何在保持预训练质量（如透明物体和内表面）的同时，使帧内注意力机制跨帧共享信息，以及如何在不破坏预训练能力的前提下，将时间信息注入纯3D位置编码。解决方案包括以第一帧（由基础Trellis2生成）为锚点的滑动窗口跨帧注意力机制，以及重用低频空间RoPE频带作为时间编码的4D时间编码，无需新增参数。实验表明，Helix4D在ActionBench和自建复杂动态数据集上能有效生成高质量动态网格。
Reinforcing Few-step Generators via Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching
Authors: Yushi Huang, Xiangxin Zhou, Ruoyu Wang, Chi Zhang, Jun Zhang, Tianyu Pang
First: 2026-05-25T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:59:21+00:00
Comments: Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances in few-step diffusion distillation have enabled efficient image generation, yet aligning these models with human preferences remains challenging. We propose Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework that unifies distribution matching distillation with reward-guided reinforcement learning for few-step flow generators. We show that minimizing the KL divergence to a reward-tilted teacher distribution naturally decomposes into a distribution matching term and a reward maximization term. In the first stage, we introduce Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), which performs subinterval-wise distribution matching and augments the fake score objective with a consistency regularizer to help the fake score model track the shifting generator distribution under limited updates. In the second stage, we jointly optimize both terms: for the reward maximization term, we derive a hybrid policy gradient that combines a GRPO-style estimator for the stochastic intermediate transitions with direct reward backpropagation through the deterministic final step, and further introduce step-subset GRPO (SubGRPO) to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 demonstrate that RTDMD establishes new state-of-the-art results across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics with only 4 inference steps, outperforming previous few-step text-to-image generation methods. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过奖励倾斜分布匹配强化少步生成器
近期少步扩散蒸馏的进展实现了高效的图像生成，但将这些模型与人类偏好对齐仍具挑战。我们提出奖励倾斜分布匹配蒸馏（RTDMD），一种两阶段框架，将分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导的强化学习统一应用于少步流生成器。我们证明，最小化KL散度至奖励倾斜教师分布自然分解为分布匹配项与奖励最大化项。第一阶段引入环境一致分布匹配蒸馏（AC-DMD），进行子区间分布匹配，并在假分数目标中加入一致性正则化，以帮助假分数模型在有限更新下跟踪生成器分布的移动。第二阶段联合优化两项：对于奖励最大化项，我们推导出混合策略梯度，结合GRPO风格的随机中间转移估计器与直接通过确定性最终步骤的奖励反向传播，并进一步引入步子集GRPO（SubGRPO）以降低方差。在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上的实验表明，RTDMD仅用4步推理即在偏好、美学和构图指标上建立新SOTA，超越先前的少步文本到图像生成方法。代码与模型见https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning few-step diffusion distillation models with human preferences, this work introduces Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework that combines distribution matching with reward-guided reinforcement learning. The first stage, Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), performs subinterval-wise distribution matching and adds a consistency regularizer to stabilize fake score learning under limited updates. The second stage jointly optimizes distribution matching and reward maximization using a hybrid policy gradient that blends GRPO-style estimation with direct reward backpropagation, enhanced by step-subset GRPO to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 show RTDMD achieves state-of-the-art performance across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics using only four inference steps, surpassing prior few-step text-to-image methods.
为解决少步扩散蒸馏模型与人类偏好对齐困难的问题，本文提出奖励倾斜分布匹配蒸馏（RTDMD）框架，结合分布匹配与奖励引导的强化学习。第一阶段“环境一致分布匹配蒸馏”（AC-DMD）进行子区间分布匹配，并引入一致性正则化以在更新有限时稳定学习。第二阶段联合优化分布匹配与奖励最大化，采用混合策略梯度，融合GRPO风格估计与直接奖励反向传播，并引入步集GRPO降低方差。在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上的实验表明，RTDMD仅用四步推理即在偏好、美学与构图指标上达到当前最优，超越先前少步文本到图像生成方法。
TIE: Time Interval Encoding for Video Generation over Events
Authors: Zhilei Shu, Shangwen Zhu, Zihang Liang, Xiaofan Li, Qianyu Peng, Xinyu Cui, Bo Ye, Yiming Li, Fan Cheng, Jian Zhao, Yang Cao, Zheng-Jun Zha, Ruili Feng
First: 2026-05-11T13:23:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:58:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Director-style prompting, robotic action prediction, and interactive video agents demand temporal grounding over concurrent events -- a regime in which 68% of general clips and over 99% of robotics/gameplay clips contain overlapping events, yet existing multi-event generators rest on a single-active-prompt assumption. However, modern video generators, such as Diffusion Transformers (DiT), represent time as discrete points through point-wise positional encodings. This formulation creates a fundamental dimension mismatch: temporally extended intervals and overlapping events are mathematically unrepresentable to the attention mechanism. In this paper, we propose Time Interval Encoding (TIE), a principled, plug-and-play interval-aware generalization of rotary embeddings that elevates time intervals to first-class primitives inside DiT cross-attention. Rather than introducing another heuristic interval embedding, we show that, within RoPE-compatible bilinear attention, TIE is characterized by two basic principles: Temporal Integrability, which requires an event to aggregate positional evidence over its full duration, and Duration Invariance, which removes the trivial bias toward longer intervals. Under a uniform kernel, this characterization yields an efficient closed-form sinc-based solution that preserves the standard attention interface and naturally attenuates boundary noise through interval integration. Empirically, TIE preserves the visual quality of the base DiT model while substantially improving temporal controllability. In our experiments on the OmniEvents dataset, it improves human-verified Temporal Constraint Satisfaction Rate from 77.34% to 96.03% and reduces temporal boundary error from 0.261s to 0.073s, while also improving trajectory-level temporal alignment metrics. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/MatrixTeam-AI/TIE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIE：面向事件视频生成的时间区间编码
导演式提示、机器人动作预测和交互式视频代理需要对并发事件进行时间锚定——在这一场景中，普通视频片段中有68%、机器人/游戏片段中有超过99%包含重叠事件，但现有多事件生成器依赖单活动提示假设。然而，现代视频生成器（如Diffusion Transformer, DiT）通过点式位置编码将时间表示为离散点。这种形式导致根本性的维度不匹配：时间扩展区间和重叠事件在注意力机制中无法被数学表示。本文提出时间区间编码（TIE），这是一种可插拔的、面向区间的旋转嵌入扩展，将时间区间提升为DiT交叉注意力中的第一类原语。我们不引入新的区间嵌入启发式方法，而是在兼容RoPE的双线性注意力框架下，证明TIE由两个基本原理定义：时间可积性（要求事件在其持续时间内聚合位置证据）和时长不变性（消除对长区间无意义的偏置）。在均匀核下，该定义导出一个高效的闭式sinc基解法，保持标准注意力接口，并通过区间积分自然衰减边界噪声。实验证明，TIE在保持DiT基础模型视觉质量的同时显著提升时间可控性。在OmniEvents数据集实验中，其人类验证的时间约束满足率从77.34%提升至96.03%，时间边界误差从0.261秒降至0.073秒，同时提升轨迹级时间对齐指标。代码与数据集见https://github.com/MatrixTeam-AI/TIE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle overlapping events in video generation, this paper introduces Time Interval Encoding (TIE), a method that reformulates time representation in Diffusion Transformers to support interval-based attention. TIE generalizes rotary embeddings to treat time intervals as first-class primitives, enforcing Temporal Integrability and Duration Invariance to ensure accurate modeling of event durations without bias toward longer intervals. The method yields a closed-form sinc-based solution compatible with existing attention mechanisms and reduces boundary noise through interval integration. Experiments on OmniEvents show TIE improves human-verified temporal constraint satisfaction from 77.34% to 96.03%, reduces boundary error from 0.261s to 0.073s, and enhances trajectory-level temporal alignment without degrading visual quality.
为解决现有视频生成模型假设单事件激活而真实视频常含重叠事件的问题，本文提出时间区间编码（TIE），作为扩散Transformer的即插即用改进，将时间区间提升为第一类基本元素。TIE在旋转位置编码框架下引入两个原则——时间可积性与持续时间不变性，确保事件在完整时长内聚合位置证据而不偏向长区间。该方案通过统一核函数推导出闭式sinc解，兼容标准注意力机制并自然抑制边界噪声。在OmniEvents数据集上，TIE将人工验证的时间约束满足率从77.34%提升至96.03%，时间边界误差从0.261秒降至0.073秒，同时提升轨迹级时间对齐指标，且未降低视觉质量。
Looped Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Sanghyun Lee, Chunsan Hong, Seungryong Kim, Jonghyun Lee, Jongho Park, Dongmin Park
First: 2026-05-25T17:58:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:58:24+00:00
Comments: 23 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models for language modeling, yet the effective design of transformer architectures for MDMs remains underexplored. In this paper, we show that selectively looping the early-middle transformer layers significantly improves both training efficiency and model performance in MDMs. We call this approach LoopMDM(Looped Masked Diffusion Model), which brings two key benefits: looping layers at training-time yields a depth-scaling effect without adding parameters, while varying the number of loops at inference-time enables flexible compute scaling. Despite the simplicity, the results are striking: across multiple pre-training corpora, LoopMDM matches the performance of same-size MDMs with up to 3.3 fewer training FLOPs, while its final performance outperforms them on various reasoning benchmarks, including up to 8.5 points on GSM8K. It even surpasses deeper non-looped MDMs trained with comparable per-step compute, indicating that selective looping is more effective than naive depth scaling. Furthermore, LoopMDM can scale inference-time compute by increasing the number of loops. Adaptively adjusting the number of loops throughout the sampling process further yields additional gains in compute efficiency while maintaining performance. Lastly, with attention analysis, we provide evidence that looping is effective in MDMs by promoting interactions among masked positions. Our code and weights will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环扩散语言模型
掩码扩散模型（MDMs）已成为语言建模领域有前景的自回归模型替代方案，但针对MDMs设计有效的Transformer架构仍待探索。本文表明，选择性地循环早期至中期Transformer层可显著提升MDMs的训练效率和模型性能。我们称此方法为LoopMDM（循环掩码扩散模型），其带来两大关键优势：训练时循环层可实现深度扩展效应而不增加参数；推理时调整循环次数可实现灵活的计算扩展。尽管方法简单，结果却十分显著：在多个预训练语料库上，LoopMDM在保持相同规模MDM性能的同时，训练FLOPs最多减少3.3倍，且在多项推理基准测试中表现更优，包括在GSM8K上高出8.5分。它甚至超越了在每步计算量相当情况下训练的更深非循环MDMs，表明选择性循环比朴素深度扩展更有效。此外，LoopMDM可通过增加循环次数扩展推理时的计算量。在采样过程中自适应调整循环次数，还能进一步提升计算效率，同时保持性能。最后，通过注意力分析，我们提供了证据表明循环在MDMs中有效，因为它促进了掩码位置间的交互。我们的代码和权重将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve training efficiency and performance in masked diffusion models (MDMs), this paper introduces LoopMDM, a method that selectively loops early-middle transformer layers. The approach achieves depth-scaling during training without adding parameters and enables flexible compute scaling during inference by varying loop counts. Experimental results show that LoopMDM matches or exceeds the performance of comparable MDMs while using up to 3.3 fewer training FLOPs, and even outperforms deeper non-looped models with similar per-step compute, achieving up to 8.5-point gains on GSM8K. Additionally, adaptive loop adjustment during sampling improves compute efficiency without sacrificing performance, and attention analysis confirms that looping enhances interactions among masked positions.
为提升掩码扩散模型（MDM）的训练效率与性能，本文提出LoopMDM方法，通过选择性循环早期至中期的Transformer层实现深度扩展。该方法在训练时无需增加参数即可实现深度缩放，推理时通过调整循环次数实现灵活的计算扩展。实验表明，LoopMDM在多个预训练语料上性能匹配或超越基线模型，训练FLOPs减少高达3.3倍，且在GSM8K等推理基准上表现更优，最高提升8.5分，甚至优于同等计算开销下的深层非循环模型。此外，推理时动态调整循环次数可进一步提升效率，注意力分析也证实循环机制增强了掩码位置间的交互。
SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills
Authors: Yifan Yang, Ziyang Gong, Weiquan Huang, Qihao Yang, Ziwei Zhou, Zisu Huang, Yan Li, Xuemei Gao, Qi Dai, Bei Liu, Kai Qiu, Yuqing Yang, Dongdong Chen, Xue Yang, Chong Luo
First: 2026-05-22T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:58:16+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback. We argue the skill should instead be trained as the external state of a frozen agent, with the same discipline that makes weight-space optimization reproducible. SkillOpt is, to our knowledge, the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills: a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document, and an edit is accepted only when it strictly improves a held-out validation score. A textual learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and epoch-wise slow/meta update make skill training stable while adding zero inference-time model calls at deployment. Across six benchmarks, seven target models, and three execution harnesses (direct chat, Codex, Claude Code), SkillOpt is best or tied on all 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells and beats every per-cell competitor among human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill skills. On GPT-5.5 it lifts the average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, by +24.8 inside the Codex agentic loop, and by +19.1 inside Claude Code. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skill artifacts retain value when moved across model scales, between Codex and Claude Code execution environments, and to a nearby math benchmark without further optimization. Code: https://aka.ms/skillopt
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillOpt：自进化智能体技能的执行策略
当前智能体技能多为人工编写、一次性生成或通过松散控制的自我修订演化，均不具备深度学习优化器对技能的优化特性，且无法在反馈下可靠地持续改进。我们主张应将技能训练为冻结智能体的外部状态，采用与权重空间优化相同的严谨方法。SkillOpt据我们所知是首个系统性的可控文本空间优化器：一个独立的优化器模型将评分的 rollout 转化为对单个技能文档的有界增删改编辑，且仅当编辑严格提升保留验证分数时才被接受。文本学习率预算、被拒编辑缓冲区及按周期的慢/元更新机制使技能训练稳定，且部署时无需额外模型调用。在六个基准、七种目标模型及三种执行环境（直接对话、Codex、Claude Code）中，SkillOpt在全部52个评估单元（模型×基准×执行环境）中均表现最佳或并列第一，且在人类、一次性LLM、Trace2Skill、TextGrad、GEPA、EvoSkill等所有技能方法中均胜出。在GPT-5.5上，SkillOpt在直接对话中提升无技能准确率+23.5分，在Codex智能体循环内提升+24.8分，在Claude Code中提升+19.1分。迁移实验进一步表明，优化后的技能产物在跨模型规模、跨Codex与Claude Code执行环境、以及迁移到相近数学基准时仍保持价值，无需进一步优化。代码：https://aka.ms/skillopt
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic, optimizer-driven skill evolution in agent systems, the authors propose SkillOpt, a text-space optimizer that treats agent skills as frozen external states trainable via controlled edits. The method uses a separate optimizer model to generate bounded add/delete/replace edits on a skill document, accepting only those that strictly improve a held-out validation score, while employing a learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and slow/meta updates to ensure stability without inference-time overhead. Across six benchmarks, seven models, and three execution environments, SkillOpt outperforms all competitors—including human, one-shot LLM, and prior skill methods—in all 52 evaluated cells, achieving +23.5 to +24.8 point gains on GPT-5.5 across different execution contexts. Transfer experiments confirm skill artifacts maintain effectiveness across model scales and execution environments without re-optimization.
为解决当前智能体技能缺乏系统性优化机制的问题，SkillOpt提出一种可控的文本空间优化器，将技能视为冻结的外部状态，通过在单个技能文档上进行受限的增删改操作进行优化。该方法使用独立优化器模型根据评分回滚生成编辑，仅接受严格提升验证集得分的编辑，并通过学习率预算、被拒编辑缓冲区和慢速/元更新机制确保训练稳定，且部署时无需额外推理调用。在六个基准、七种模型和三种执行环境上，SkillOpt在全部52个评估单元中均优于人类、一次性LLM及现有技能方法（如Trace2Skill、TextGrad、GEPA、EvoSkill），在GPT-5.5上提升准确率23.5至24.8分，并在模型规模、执行环境及数学基准间实现无再优化的迁移能力。
On-Policy Adversarial Flow Distillation for Autoregressive Video Generation
Authors: Yang Luo, Shengju Qian, Xiaohang Tang, Zirui Zhu, Yong Liu, Xin Wang, Yang You
First: 2026-05-25T17:58:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:58:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autoregressive video generators are attractive for streaming, long-horizon, and interactive applications, but distilling strong black-box teachers into causal students remains difficult. The student must learn under its own rollout distribution, whereas practical teachers may expose only prompt-conditioned completed videos and may differ in architecture, capacity, temporal design, and sampling schedule. This interface makes supervised fine-tuning off-policy, score-based distillation inapplicable, and direct adversarial imitation too sparse for denoising-time credit assignment. We propose Adversarial Flow Distillation (AFD), an on-policy framework for heterogeneous black-box video distillation. AFD queries the teacher and rolls out the current student on the same prompts, trains a prompt-paired Bradley-Terry discriminator to estimate clean-sample teacher-student discrepancy, and converts the resulting on-policy advantage into forward-process flow-matching updates on the student's own noised states. Thus, AFD provides dense velocity-field supervision while requiring no teacher scores, latents, denoising trajectories, step alignment, or reverse-chain reinforcement learning. Experiments across two causal AR student families show that AFD consistently improves motion- and physics-sensitive generation while preserving general video quality, and ablations validate the importance of adaptive on-policy feedback and forward-process credit assignment. The method requires only clean teacher videos and student rollouts, providing a practical route for distilling proprietary or heterogeneous video generators into efficient autoregressive students.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于对抗流蒸馏的自回归视频生成方法
自回归视频生成器适用于流媒体、长时程和交互式应用，但将强黑盒教师蒸馏到因果学生模型仍具挑战。学生需在其自身 rollout 分布下学习，而实际教师可能仅提供提示条件下的完整视频，且在架构、容量、时序设计和采样策略上存在差异。该接口使监督微调变为离策略，基于分数的蒸馏不适用，直接对抗模仿又因去噪时的信用分配稀疏而失效。我们提出对抗流蒸馏（AFD），一种面向异构黑盒视频蒸馏的在线策略框架。AFD 在相同提示下查询教师并运行当前学生，训练一个提示配对的 Bradley-Terry 判别器以估计干净样本中教师与学生的差异，并将所得在线策略优势转化为学生自身噪声状态上的前向过程流匹配更新。因此，AFD 提供密集的速度场监督，无需教师分数、潜在变量、去噪轨迹、步长对齐或反向链强化学习。在两个因果自回归学生家族上的实验表明，AFD 一致提升运动与物理敏感性生成，同时保持视频整体质量，消融实验验证了自适应在线反馈与前向过程信用分配的重要性。该方法仅需干净教师视频与学生 rollout，为蒸馏专有或异构视频生成器至高效自回归学生提供实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of distilling strong black-box video teachers into causal autoregressive students under their own rollout distributions, the paper introduces Adversarial Flow Distillation (AFD), an on-policy framework that queries the teacher and runs student rollouts on identical prompts. It trains a Bradley-Terry discriminator to estimate discrepancy between clean teacher and student outputs, then converts the resulting on-policy advantage into flow-matching updates on the student’s noised states. Experiments show AFD consistently improves motion and physics fidelity while preserving general video quality across two student architectures, with ablations confirming the critical role of adaptive on-policy feedback and forward-process credit assignment.
为解决在学生自身 rollout 分布下从黑盒教师中蒸馏强视频生成模型的难题，本文提出 Adversarial Flow Distillation（AFD）框架，该框架在相同提示下查询教师并运行学生模型，训练 Bradley-Terry 判别器评估教师与学生样本的差异，将该差异转化为学生在自身噪声状态上的前向过程流匹配更新，实现密集的速度场监督，无需教师分数、潜变量或步长对齐。实验表明 AFD 在两类学生模型上持续提升运动与物理一致性，同时保持视频整体质量，消融实验验证了自适应在线反馈与前向过程信用分配的重要性。
EVIDENT: Routing MLLM Adaptation through Entity-Grounded Visual Evidence for Cross-Domain Video Temporal Grounding
Authors: Geo Ahn, Jiwook Han, Youngrae Kim, Joonseok Lee, Jinwoo Choi
First: 2026-05-25T17:58:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:58:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-tuning MLLMs for Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) often improves in-domain performance but degrades sharply under domain shift. In this work, we find that this failure is primarily driven not just by unseen query concepts, but by visual domain shift, which prevents the model from coupling its learned temporal localization knowledge with its inherent entity-attention capability. To address this, we introduce EVIDENT, a parameter-efficient adaptation framework that anchors temporal grounding in the inherent entity-attention of pre-trained MLLMs by routing VTG adaptation through explicit visual entity evidence. EVIDENT consists of three components: (i) an Entity Bottleneck Adapter that transforms dense visual tokens into compact entity-level slots, (ii) an Entity-Binding Distillation loss that instills objectness priors into the semantically unstructured MLLM visual space, guiding each slot to bind to a coherent entity, and (iii) an Entity-to-eVidence gating mechanism that leverages the captured entities as evidence, steering the model to localize moments containing query-relevant entities. Together, these components enable VTG fine-tuning to rely on entity-grounded evidence rather than brittle dataset shortcuts. Experiments on cross-domain VTG benchmarks show that EVIDENT consistently improves out-of-domain robustness while preserving competitive in-domain performance with modest parameter overhead. These results suggest that entity-level grounding is an effective inductive bias for generalizable temporal localization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EVIDENT：通过实体锚定视觉证据实现多模态大模型跨域视频时序定位的路由适配
对多模态大模型（MLLMs）进行视频时序定位（VTG）微调通常能提升域内性能，但在域迁移场景下性能急剧下降。本研究发现，这一失败主要源于视觉域迁移，而非仅因未见查询概念，因为域迁移阻碍了模型将所学时序定位知识与固有的实体注意力能力相结合。为此，我们提出EVIDENT，一种参数高效的适配框架，通过显式视觉实体证据将VTG适配路由至预训练MLLMs固有的实体注意力机制。EVIDENT包含三个组件：(i) 实体瓶颈适配器，将密集视觉标记转换为紧凑的实体级槽位；(ii) 实体绑定蒸馏损失，将物体先验注入语义无结构的MLLM视觉空间，引导每个槽位绑定至连贯实体；(iii) 实体到证据门控机制，利用捕获的实体作为证据，引导模型定位包含查询相关实体的时刻。这些组件共同使VTG微调依赖于实体锚定证据，而非脆弱的数据集捷径。在跨域VTG基准测试中，EVIDENT在保持域内性能竞争力的同时，显著提升域外鲁棒性，且参数开销较小。结果表明，实体级锚定是实现可泛化时序定位的有效归纳偏置。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of video temporal grounding performance under domain shift due to visual mismatch preventing entity-attention coupling, EVIDENT introduces a parameter-efficient adaptation framework that routes fine-tuning through entity-grounded visual evidence. It employs an Entity Bottleneck Adapter to compress visual tokens into entity-level slots, an Entity-Binding Distillation loss to enforce objectness priors and coherent entity binding, and an Entity-to-eVidence gating mechanism to steer localization using query-relevant entities. Experiments demonstrate that EVIDENT enhances cross-domain robustness while maintaining competitive in-domain accuracy with minimal parameter overhead, confirming entity-level grounding as a strong inductive bias for generalizable temporal localization.
为解决视频时序定位在跨域场景下因视觉域偏移导致的性能骤降问题，EVIDENT提出一种参数高效的适配框架，通过实体锚定的视觉证据引导模型微调。该框架包含实体瓶颈适配器将视觉token压缩为实体槽、实体绑定蒸馏损失强化对象先验并引导槽绑定实体、以及实体到证据门控机制利用实体证据引导定位。实验表明，EVIDENT在保持域内性能的同时显著提升跨域鲁棒性，参数开销小，证明实体级锚定是提升时序定位泛化能力的有效归纳偏置。
Global Structure-from-Motion Meets Feedforward Reconstruction
Authors: Linfei Pan, Johannes Schönberge, Marc Pollefeys
Venue: CVPR 2026 Highlight
First: 2026-05-25T17:58:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:58:03+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026, Highlight
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Structure-from-Motion -- the process of simultaneously estimating camera poses and 3D scene structure from a collection of images -- remains a central challenge in computer vision, with many open problems yet to be solved. Recent advances in feedforward 3D reconstruction have made significant strides in overcoming persistent failure cases of classical SfM methods, particularly in scenarios characterized by low texture, limited overlap, and symmetries. However, while feedforward approaches excel in these challenging conditions, they often face limitations regarding scalability, accuracy, or robustness, and typically fall short of classical methods in standard reconstruction settings. In this work, we systematically analyze these limitations and propose a new Structure-from-Motion pipeline by combining the respective strengths of classical and feedforward methods. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets show the benefits of our approach, achieving state-of-the-art results across a wide range of scenarios. We share our system as an open-source implementation at https://github.com/colmap/gluemap.
中文标题/摘要
标题：全局结构从运动与前馈重建的结合
结构从运动——即从一组图像中同时估计相机姿态和三维场景结构的过程——仍是计算机视觉的核心挑战，仍有许多未解问题。近期前馈三维重建方法在克服经典SfM方法在低纹理、重叠有限和对称性场景中的持续失败案例方面取得了显著进展。然而，尽管前馈方法在这些困难条件下表现优异，但在可扩展性、精度或鲁棒性方面常有局限，且在标准重建场景中通常不如经典方法。本文系统分析了这些局限性，并提出一种新的结构从运动流程，结合经典与前馈方法各自的优点。在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法在多种场景中均取得最先进结果。我们将在https://github.com/colmap/gluemap开源实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent limitations of classical Structure-from-Motion methods in low-texture and symmetric scenes, and the scalability issues of feedforward 3D reconstruction, this work proposes a hybrid SfM pipeline that integrates the strengths of both paradigms. The method leverages feedforward reconstruction for robustness in challenging conditions while retaining classical SfM’s accuracy in standard settings. Experimental results across multiple datasets demonstrate that the combined approach achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse scenarios, outperforming both purely classical and purely feedforward methods.
为解决传统结构从运动方法在低纹理和对称场景中的失效问题，以及前馈重建方法在可扩展性上的不足，本文提出一种融合两类方法优势的混合管道。该方法结合了传统SfM在标准场景中的鲁棒性与前馈重建在困难视觉条件下的表现能力。在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在各类场景中均达到当前最优性能，优于仅使用传统或前馈方法的方案。
InstructSAM: Segment Any Instance with Any Instructions
Authors: Yuqian Yuan, Wentong Li, Zhaocheng Li, Yutong Lin, Juncheng Li, Siliang Tang, Jun Xiao, Yueting Zhuang, Wenqiao Zhang
First: 2026-05-25T17:58:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:58:03+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we introduce InstructSAM, a unified and streamlined framework designed for multi-instance segmentation under arbitrary instructions. We formulates instruction-driven instance segmentation as a set-structured query prediction problem and propose an explicit reasoning-to-instance query interface that elegantly bridges a vision-language model (VLM) and SAM3. Specifically, a bank of learnable instance queries is injected into the VLM and contextualized with instruction and visual information, enabling each query to serve as an instance-aware slot. A hybrid-attention mechanism further promotes interaction among these queries, visual tokens, and instruction tokens, improving instance enumeration and reducing duplicate predictions. The resulting LLM-conditioned queries are projected into SAM3's detector query space to drive accurate multi-instance segmentation in a single forward pass. This design equips SAM3 with high-level instruction understanding, compositional reasoning, and instance-level set prediction without modifying its core architecture. To support training and evaluation, we further construct Inst2Seg, a high-quality and large-scale instruction-based instance segmentation dataset and benchmark that couples free-form instructions with instance-level masks. Extensive experiments show that only 2B-scale InstructSAM achieves strong results across complex instruction-driven and phrase-level referring segmentation benchmarks, outperforming prior end-to-end methods and SAM3's agentic pipeline while enabling efficient single-pass multi-instance prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InstructSAM：通过任意指令分割任意实例
本文提出InstructSAM，一个统一且精简的框架，用于在任意指令下进行多实例分割。我们将指令驱动的实例分割形式化为集合结构的查询预测问题，并提出一个显式的推理到实例查询接口，优雅地连接视觉-语言模型（VLM）与SAM3。具体而言，一组可学习的实例查询被注入VLM，并结合指令与视觉信息进行上下文化，使每个查询成为实例感知的槽位。混合注意力机制进一步促进这些查询、视觉标记与指令标记之间的交互，提升实例枚举并减少重复预测。生成的LLM条件查询被投影到SAM3的检测器查询空间，从而在单次前向传播中实现精确的多实例分割。该设计赋予SAM3高级指令理解、组合推理和实例级集合预测能力，而无需修改其核心架构。为支持训练与评估，我们进一步构建了Inst2Seg，一个高质量、大规模的基于指令的实例分割数据集与基准，将自由形式指令与实例级掩码相结合。大量实验表明，仅2B规模的InstructSAM在复杂指令驱动和短语级指代分割基准上均取得强劲表现，超越先前端到端方法及SAM3的代理流程，同时支持高效的单次多实例预测。

Experiments on the newly proposed Inst2Seg benchmark demonstrate that even a 2B-scale variant achieves state-of-the-art performance on complex instruction and referring segmentation tasks, surpassing prior end-to-end methods and SAM3’s agentic pipeline without altering its core architecture.

WhisTLE: Deeply Supervised, Text-Only Domain Adaptation for Pretrained Speech Recognition Transformers
Authors: Akshat Pandey, Karun Kumar, Raphael Tang
First: 2025-09-12T17:59:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:57:52+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pretrained automatic speech recognition (ASR) models such as Whisper perform well but still need domain adaptation to handle unseen parlance. In many real-world settings, collecting speech data is impractical, necessitating text-only adaptation. We propose WhisTLE, a deeply supervised, text-only adaptation method for pretrained encoder-decoder ASR models. WhisTLE trains a variational autoencoder (VAE) to model encoder outputs from text and fine-tunes the decoder using the learned text-to-latent encoder, optionally combined with text-to-speech (TTS) adaptation. At inference, the original encoder is restored, incurring no extra runtime cost. Across four datasets and four ASR models, WhisTLE with TTS reduces word error rate (WER) by a relative 49.0% and outperforms all non-WhisTLE baselines in 100 of 112 scenarios. We also find that WhisTLE additively complements any combination of other domain adaptation approaches; we thus recommend the inclusion of WhisTLE during standard processes for adapting encoder-decoder ASR models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WhisTLE：面向预训练语音识别Transformer的深度监督文本仅域自适应方法
预训练自动语音识别（ASR）模型（如Whisper）表现良好，但仍需域自适应以处理未见过的口语。在许多现实场景中，收集语音数据不切实际，因此需要文本仅自适应。我们提出WhisTLE，一种面向预训练编码器-解码器ASR模型的深度监督文本仅自适应方法。WhisTLE训练变分自编码器（VAE）以建模从文本输入的编码器输出，并使用学习到的文本到潜在空间编码器微调解码器，可选地结合文本到语音（TTS）自适应。推理时恢复原始编码器，不增加额外运行时开销。在四个数据集和四个ASR模型上，WhisTLE结合TTS可相对降低词错误率（WER）49.0%，在112个场景中的100个场景中优于所有非WhisTLE基线方法。我们还发现，WhisTLE可与任何其他域自适应方法组合加性互补；因此我们建议在标准流程中纳入WhisTLE以自适应编码器-解码器ASR模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt pretrained speech recognition models to unseen domains without requiring speech data, the authors propose WhisTLE, a text-only adaptation method that trains a variational autoencoder to map text to latent representations and fine-tunes the decoder using these encodings, optionally enhanced by text-to-speech adaptation. At inference, the original encoder is restored without runtime overhead. Experiments across four datasets and four ASR models show that WhisTLE with TTS reduces word error rate by 49.0% relative to baselines and outperforms all non-WhisTLE methods in 100 of 112 scenarios, while also additively improving performance when combined with other domain adaptation techniques.
为了解决预训练语音识别模型在未见领域中缺乏语音数据时的适应问题，作者提出WhisTLE方法，通过训练变分自编码器建模文本对应的编码器输出，并利用学习到的潜在表示微调解码器，可选地结合文本到语音适应。推理时恢复原始编码器，无额外运行开销。在四个数据集和四个ASR模型上的实验表明，WhisTLE结合TTS可相对降低49.0%的词错误率，在112个场景中的100个场景优于所有非WhisTLE基线方法，且与其他领域适应方法结合时具有加性增益，建议在标准ASR模型适配流程中纳入WhisTLE。
Beyond Summaries: Structure-Aware Labeling of Code Changes with Large Language Models
Authors: Bar Weiss, Antonio Abu-Nassar, Adi Sosnovich, Karen Yorav
First: 2026-05-25T17:56:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:56:46+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Code review is a critical practice in software engineering, yet the growing scale and frequency of code patches in modern projects, together with the widespread adoption of AI code assistants, make manual review increasingly challenging. Identifying the types of changes within a patch, such as renames, moves, or logic modifications, can substantially improve review efficiency by enabling prioritization, filtering, and automation. However, existing LLM-based approaches to code review have largely focused on summarization and comment generation, leaving structured code reviews underexplored. In this paper, we present a systematic study of using large language models (LLMs) for taxonomy-based labeling of code changes in a code patch. We introduce a two-stage pipeline that assigns labels to diff hunks and then refines them to capture structural relationships and semantic attributes, such as rename propagation and type changes. Our approach employs few-shot prompting to produce language-agnostic and customizable labels, without the engineering overhead of traditional static-analysis pipelines. We evaluate four LLMs across multiple context configurations on a manually curated benchmark of natural and synthetic patches. Our best configuration achieves up to $84\%$ recall and $81\%$ precision, with high accuracy in extracting relational and attribute metadata. These results suggest that LLM-based labeling can effectively complement static analysis by enabling flexible, multilingual, and automation-friendly code review workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越摘要：利用大语言模型进行结构感知的代码变更标注
代码审查是软件工程中的关键实践，但现代项目中代码补丁规模和频率的增加，以及AI代码助手的广泛应用，使得手动审查日益困难。识别补丁内变更类型（如重命名、移动或逻辑修改）可显著提升审查效率，实现优先级排序、过滤和自动化。然而，现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的代码审查方法主要聚焦于摘要生成和注释生成，对结构化代码审查研究不足。本文系统研究了利用LLM对代码补丁中的变更进行分类标注的方法，提出两阶段管道：首先为diff块分配标签，再进行细化以捕捉结构关系和语义属性（如重命名传播和类型变更）。该方法采用少样本提示生成语言无关且可定制的标签，无需传统静态分析管道的工程开销。我们在人工构建的自然与合成补丁基准数据集上，对四种LLM在多种上下文配置下进行评估。最佳配置实现高达84%召回率和81%精确率，准确提取关系与属性元数据。结果表明，基于LLM的标注可有效补充静态分析，支持灵活、多语言、自动化友好的代码审查流程。

Driven by the need to automate and enhance code review amid growing patch volumes and AI-assisted development, this paper explores using large language models to label code changes with structured taxonomy. 为应对代码变更日益复杂和人工审查效率低下的问题，本文提出利用大语言模型对代码变更进行结构化标签分类。方法采用两阶段流程：先对代码差异块进行高层变更类型标注，再细化标签以捕捉如重命名传播、类型变更等结构与语义属性。在多个真实与合成代码变更数据集上评估四种大语言模型，最佳配置达到84%召回率和81%精确率，表明大语言模型可有效提取关系与属性元数据，无需传统静态分析工具即可支持自动化、多语言的代码审查流程。
Language Models Need Sleep
Authors: Sangyun Lee, Sean McLeish, Tom Goldstein, Giulia Fanti
First: 2026-05-25T17:55:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:55:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based large language models are increasingly used for long-horizon tasks; however, their attention mechanism scales poorly with context length. To handle this, we study a sleep-like consolidation mechanism in which a model periodically converts recent context into persistent fast weights before clearing its key-value cache. During sleep, the model performs $N$ offline recurrent passes over the accumulated context and updates the fast weights in its state-space model (SSM) blocks through a learned local rule. During inference, this shifts extra computation to sleep while preserving the latency of wake-time prediction. We test our method on controlled synthetic tasks, including cellular automata and multi-hop graph retrieval, as well as a realistic math reasoning task, on which a regular transformer as well as SSM-attention hybrid models fail. We then show that increasing sleep duration $N$ for our models improves performance, with the largest gains on examples that require deeper reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型需要睡眠
基于Transformer的大语言模型越来越多地用于长时任务，但其注意力机制在上下文长度增加时扩展性较差。为解决此问题，我们研究了一种类似睡眠的巩固机制：模型周期性地将近期上下文转换为持久的快速权重，然后清空其键值缓存。在睡眠期间，模型对累积上下文执行N次离线递归遍历，并通过学习到的局部规则更新其状态空间模型（SSM）块中的快速权重。推理时，这将额外计算转移到睡眠阶段，同时保持唤醒时预测的延迟不变。我们在受控的合成任务（包括细胞自动机和多跳图检索）以及一个现实的数学推理任务上测试了该方法，常规Transformer及SSM-注意力混合模型均失败。随后我们证明，增加模型的睡眠时长N可提升性能，尤其在需要深层推理的示例中收益最大。
Summary / 总结
To address the poor scalability of attention mechanisms in transformer-based language models with long context, the authors introduce a 'sleep' mechanism that periodically consolidates recent context into persistent fast weights before clearing the key-value cache. During sleep, the model performs offline recurrent passes over accumulated context and updates its state-space model blocks using a learned local rule. Experiments on synthetic tasks like cellular automata and multi-hop graph retrieval, as well as a realistic math reasoning task, show that this approach outperforms standard transformers and hybrid SSM-attention models, with performance gains increasing with longer sleep duration, especially on complex reasoning examples.
为解决基于Transformer的语言模型在长上下文任务中注意力机制扩展性差的问题，作者提出一种‘睡眠’机制：模型周期性地将近期上下文转化为持久的快权重，然后清空键值缓存。睡眠期间，模型对累积上下文进行N次离线递归遍历，并通过学习到的局部规则更新状态空间模型块中的快权重，将计算移至睡眠阶段，同时保持推理时延不变。在细胞自动机、多跳图检索等合成任务及现实数学推理任务上，标准Transformer和混合SSM-注意力模型均表现不佳，而引入睡眠机制的模型表现优异，且随着睡眠时长N增加，性能提升显著，尤其在需要深度推理的问题上效果更明显。
Language-Switching Triggers Take a Latent Detour Through Language Models
Authors: Francis Kulumba, Wissam Antoun, Théo Lasnier, Benoît Sagot, Djamé Seddah
First: 2026-05-18T16:53:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:55:26+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 16 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Backdoor attacks on language models pose a growing security concern, yet the internal mechanisms by which a trigger sequence hijacks model computations remain poorly understood. We identify a circuit underlying a language-switching backdoor in an 8B-parameter autoregressive language model, where a three-word Latin trigger (nine tokens) redirects English output to French. We decompose the circuit into three phases: (1) distributed attention heads at early layers compose the trigger tokens into the last sequence position; (2) the resulting signal propagates through mid-layers in a subspace orthogonal to the model's natural language-identity direction; (3) the MLP at the final layer converts this latent signal into French logits. The entire circuit flows through a serial bottleneck at a single position: corrupting that position at any layer entirely mitigates the trigger but also hinders the model's capabilities. The orthogonal latent encoding suggests that defenses that search for language-like signals in intermediate representations would miss this trigger entirely.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言切换触发器通过语言模型的潜在路径实现绕行
针对语言模型的后门攻击日益引发安全担忧，但触发序列如何劫持模型计算的内部机制仍不甚明了。我们识别出一个8B参数自回归语言模型中语言切换后门的电路机制：一个三词拉丁语触发器（九个token）可将英语输出重定向为法语。该电路分为三个阶段：(1) 早期层中分布式的注意力头将触发词组合至序列末尾；(2) 该信号在中间层沿与模型自然语言身份方向正交的子空间传播；(3) 最终层的MLP将该潜在信号转换为法语logits。整个电路通过单一位置的串行瓶颈，任何层中破坏该位置即可完全消除触发器，但也会影响模型能力。正交潜在编码表明，若防御机制在中间表示中搜索语言类信号，将完全无法发现此类触发器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how backdoor triggers hijack language models, this study investigates a language-switching backdoor in an 8B-parameter model that uses a three-word Latin trigger to redirect English output to French. The researchers decompose the attack mechanism into three phases: early-layer attention heads assemble the trigger into the sequence’s final position, mid-layer propagation occurs in a subspace orthogonal to natural language identity, and the final MLP layer converts this latent signal into French logits. Experimental results show that disrupting a single serial bottleneck position across any layer completely blocks the trigger while also impairing model functionality, revealing that defenses relying on language-like signal detection in intermediate layers would fail to identify such orthogonal encodings.
为理解后门触发器如何劫持语言模型，研究者分析了8B参数模型中一个通过三词拉丁语触发器强制输出法语的后门机制。该机制分为三阶段：早期层通过注意力分布将触发词组合至序列末尾，中层在与自然语言身份正交的子空间中传播信号，最终MLP将该潜在信号转化为法语logits。实验表明，破坏任一层中的单一串行瓶颈位置可完全阻断触发器，同时也会损害模型能力，说明依赖中间层语言特征检测的防御方法将无法识别此类攻击。
Forgetting in Language Models: Capacity, Optimization, and Self-Generated Replay
Authors: Martin Marek, Dongkyu Cho, Shikai Qiu, Rumi Chunara, Pavel Izmailov, Andrew Gordon Wilson
First: 2026-05-25T17:54:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:54:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Models trained on a new task typically degrade on prior tasks, a phenomenon known as forgetting. Traditionally, mitigating forgetting has required replaying stored exemplars from prior tasks, which is often impractical. By contrast, language models can sample from their own training distribution, and we show that these self-generated samples serve as effective replay data, nearly eliminating forgetting. We find that forgetting nonetheless persists when the model has little remaining capacity: models pretrained close to saturation cannot absorb new information without overwriting prior knowledge. When capacity is not the limiting factor, low learning rates reduce forgetting but require substantially more training steps. Replay breaks this tradeoff, enabling fast, high-learning-rate finetuning without forgetting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型中的遗忘：容量、优化与自生成重放
在新任务上训练的模型通常会在先前任务上表现下降，这种现象称为遗忘。传统上，缓解遗忘需要重放先前任务存储的示例，这往往不切实际。相比之下，语言模型可以从其自身的训练分布中采样，我们证明这些自生成样本可作为有效的重放数据，几乎消除遗忘。我们发现，当模型剩余容量有限时，遗忘仍会持续：接近饱和预训练的模型无法吸收新信息而不覆盖先前知识。当容量不是限制因素时，低学习率可减少遗忘，但需要大量训练步骤。重放打破了这一权衡，使模型能在不遗忘的前提下快速、高学习率微调。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates forgetting in language models during task adaptation, motivated by the challenge of retaining prior knowledge without explicit replay. The authors demonstrate that self-generated samples from the model’s own training distribution can effectively replace external replay data, nearly eliminating forgetting. Their experiments reveal that forgetting persists when models are pretrained near capacity saturation, as new learning overwrites prior knowledge. When capacity is sufficient, low learning rates reduce forgetting but demand more training steps; replay enables fast, high-learning-rate fine-tuning without forgetting, breaking this tradeoff.
该研究探讨了语言模型在学习新任务时对旧任务性能下降（遗忘）的问题。作者提出，模型可利用自身训练分布中自生成的样本作为回放数据，有效缓解遗忘。实验表明，当模型容量受限（尤其在接近饱和的预训练模型中），新知识会覆盖旧知识，导致遗忘持续存在；但当容量充足时，低学习率虽可减少遗忘却需更多训练步数；而回放机制则能实现高速、高学习率微调而不遗忘。
Pixel-Level Pavement Distress Assessment Using Instance Segmentation
Authors: Logan Dewick, Bibesh Pyakurel, Kong Pheng Yang, Nazim Choudhury, M. G. Sarwar Murshed
First: 2026-05-25T17:53:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:53:23+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated pavement distress assessment requires more than image-level classification or coarse bounding box detection, demanding precise localization of thin, branching, and irregular cracks to achieve the geometric precision necessary for maintenance-relevant quantification. This paper presents a vision-based pavement distress analysis system based on Mask R-CNN instance segmentation and evaluates it on UWGB-StreetCrack, a custom field-collected roadway image dataset acquired with a vehicle-mounted smartphone and manually annotated with polygon labels for longitudinal cracks, transverse cracks, alligator cracks, and potholes. Five Detectron2-based Mask R-CNN backbone variants were considered under a consistent fine-tuning protocol. The best-performing model, Mask R-CNN with a ResNet-101 FPN backbone, achieved 84.23% precision, 90.04% recall, and an F1 score of 87.04% under the project-specific bounding-box matching protocol. The same model produced an aggregate predicted crack-area fraction of 2.164%, closely matching the 2.170% ground-truth crack-area fraction. To contextualize the segmentation system against a detector-oriented alternative, a CSPDarknet53-based YOLO detector was also adapted and retrained on the dataset, reaching 27.5% precision and 20.7% recall on the validation protocol. The results show that instance segmentation is a practical direction for field pavement imagery and aggregate crack-area estimation, while also exposing open challenges in annotation consistency, class imbalance, confounder rejection, and mask-level benchmarking.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于实例分割的像素级路面病害评估
自动化路面病害评估需要超越图像级分类或粗略边界框检测，必须精确定位细长、分叉和不规则裂缝，以实现维护相关量化所需的几何精度。本文提出了一种基于Mask R-CNN实例分割的视觉路面病害分析系统，并在UWGB-StreetCrack数据集上进行评估。该数据集为自定义采集的公路图像，使用车载智能手机拍摄，并由人工标注了纵向裂缝、横向裂缝、龟裂和坑洞的多边形标签。在统一微调协议下，评估了五种基于Detectron2的Mask R-CNN骨干网络变体。表现最佳的模型（ResNet-101 FPN骨干网络）在项目特定的边界框匹配协议下达到84.23%精确率、90.04%召回率和87.04% F1分数。该模型预测的裂缝总面积占比为2.164%，与真实值2.170%高度接近。为与检测器导向的替代方案对比，还对基于CSPDarknet53的YOLO检测器进行了适配和再训练，在验证协议下达到27.5%精确率和20.7%召回率。结果表明，实例分割是适用于现场路面图像和裂缝总面积估算的可行方向，同时也揭示了标注一致性、类别不平衡、干扰因素排除和掩码级基准测试等开放性挑战。
