A YOLO-based detector was also tested for comparison, yielding significantly lower performance (27.5% precision, 20.7% recall), highlighting instance segmentation’s superiority for this task while revealing challenges in annotation consistency and class imbalance.
该研究为实现高精度路面病害检测，提出基于Mask R-CNN实例分割的视觉分析系统，以精确定位细长、分支状裂缝，满足维护所需的几何精度。在包含手动标注多类裂缝与坑洞的UWGB-StreetCrack数据集上，五种Mask R-CNN变体在统一微调下测试，其中ResNet-101 FPN模型表现最佳，精度84.23%，召回率90.04%，F1分数87.04%，预测裂缝面积2.164%与真实值2.170%高度吻合。对比实验中，YOLO检测器仅达到27.5%精度和20.7%召回率，凸显实例分割方法的优势。研究同时指出标注一致性、类别不平衡、干扰因素排除及掩码级基准缺失等挑战仍待解决。
Goal-driven Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design for Robust Decision-Making Under Model Uncertainty
Authors: Jinwoo Go, Xiaoning Qian, Byung-Jun Yoon
First: 2026-05-25T17:53:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:53:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian optimal experimental design (BOED) selects experiments to maximize information gain about model parameters. However, in decision-critical settings, reducing parameter uncertainty does not necessarily improve downstream decisions, as only specific parameter directions relevant to the objective truly matter. We propose GoBOED, a goal-driven BOED framework that directly optimizes experimental designs for a specified decision-making objective. GoBOED combines an amortized variational posterior surrogate with a differentiable convex decision layer, enabling gradient-based design optimization that is fully decision-focused. We theoretically show that GoBOED gradients are insensitive to parameter directions irrelevant to the decision objective, providing a formal justification for why goal-driven design achieves equivalent decision quality over a wider set of experimental designs than information-gain maximization. Empirically, across source localization, epidemic management, and pharmacokinetic control, GoBOED identifies designs that better align with downstream decision objectives and reveals that near-optimal design windows are substantially wider than those predicted by goal-agnostic BOED approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向目标的贝叶斯最优实验设计：在模型不确定性下的稳健决策
贝叶斯最优实验设计（BOED）选择实验以最大化对模型参数的信息增益。然而，在决策关键场景中，降低参数不确定性并不必然提升下游决策效果，因为仅与目标相关的参数方向才真正重要。我们提出GoBOED，一种面向目标的BOED框架，直接为指定决策目标优化实验设计。GoBOED结合了变分后验代理模型与可微凸决策层，实现完全以决策为中心的梯度优化设计。理论上，我们证明GoBOED梯度对与决策目标无关的参数方向不敏感，从而为面向目标的设计为何能在更广泛的实验设计中实现等效决策质量提供了理论依据。实证上，在源定位、流行病管理及药代动力学控制中，GoBOED识别出更契合下游决策目标的设计方案，并揭示近最优设计窗口远比目标无关BOED方法预测的更宽泛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that traditional Bayesian optimal experimental design (BOED) maximizes information gain without considering downstream decision objectives, this work introduces GoBOED, a goal-driven framework that directly optimizes experiments to improve decision outcomes. GoBOED combines a variational posterior surrogate with a differentiable convex decision layer to enable gradient-based optimization focused on the decision goal. Theoretical analysis confirms that its gradients ignore irrelevant parameter directions, justifying its superior decision robustness. Empirical results across source localization, epidemic management, and pharmacokinetic control show GoBOED identifies designs more aligned with decision objectives and achieves near-optimal performance over significantly wider experimental windows than conventional BOED.
传统贝叶斯最优实验设计（BOED）聚焦于最大化参数信息增益，但未考虑下游决策目标，导致设计可能与实际决策脱节。本文提出GoBOED框架，通过结合变分后验代理与可微凸决策层，直接优化面向特定决策目标的实验设计，实现梯度驱动的决策导向优化。理论分析表明，GoBOED梯度对决策无关的参数方向不敏感，从而解释其决策鲁棒性更强。实验证明，在源定位、流行病管理与药代动力学控制等任务中，GoBOED设计更贴合决策目标，且其近优设计窗口显著宽于传统BOED方法。
BioLip: Language-Generalizable Lip-Sync Deepfake Detection via Biomechanical Constraint Violation Modeling
Authors: Hao Chen, Junnan Xu
First: 2026-04-18T03:32:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:53:00+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 5 figures. Keywords: Deepfake detection, lip-sync forgery, biomechanical constraints, landmark kinematics, cross-lingual generalization, video forensics, privacy-preserving inference, compression robustness
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing lip-sync deepfake detectors rely on pixel artifacts or audio-visual correspondence, and both fail under generator or language shift because the features they learn are tied to the training distribution. We take a different approach. Authentic lip motion is constrained by tissue mechanics and neuromuscular bandwidth; current generators typically do not impose these constraints, producing trajectories with elevated variance in velocity, acceleration, and jerk that real speech does not exhibit. We exploit this signal, which we term temporal lip jitter, by computing kinematic statistics from 64 perioral landmarks over short sliding windows and feeding them into a lightweight three-branch network. The model uses only landmark coordinates: no pixels, no audio, and no voiceprint data. We train only on English data and test in a zero-shot setting on five unseen generators and seven languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BioLip：通过生物力学约束违反建模实现语言泛化的唇同步深度伪造检测
现有唇同步深度伪造检测器依赖像素伪影或视听一致性，但在生成器或语言变化时均失效，因为它们所学习的特征与训练分布绑定。我们采取不同方法：真实唇动受组织力学和神经肌肉带宽约束，当前生成器通常不施加这些约束，导致其运动轨迹在速度、加速度和急动度上的方差高于真实语音。我们利用这一信号——称为时间唇抖动——通过在64个口周关键点上计算短滑动窗口内的运动学统计量，并输入轻量级三分支网络。模型仅使用关键点坐标：无需像素、无需音频、无需声纹数据。仅在英语数据上训练，零样本测试于五个未见生成器和七种语言。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing lip-sync deepfake detectors that fail under language or generator shifts due to distribution-dependent features, this work introduces BioLip, a method that leverages biomechanical constraints of authentic lip motion. It computes temporal lip jitter—kinematic statistics from 64 perioral landmarks over sliding windows—and feeds them into a lightweight three-branch network that uses only landmark coordinates, avoiding pixels, audio, or voiceprint data. Trained solely on English data, the model achieves zero-shot detection across five unseen generators and seven languages, demonstrating strong cross-lingual generalization and robustness to compression.
为了解决现有唇同步深度伪造检测器依赖像素或视听特征、在语言或生成器变化时失效的问题，本文提出BioLip方法，利用真实唇部运动的生物力学约束。该方法通过滑动窗口计算64个唇周关键点的运动学统计量，输入轻量级三分支网络，仅使用关键点坐标，无需像素、音频或声纹数据。模型仅在英语数据上训练，即可在零样本设置下对五种未见生成器和七种语言实现有效检测，展现出强大的跨语言泛化能力。
OrpQuant: Geometric Orthogonal Residual Projection for Multiplier-Free Power-of-Two Transformer Quantization
Authors: Maoyang Xiang, Bo Wang, Tao Luo
First: 2026-05-25T17:52:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:52:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) on edge devices is significantly constrained by memory limitations and the critical timing bottlenecks introduced by dense Multiply-Accumulate (MAC) arrays. In the ultra-low bit regime, logarithmic Power-of-Two (PoT) quantization provides a hardware-efficient alternative by replacing MAC operations with bit-shifts. However, the non-uniform exponential lattice is inherently limited by a \textbf{Low Angular Resolution Regime}, a structural flaw that becomes particularly pronounced at sub-4-bit thresholds, leading to a notable degradation of high-dimensional feature manifolds. To address this geometric limitation, we propose Orthogonal Residual Projection (ORP), an algorithm-hardware co-design framework. By formulating quantization as a dual-basis geometric projection, ORP adaptively synthesizes a higher-resolution residual lattice using strictly shift-and-add operations. Furthermore, ORP's analytical solver offers a practical alternative to computationally intensive gradient-based optimization, reducing the full-model calibration time for LLaMA-2-7B to approximately \textbf{15 minutes}. Extensive evaluations demonstrate ORP's applicability across modalities and its hardware efficiency. Under the 3-bit (W3/A16) constraint, ORP achieves a perplexity of 6.10 on LLaMA-2-7B, comparing favorably to conventional MAC-intensive baselines like AWQ without relying on asymmetric scaling, while maintaining competitive accuracy in 4-bit scenarios. At the silicon level, standard-cell RTL synthesis at a 28nm node indicates that ORP effectively mitigates the timing bottlenecks associated with dense multiplier trees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OrpQuant：用于无乘法器幂次二量化变换的几何正交残差投影
大型语言模型（LLMs）和视觉变换器（ViTs）在边缘设备上的部署受内存限制和密集乘累加（MAC）阵列引入的关键时序瓶颈显著制约。在超低位域中，对数幂次二（PoT）量化通过将MAC操作替换为位移操作，提供了一种硬件高效的替代方案。然而，非均匀指数格点固有地受限于\textbf{低角分辨率域}，这一结构缺陷在低于4比特阈值时尤为明显，导致高维特征流形显著退化。 为解决这一几何限制，我们提出正交残差投影（ORP），一种算法-硬件协同设计框架。通过将量化建模为双基几何投影，ORP自适应地利用纯移位与加法操作合成更高分辨率的残差格点。此外，ORP的解析求解器提供了一种替代计算密集型梯度优化的实用方案，将LLaMA-2-7B全模型校准时间缩短至约\textbf{15分钟}。 广泛评估表明ORP在多模态场景中具有适用性及硬件效率。在3比特（W3/A16）约束下，ORP在LLaMA-2-7B上实现困惑度6.10，相比传统依赖MAC运算的基线（如AWQ）表现更优，且无需非对称缩放，同时在4比特场景中保持竞争力。在硅片层面，28nm标准单元RTL综合结果表明，ORP有效缓解了密集乘法器树结构相关的时序瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the performance degradation in ultra-low-bit quantization of Transformers caused by the geometric limitations of logarithmic Power-of-Two quantization, the authors propose Orthogonal Residual Projection (ORP), a co-designed algorithm-hardware framework that reformulates quantization as a geometric projection onto a higher-resolution residual lattice using only shift-and-add operations. ORP’s analytical solver eliminates the need for gradient-based optimization, reducing full-model calibration time for LLaMA-2-7B to 15 minutes. Experimental results show ORP achieves a perplexity of 6.10 on 3-bit LLaMA-2-7B, outperforming MAC-intensive baselines like AWQ without asymmetric scaling, while maintaining competitive accuracy at 4-bit and effectively mitigating timing bottlenecks in 28nm RTL synthesis.
为解决大模型在边缘设备部署中的内存与计算瓶颈，本文提出OrpQuant方法，利用Power-of-Two量化将乘加操作替换为位移操作。针对指数格点低角分辨率的几何缺陷，该方法提出正交残差投影（ORP）框架，通过移位与加法自适应构建高分辨率残差格点。其解析求解器避免梯度优化，将LLaMA-2-7B校准时间缩短至15分钟。实验表明，在3比特约束下，ORP在LLaMA-2-7B上达到6.10的困惑度，优于无需非对称缩放的MAC密集型基线AWQ，且在4比特场景保持竞争力，硅片级28nm实现验证其有效缓解乘法器树的时序瓶颈。
Channel-wise Vector Quantization
Authors: Wei Song, Tianhang Wang, Yitong Chen, Tong Zhang, Zuxuan Wu, Ming Li, Jiaqi Wang, Kaicheng Yu
First: 2026-05-25T17:52:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:52:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Channel-wise Vector Quantization (CVQ), a novel image tokenization paradigm that replaces patch-wise tokens with channel-wise tokens. Unlike conventional vector quantization, which assigns a discrete token to each patch feature vector, CVQ quantizes each channel of the feature map. This formulation represents an image as discrete levels of visual details, rather than as a grid of spatial patches. Based on CVQ, we introduce a new visual autoregressive framework with "next-channel prediction". Instead of rendering images patch by patch in raster order, our Channel-wise Autoregressive (CAR) model predicts image channels sequentially, producing progressively enriched visual details. Specifically, it first sketches global structure and then refines fine-grained attributes, akin to a human artist's workflow. Empirically, we show that: (1) CVQ achieves 100% codebook utilization with a 16K+ codebook size without any bells and whistles, and substantially improves reconstruction quality over conventional VQ; and (2) CAR attains a DPG score of 86.7 and a GenEval score of 0.79, demonstrating strong effectiveness for text-to-image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通道级向量量化
我们提出了通道级向量量化（CVQ），一种新颖的图像分词范式，用通道级分词替代了传统的块级分词。与传统向量量化将离散分词分配给每个块特征向量不同，CVQ对特征图的每个通道进行量化。该方法将图像表示为视觉细节的离散层级，而非空间块的网格。基于CVQ，我们引入了一种新的视觉自回归框架——“下一通道预测”。与按光栅顺序逐块生成图像不同，我们的通道级自回归（CAR）模型按顺序预测图像通道，逐步丰富视觉细节。具体而言，它先勾勒全局结构，再细化细粒度属性，类似于人类艺术家的工作流程。实验证明：（1）CVQ在无任何额外优化的情况下，仅用16K+码本即可实现100%码本利用率，并显著优于传统VQ的重建质量；（2）CAR在DPG得分达86.7、GenEval得分达0.79，展现出强大的文本到图像生成能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of patch-wise tokenization in image modeling, this work introduces Channel-wise Vector Quantization (CVQ), which quantizes feature maps channel-by-channel rather than patch-by-patch, enabling more efficient representation of visual details. The method replaces conventional vector quantization with channel-level discrete tokens, allowing an image to be represented as a sequence of channel-wise visual levels. Building on CVQ, the authors propose Channel-wise Autoregressive (CAR), a novel autoregressive framework that predicts image channels sequentially, starting with global structure and refining toward fine-grained details. Experiments show CVQ achieves 100% codebook utilization with a 16K+ codebook without additional tricks and significantly outperforms standard VQ in reconstruction. CAR achieves a DPG score of 86.7 and GenEval score of 0.79, demonstrating strong performance in text-to-image generation.
为提升图像生成中向量量化效率，本文提出通道级向量量化（CVQ），将特征图按通道而非图像块进行量化，使视觉细节以离散层级表示。该方法构建了通道级自回归模型（CAR），按通道顺序生成图像，先构建全局结构再细化局部细节，模拟人类绘画流程。实验表明，CVQ在16K+码本规模下实现100%码本利用率，且无需额外技巧即可显著优于传统VQ；CAR在文本到图像生成任务中取得DPG分数86.7和GenEval分数0.79，表现优异。
HALvest-Contrastive: Retrieval-Like Authorship Attribution with Patch-Level Late Interaction
Authors: Francis Kulumba, Wissam Antoun, Guillaume Vimont, Laurent Romary, Florian Cafiero
First: 2024-07-30T07:14:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:50:46+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Authorship attribution asks whether two pieces of text share a writer, but topical confound makes the task deceptively easy: two authors covering the same topic may look more alike than one author covering two topics. Scholarly prose offers a natural remedy, academic writers produce multiple papers on related but distinct topics while maintaining consistent stylistic habits. We introduce HALvest, a 17-billion-token multilingual corpus of open-access academic papers, and its English contrastive derivative HALvest-Contrastive, where same-author passages are drawn from distinct papers within a disciplinary field to minimize topical overlap. We validate our benchmark by showing that a strong lexical baseline collapses once topical shortcuts are removed. On this same benchmark, we revisit how authorship is scored. Standard systems compress each document into a single vector. We instead keep a sequence of vectors and compare them with late interaction, then propose patch-level late interaction, which groups neighboring tokens into patches before matching. Matching at the sequence level greatly improves performance over the single-vector baseline, but the optimal interaction granularity is subtle.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HALvest-对比：基于补丁级晚期交互的检索式作者归属
作者归属任务旨在判断两段文本是否出自同一作者，但主题混淆使该任务看似简单：两位作者撰写相同主题的内容可能比一位作者撰写不同主题的内容更相似。学术写作天然提供了解决方案：学术作者在相关但不同的主题上撰写多篇论文，同时保持一致的风格习惯。我们引入了HALvest，一个包含170亿词的多语言开放获取学术论文语料库，以及其英文对比版本HALvest-对比，其中同作者段落来自同一学科领域内不同论文，以最小化主题重叠。我们通过验证基准表明，一旦移除主题捷径，强词典基线将失效。在该基准上，我们重新审视作者归属评分方式。标准系统将每篇文档压缩为单个向量。我们则保留序列向量并采用晚期交互进行比较，进而提出补丁级晚期交互，即将相邻词元分组为补丁后再进行匹配。序列级匹配相比单向量基线显著提升性能，但最优交互粒度微妙。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the confounding effect of topic similarity in authorship attribution, this work introduces HALvest-Contrastive, a multilingual academic corpus designed to minimize topical overlap by pairing same-author passages from distinct papers within disciplines. The method employs patch-level late interaction, grouping neighboring tokens into patches before comparing document representations, moving beyond standard single-vector compression. Experimental results show that this approach significantly outperforms lexical baselines and single-vector methods, demonstrating that fine-grained, sequence-level interaction is critical for accurate authorship attribution in scholarly text.
为解决作者归属任务中主题相似性带来的干扰，该研究构建了HALvest-Contrastive语料库，从中选取同一作者在不同论文中的段落以降低主题重叠。实验表明，当消除主题捷径后，传统词典基线方法失效。作者提出“块级后期交互”方法，将相邻词元分组后进行比较，相比单向量基线显著提升性能，验证了更细粒度交互的有效性。
DiscoverPhysics: Benchmarking LLMs for Out-of-the-Box Scientific Thinking
Authors: Matt L. Wiemann, Lindsay M. Smith, Peter Melchior, Siddharth Mishra-Sharma, Andrew Gordon Wilson, Pavel Izmailov, Carolina Cuesta-Lázaro
First: 2026-05-25T17:50:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:50:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier LLMs now perform strongly across a wide range of physics evaluations, but it is hard to disentangle genuine reasoning from recall of established science. We introduce DiscoverPhysics, an interactive benchmark that asks a LLM agent to discover the laws of motion of a simulated world whose physics deliberately deviates from our own. We construct 22 worlds governed by, among others, screened and fractional-power gravity, multi-species couplings, hidden dark-matter-like particles, non-coordinate-free physics, and time-varying interactions. Each world is generated on demand by an N-body simulator, for which the agent proposes several rounds of experiments, observes raw trajectory data, and ultimately submits both a natural-language explanation of the world's physics and a Python implementation of the inferred law. Because solving a world requires the agent to design informative experiments and revise its hypotheses, the benchmark probes long-horizon reasoning over an experimental history. We evaluate submissions along two complementary axes: trajectory MSE on held-out particles and an LLM-judged explanation score following an expert-written rubric assessing conceptual understanding of each world. Across eleven frontier models, we find that the strongest agents pass only half of the worlds and consistently fail on those where latent structure must be uncovered. Open-source models lag substantially behind commercial models, both in their ability to design informative experiments and in extracting conclusions from the data. We further find that good predictive accuracy does not guarantee high explanation quality and that conceptual understanding depends on hypothesis refinement through well-chosen experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiscoverPhysics：用于评估大语言模型科学思维能力的基准测试
前沿大语言模型在广泛的物理评估中表现强劲，但难以区分真实推理与对既有科学知识的回忆。我们提出了DiscoverPhysics，一个交互式基准测试，要求大语言模型代理发现一个模拟世界的运动定律，该世界的物理规律故意偏离现实。我们构建了22个世界，其物理规律包括屏蔽引力、分数幂次引力、多物种耦合、类暗物质粒子、非坐标无关物理以及随时间变化的相互作用等。每个世界由N体模拟器按需生成，代理需提出多轮实验、观察原始轨迹数据，并最终提交对该世界物理规律的自然语言解释及推断定律的Python实现。由于解决一个世界需要代理设计有信息量的实验并修正假设，该基准测试考察了代理在实验历史上的长期推理能力。我们从两个互补维度评估提交结果：在未参与训练粒子上的轨迹均方误差，以及基于专家制定评分标准的LLM评估解释质量。在11个前沿模型中，最强代理仅能通过一半世界，且在需要揭示潜在结构的世界中持续失败。开源模型在设计有信息量实验和从数据中提取结论的能力上显著落后于商业模型。我们进一步发现，高预测准确性并不保证高解释质量，概念理解依赖于通过精心选择的实验进行假设修正。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to distinguish genuine scientific reasoning from mere recall in large language models, the paper introduces DiscoverPhysics, an interactive benchmark where LLM agents must discover physical laws in simulated worlds with non-standard physics. The method involves constructing 22 distinct worlds with altered physical rules, each generated on demand via an N-body simulator, requiring agents to design experiments, analyze trajectory data, and submit both natural-language explanations and Python code implementing inferred laws. Experimental results show that even top frontier models succeed in only half the worlds, particularly failing those requiring latent structure discovery; open-source models underperform commercial ones in both experimental design and inference; and high predictive accuracy does not correlate with strong conceptual understanding, which depends on iterative hypothesis refinement through well-chosen experiments.
为区分大语言模型中的真实科学推理与记忆性输出，作者设计了DiscoverPhysics基准，要求模型在具有非标准物理规则的模拟世界中发现物理定律。方法包括生成22个不同物理规则的世界，通过N体模拟器动态生成，模型需设计实验、观测轨迹数据，并提交自然语言解释与Python代码实现推断的物理规律。实验结果显示，即使顶尖模型也只能通过一半世界，尤其在需推断隐含结构的世界中表现不佳；商业模型显著优于开源模型，且高预测准确率并不保证解释质量或概念理解深度。
Claw-Anything: Benchmarking Always-On Personal Assistants with Broader Access to User's Digital World
Authors: Yusong Lin, Xinyuan Liang, Haiyang Wang, Qipeng Gu, Siqi Cheng, Jiangui Chen, Shuzhe Wu, Feiyang Pan, Lue Fan, Sanyuan Zhao, Dandan Tu
First: 2026-05-25T17:50:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:50:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model agents are increasingly envisioned as always-on personal assistants with access to anything relevant in the user's digital world. Yet current systems operate over only narrow slices of that world, limiting context-sensitive reasoning and effective assistance. Existing benchmarks similarly provide only partial user state and therefore fail to capture performance in such a broad, always-on setting. To address this gap, we introduce Claw-Anything, a benchmark that expands agent context along three dimensions: long-horizon activity histories, interdependent backend services, and integrated GUI and CLI interaction across multiple devices. To instantiate this setting, we simulate months of user activity through multi-round event injection, producing complex world states and realistic noise, including irrelevant events and conflicting signals. Agents must reason over rich contextual environments while remaining robust to such noise. This expanded scope also enables the evaluation of proactive assistance, requiring agents to anticipate user needs and deliver timely recommendations. Experiments show that GPT-5.5 achieves only 34.5% pass@1, substantially below prior benchmarks, underscoring a gap between current agent capabilities and the demands of always-on personal assistance. Alongside the benchmark, we release an automated data-generation pipeline that yields 2,000 training environments and improves the base model by 23.7%, demonstrating its utility of scalable data infrastructure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Claw-Anything：通过更广泛访问用户数字世界来基准测试始终在线个人助手
大型语言模型代理正被设想为始终在线的个人助手，可访问用户数字世界中任何相关的内容。然而，当前系统仅能操作该世界中的狭窄部分，限制了情境感知推理和有效协助。现有基准测试同样仅提供部分用户状态，因此无法捕捉在如此广泛、始终在线环境中的表现。为解决这一差距，我们引入Claw-Anything基准测试，从三个维度扩展代理上下文：长时段活动历史、相互依赖的后台服务，以及跨多个设备的集成GUI和CLI交互。为实现该环境，我们通过多轮事件注入模拟数月用户活动，生成复杂世界状态和现实噪声，包括无关事件和冲突信号。代理必须在丰富的情境环境中进行推理，同时保持对噪声的鲁棒性。该扩展范围还支持主动协助的评估，要求代理预测用户需求并及时提供推荐。实验表明，GPT-5.5仅达到34.5%的pass@1，远低于先前基准，凸显当前代理能力与始终在线个人助手需求之间的差距。同时，我们发布了一个自动化数据生成流水线，生成2000个训练环境，并使基础模型性能提升23.7%，证明了可扩展数据基础设施的实用性。

Motivated by the need to evaluate large language model agents as always-on personal assistants with full access to a user's digital world, the authors introduce Claw-Anything, a benchmark that expands agent context across long-horizon activity histories, interdependent backend services, and cross-device GUI/CLI interactions.
