为评估大语言模型作为全天候个人助理在用户数字生态中的能力，作者提出Claw-Anything基准，扩展了代理上下文维度，涵盖长时活动日志、相互依赖的后台服务及跨设备GUI/CLI交互。通过多轮事件注入模拟数月用户行为，生成包含无关事件与冲突信号的复杂噪声环境。代理需在这些丰富上下文中稳健推理并主动预判用户需求。实验显示GPT-5.5仅达34.5% pass@1，远低于先前基准，揭示当前能力与全天候助理需求间的显著差距。配套自动化数据生成流水线产出2000个训练环境，使基线模型性能提升23.7%，验证了可扩展数据基础设施的价值。
Subspace Aggregation Query and Index Generation for Multidimensional Resource Space Model
Authors: Xiaoping Sun, Hai Zhuge
First: 2025-05-04T14:36:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:47:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Organizing large-scale resources in a multidimensional semantic space is an approach to efficiently managing and querying resources from different semantic dimensions. To support advanced applications, this paper proposes a resource space model for aggregation query on subspaces defined by a range within the partial order on the coordinate trees representing each dimension, where each point in the subspace contains resources aggregated along the paths of the partial order relations on the coordinate trees and the aggregated resources at each point can be measured, ranked and selected by applications. To efficiently locate non-empty points in a large subspace, an approach to generating graph index is proposed to build partial order relations on coordinates of dimensions to enable a subspace query to reach non-empty points through indexing links and aggregate resources along indexing paths to their super points. Generating such an index is costly as the number of children of an indexing node can be large so that the total number of indexing nodes can be very large (exponentially growing with the number of dimensions and scale of dimensions). The proposed approach adopts the a set of strategies to reduce the cost. Analysis and experiments show the effectiveness of the generated index in supporting subspace aggregation query.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多维资源空间模型的子空间聚合查询与索引生成
在多维语义空间中组织大规模资源，是高效管理与查询不同语义维度资源的一种方法。为支持高级应用，本文提出一种资源空间模型，用于在由坐标树表示的各维度偏序关系范围内定义的子空间上执行聚合查询，其中子空间中的每个点包含沿坐标树偏序关系路径聚合的资源，且每个点的聚合资源可被应用测量、排序和选择。为高效定位大型子空间中的非空点，本文提出一种图索引生成方法，通过构建维度坐标间的偏序关系，使子空间查询可通过索引链接定位非空点，并沿索引路径聚合资源至其超点。由于索引节点的子节点数量可能很大，导致索引节点总数呈指数级增长（随维度数和维度规模增长），因此本文提出一系列策略降低生成成本。分析与实验表明，所生成索引在支持子空间聚合查询方面有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently manage and query large-scale resources across multiple semantic dimensions, this paper introduces a resource space model that enables aggregation queries over subspaces defined by coordinate ranges in partial order trees. The method constructs a graph-based index that maps partial order relations across dimensions to locate non-empty resource points and aggregate resources along indexing paths to their super points. To mitigate the exponential growth of indexing nodes, the approach employs cost-reduction strategies. Experimental results confirm that the proposed index effectively supports subspace aggregation queries while significantly reducing computational overhead.
为高效管理多语义维度下的大规模资源，本文提出一种资源空间模型，支持基于坐标树偏序关系定义的子空间聚合查询。方法通过构建图索引，沿索引路径定位非空点并沿路径聚合资源至超点。为应对高维与大规模坐标空间导致的索引节点指数级增长问题，采用多种成本优化策略。实验结果表明，所提索引方法能有效支持子空间聚合查询，且计算开销可控。
VeriTrace: Evolving Mental Models for Deep Research Agents
Authors: Haolang Zhao, Yunbo Long, Lukas Beckenbauer, Alexandra Brintrup
First: 2026-05-25T17:46:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:46:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep research agents face vast, interdependent, and pervasively uncertain information. Existing systems explore what evolving intermediate representations should look like, but leave their evolution to the LLM's implicit reasoning. Without explicit regulation, the intermediate layer is easily contaminated by mixed-quality information and propagates errors along its dependencies, so model scale often ends up substituting for absent regulation. We argue that an agent's mental model should instead evolve through explicit feedback that continuously aligns task understanding with reality, and identify three regulatory loops: interpretive update, deviation feedback, and schema revision. We realise this in VeriTrace, a cognitive-graph framework that explicitly implements the three loops. Using matched Qwen3.5-27B backbones, VeriTrace improves over the strongest matched baseline by 4.22 pp on DeepResearch Bench (DRB) Insight (1.49 pp Overall) and by 5.9 pp Overall win rate on DeepConsult. With Config-DeepSeek, it achieves the strongest reproducible open-source result on DRB.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VeriTrace：面向深度研究代理的演进心智模型
深度研究代理面临海量、相互依赖且普遍不确定的信息。现有系统探索中间表示应如何演化，但将其演化依赖于大语言模型的隐式推理。缺乏显式调控时，中间层易受混杂质量信息污染，并沿依赖关系传播错误，导致模型规模常替代缺失的调控。我们认为，代理的心智模型应通过显式反馈持续演进，使任务理解与现实对齐，并识别三种调控循环：解释性更新、偏差反馈与模式修订。我们在VeriTrace中实现该框架，其为认知图框架，显式实现上述三类循环。使用匹配的Qwen3.5-27B骨干模型，VeriTrace在DeepResearch Bench（DRB）Insight上相较最强基线提升4.22个百分点（整体提升1.49个百分点），在DeepConsult上整体胜率提升5.9个百分点。使用Config-DeepSeek配置时，其在DRB上达成最强可复现开源结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage uncertainty and interdependence in deep research tasks, VeriTrace introduces a cognitive-graph framework that explicitly evolves an agent’s mental model through three regulatory loops: interpretive update, deviation feedback, and schema revision. Unlike prior systems that rely on implicit LLM reasoning, VeriTrace uses explicit feedback to align task understanding with reality, preventing error propagation from mixed-quality information. Experiments with Qwen3.5-27B show VeriTrace outperforms the strongest baseline by 4.22 percentage points on DRB Insight and 5.9 percentage points on Overall win rate in DeepConsult, achieving the best reproducible open-source result on DRB with Config-DeepSeek.
为应对深度研究任务中信息的不确定性与相互依赖性，VeriTrace提出一种认知图框架，通过三个显式调节循环——解释性更新、偏差反馈与模式修订——显式演化代理的思维模型，而非依赖大语言模型的隐式推理。该方法通过结构化反馈持续对齐任务理解与现实，避免低质量信息污染导致的错误传播。实验表明，使用Qwen3.5-27B模型时，VeriTrace在DRB Insight上比最强基线高出4.22个百分点，在DeepConsult整体胜率上提升5.9个百分点，并在DRB上取得最佳可复现开源结果。
Automated Benchmark Auditing for AI Agents and Large Language Models
Authors: Junlin Wang, Federico Bianchi, Shang Zhu, Fan Nie, Yongchan Kwon, Bhuwan Dhingra, James Zou
First: 2026-05-25T17:44:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:44:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern AI benchmarks operate at a complexity that outpaces traditional verification methods. Tasks authored by domain experts often contain implicit assumptions, incomplete environment specifications, and brittle evaluation logic that human annotation cannot reliably catch. We introduce Auto Benchmark Audit (ABA), an agentic framework that systematically audits individual benchmark tasks, uncovering issues such as hidden environment dependencies, specification gaps, and limited grading logic. We run ABA on a collection of frontier LLM benchmarks and previous NeurIPS publications, totaling 168 benchmarks across nine domains. Across this corpus, ABA identifies critical issues including ambiguous task design, execution environment conflicts, and incorrect ground truths in over 25.7% of the evaluated tasks. The precision of these automated audits is validated by expert review and independent third-party reports such as upstream PRs. Crucially, we demonstrate that these problematic tasks severely distorts capability assessments for agents and LLMs: filtering out these tasks with issues shifts model rankings and increases average performance on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2 by 9.9% and 9.6%, respectively. We release the agentic tool and all task annotations to support the future development of frontier benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向AI代理与大语言模型的自动化基准审计
现代AI基准测试的复杂度已超出传统验证方法的能力。由领域专家编写的任务常包含隐含假设、不完整环境规范和脆弱的评估逻辑，人类标注难以可靠发现这些问题。我们提出了Auto Benchmark Audit（ABA），一种代理框架，系统性地审计单个基准任务，揭示隐藏的环境依赖、规范漏洞和有限评分逻辑等问题。我们在一组前沿LLM基准及过往NeurIPS论文中运行ABA，涵盖9个领域共168个基准。在该语料库中，ABA在超过25.7%的评估任务中发现关键问题，包括任务设计模糊、执行环境冲突和错误的基准答案。这些自动化审计的精确性经专家评审及上游PR等第三方独立报告验证。重要的是，我们证明这些有问题的任务严重扭曲了代理与LLM的能力评估：剔除这些问题任务后，模型排名发生改变，SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench 2的平均性能分别提升9.9%和9.6%。我们发布该代理工具及所有任务注释，以支持前沿基准的未来发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing complexity of AI benchmarks and the limitations of human verification, this paper introduces Auto Benchmark Audit (ABA), an agentic framework designed to systematically audit benchmark tasks for hidden dependencies, specification gaps, and flawed evaluation logic. Applied to 168 benchmarks across nine domains from frontier LLM evaluations and NeurIPS publications, ABA uncovered critical issues—including ambiguous task design and incorrect ground truths—in over 25.7% of tasks. These findings were validated by expert review and third-party reports. Crucially, removing problematic tasks improved model performance rankings and boosted average scores on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2 by 9.9% and 9.6%, respectively, demonstrating the significant impact of benchmark quality on capability assessment.
鉴于当前AI基准测试复杂度日益提升且人工验证能力有限，本文提出Auto Benchmark Audit（ABA）框架，用于系统性审计基准任务中的隐含依赖、规范缺失与评估逻辑缺陷。在对168个来自前沿大模型评估与NeurIPS论文的基准任务进行审计后，ABA发现超过25.7%的任务存在关键问题，如任务设计模糊或真实答案错误，且结果经专家评审与第三方报告验证。剔除这些有问题的任务后，模型在SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench 2上的平均表现分别提升9.9%和9.6%，显著改善了能力评估的准确性。
Global Convergence of Wasserstein Policy Gradient for Entropy-Regularized Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Zhaoyu Zhu, Rui Gao, Shuang Li
First: 2026-05-25T17:42:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:42:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Wasserstein policy gradient (WPG) is a policy optimization method for reinforcement learning (RL) that exploits the optimal-transport geometry of action distributions. For the entropy-regularized RL objective, WPG evolves each state-conditional policy by transporting it along the action gradient of the soft Q-function together with a Langevin-type diffusion. Despite its appeal for continuous-control problems, its global convergence properties remain poorly understood. Standard Langevin analyses do not directly apply, because the RL objective depends on the policy through the Bellman recursion rather than through a static convex functional, and the Langevin drift is determined by the soft Q-function, whose regularity must be controlled along the policy iterates. In this paper, we develop a global convergence theory for WPG by exploiting the Bellman structure of entropy-regularized RL. We show that the role usually played by convexity can be replaced by a Bellman-based argument: the soft Bellman residual admits a statewise KL representation with respect to a Gibbs policy; Bellman contraction relates this residual to the global optimality gap; and a Bellman resolvent identity connects value improvement to relative Fisher information. Combined with a uniform log-Sobolev inequality (LSI) for the evolving Gibbs family, these ingredients yield a distributional Polyak--Łojasiewicz condition. We further establish the regularity and uniform bounds needed to control the discretization error, thereby obtaining geometric contraction up to a discretization bias. Conceptually, our analysis shows that although entropy-regularized RL is not convex in the usual flat sense, the Bellman recursion induces a favorable Polyak--Lojasiewicz-type (PL) geometry that supports global convergence of WPG.
中文标题/摘要
标题：熵正则化强化学习中Wasserstein策略梯度的全局收敛性
Wasserstein策略梯度（WPG）是一种利用动作分布最优传输几何结构的强化学习策略优化方法。对于熵正则化RL目标，WPG通过沿软Q函数的动作梯度并结合朗之万型扩散，演化每个状态条件策略。尽管其在连续控制问题中具有吸引力，但其全局收敛性质仍不明确。标准朗之万分析不直接适用，因为RL目标通过贝尔曼递归依赖于策略，而非静态凸函数，且朗之万漂移由软Q函数决定，其正则性需沿策略迭代过程控制。 本文通过利用熵正则化RL的贝尔曼结构，发展了WPG的全局收敛理论。我们表明，通常由凸性所扮演的角色可被贝尔曼论证替代：软贝尔曼残差相对于吉布斯策略具有状态-wise KL表示；贝尔曼收缩将该残差与全局最优间隙关联；贝尔曼共振恒等式将价值改进与相对Fisher信息联系起来。结合演化吉布斯族的统一对数索博列夫不等式（LSI），这些要素共同导出分布型Polyak–Łojasiewicz条件。我们进一步建立了控制离散化误差所需的正则性与统一界，从而获得至离散化偏差的几何收缩。概念上，我们的分析表明，尽管熵正则化RL在通常的平坦意义下非凸，但贝尔曼递归诱导出有利的Polyak–Łojasiewicz型（PL）几何结构，支持WPG的全局收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand global convergence in entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, this paper analyzes Wasserstein policy gradient (WPG), a method that uses optimal transport to evolve policies along action gradients of the soft Q-function with Langevin diffusion. The authors develop a theoretical framework leveraging the Bellman structure of the RL objective, replacing convexity with Bellman-based arguments: they show the soft Bellman residual admits a statewise KL divergence representation, relate it to the global optimality gap via Bellman contraction, and connect value improvement to relative Fisher information via a resolvent identity. Combined with a uniform log-Sobolev inequality for the evolving Gibbs policies, these yield a distributional Polyak–Łojasiewicz condition, enabling geometric contraction up to discretization bias, thereby proving global convergence of WPG despite the non-convex nature of the problem.
为理解熵正则化强化学习中的全局收敛性，本文分析了利用最优传输优化策略的Wasserstein策略梯度（WPG）方法，其通过由软Q函数引导的朗之万扩散进行策略更新。作者利用贝尔曼递归结构构建理论框架，用贝尔曼方法替代凸性：证明软贝尔曼残差可表示为状态间KL散度，通过贝尔曼收缩将其与全局最优间隙关联，并利用贝尔曼共振恒等式将价值提升与相对Fisher信息联系起来。结合演化Gibbs族的统一对数索博列夫不等式，这些要素共同构建分布型Polyak–Łojasiewicz条件，实现几何收敛（至离散化偏差），即使问题不具备标准凸性。
RecGOAT: Graph Optimal Adaptive Transport for LLM-Enhanced Multimodal Recommendation with Dual Semantic Alignment
Authors: Yuecheng Li, Hengwei Ju, Zeyu Song, Wei Yang, Chi Lu, Peng Jiang, Kun Gai
First: 2026-01-31T11:58:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:41:39+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Integrating large language model (LLM) representations into multimodal recommendation has shown promise, yet a fundamental challenge remains largely overlooked: the semantic heterogeneity between generative LM representations and the ID-based collaborative signals that recommendation systems rely on. Naively injecting LM features without alignment degrades recommendation performance rather than improving it. To resolve this, we propose RecGOAT, a dual-granularity semantic alignment framework built on graph neural networks and optimal transport theory. RecGOAT first enriches collaborative semantics through multimodal attentive graphs that capture item-item, user-item, and user-user relationships, initializing user representations via LLM-inferred behavioral preferences. It then aligns LM-derived modality representations with recommendation IDs at two complementary granularities: (1) instance-level alignment via cross-modal contrastive learning (CMCL), which produces discriminative per-sample representations; and (2) distribution-level alignment via optimal adaptive transport (OAT), which minimizes the 1-Wasserstein distance between ID distributions and LLM semantics to produce a unified, consistently aligned feature space. Theoretically, we prove that the unified representation achieves strictly lower target error than any single-modality representation, with the gap bounded by the Wasserstein distance and the InfoNCE loss, providing rigorous guarantees for both alignment consistency and fusion comprehensiveness. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. Deployment on a large-scale online advertising platform further validates RecGOAT's industrial scalability. Our code is available at https://github.com/6lyc/RecGOAT-LLM4Rec.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RecGOAT：基于图最优传输的LLM增强多模态推荐与双语义对齐
将大语言模型（LLM）表示融入多模态推荐已显现出潜力，但一个根本性挑战仍被忽视：生成式LM表示与推荐系统依赖的基于ID的协同信号之间存在语义异质性。若未经对齐直接注入LM特征，反而会降低推荐性能。为此，我们提出RecGOAT，一种基于图神经网络与最优传输理论的双粒度语义对齐框架。RecGOAT首先通过多模态注意力图丰富协同语义，捕捉物品-物品、用户-物品及用户-用户关系，并通过LLM推断的行为偏好初始化用户表示；随后在两个互补粒度上对齐LM衍生模态表示与推荐ID：(1) 实例级对齐通过跨模态对比学习（CMCL），生成判别性样本级表示；(2) 分布级对齐通过最优自适应传输（OAT），最小化ID分布与LLM语义间的1-维Wasserstein距离，构建统一且一致对齐的特征空间。理论上，我们证明统一表示的目标误差严格低于任一单模态表示，误差差距由Wasserstein距离与InfoNCE损失界定，为对齐一致性与融合全面性提供严格保障。在三个公开基准上的大量实验验证了其最先进性能，大规模在线广告平台部署进一步证实其工业可扩展性。代码开源于https://github.com/6lyc/RecGOAT-LLM4Rec。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning generative LLM representations with ID-based collaborative signals in multimodal recommendation, RecGOAT introduces a dual-granularity semantic alignment framework combining graph neural networks and optimal transport theory. It first constructs multimodal attentive graphs to capture item-item, user-item, and user-user relationships, initializing user representations via LLM-inferred behavioral preferences. Then, it aligns LLM-derived modality features with recommendation IDs at two levels: instance-level via cross-modal contrastive learning for sample-specific discrimination, and distribution-level via optimal adaptive transport to minimize 1-Wasserstein distance, creating a unified feature space. Theoretical analysis proves the unified representation achieves strictly lower error than any single modality, bounded by Wasserstein and InfoNCE losses. Experiments on three public benchmarks show state-of-the-art performance, and deployment on a large-scale advertising platform confirms scalability.
为解决LLM生成表示与推荐系统依赖的ID型协同信号语义异构问题，RecGOAT提出基于图神经网络与最优传输理论的双粒度语义对齐框架。首先构建多模态注意力图，捕捉物品-物品、用户-物品、用户-用户关系，并通过LLM推断的用户偏好初始化用户表示。随后在两个粒度上对齐模态特征与推荐ID：实例级通过跨模态对比学习生成判别性样本表示，分布级通过最优自适应传输最小化Wasserstein距离，统一特征空间。理论证明统一表示误差严格低于任一单模态表示，误差差距由Wasserstein距离与InfoNCE损失界定。在三个公开数据集上实验达到SOTA性能，大规模广告平台部署验证其工业可扩展性。
URS: A Unified Neural Routing Solver for Cross-Problem Zero-Shot Generalization
Authors: Changliang Zhou, Canhong Yu, Shunyu Yao, Xi Lin, Zhenkun Wang, Yu Zhou, Qingfu Zhang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-09-27T17:11:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:39:39+00:00
Comments: accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-task neural routing solvers have emerged as a promising paradigm for their ability to solve multiple vehicle routing problems (VRPs) using a single model. However, existing neural solvers typically rely on predefined problem constraints or require per-problem fine-tuning, which substantially limits their zero-shot generalization ability to unseen VRP variants. To address this critical bottleneck, we propose URS, a unified neural routing solver that achieves zero-shot generalization across a wide range of unseen VRPs with a single model. We propose a unified data representation (UDR) that replaces problem enumeration with data unification, thereby broadening the problem coverage and reducing reliance on domain expertise. In addition, we introduce a Mixed Bias Module (MBM) during encoding to improve node embeddings, which efficiently captures multiple priors inherent to various problems. On top of the UDR, we develop a problem-conditioned parameter generator to further improve zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments show that URS consistently produces high-quality solutions for 110 VRP variants (including 99 unseen variants) while demonstrating impressive scalability to large-scale instances with up to 7000 nodes. To the best of our knowledge, URS is the first neural solver to handle over 100 VRP variants with a single model. Our code is available at https://github.com/CIAM-Group/URS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：URS：一种统一的神经路由求解器，支持跨问题零样本泛化
多任务神经路由求解器因其能使用单一模型解决多种车辆路径问题（VRP）而成为有前景的范式。然而，现有神经求解器通常依赖预定义问题约束或需针对每个问题进行微调，这严重限制了其对未见VRP变体的零样本泛化能力。为解决这一关键瓶颈，我们提出URS，一种统一的神经路由求解器，可使用单一模型实现对大量未见VRP的零样本泛化。我们提出统一数据表示（UDR），用数据统一替代问题枚举，从而扩大问题覆盖范围并减少对领域知识的依赖。此外，我们在编码阶段引入混合偏置模块（MBM），以提升节点嵌入，高效捕捉各类问题固有的多重先验。在UDR基础上，我们开发了问题条件参数生成器，进一步提升零样本泛化能力。大量实验表明，URS在110种VRP变体（含99种未见变体）上持续生成高质量解，并展现出对大规模实例（最多7000节点）的优异可扩展性。据我们所知，URS是首个使用单一模型处理超过100种VRP变体的神经求解器。代码已开源：https://github.com/CIAM-Group/URS。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a single neural model capable of zero-shot generalization across diverse vehicle routing problems (VRPs), the authors propose URS, a unified neural routing solver that eliminates reliance on predefined constraints or per-problem fine-tuning. URS employs a unified data representation (UDR) to unify problem instances without enumeration, and introduces a Mixed Bias Module (MBM) to enhance node embeddings by capturing multiple problem priors during encoding. It further uses a problem-conditioned parameter generator to adapt to unseen VRP variants. Experiments demonstrate that URS delivers high-quality solutions for 110 VRP variants—including 99 unseen ones—and scales effectively to large instances with up to 7000 nodes, setting a new record for the number of VRP variants handled by a single model.
为解决现有神经求解器在零样本泛化能力上的局限，URS提出统一数据表示（UDR）替代问题枚举，降低对领域知识依赖，并引入混合偏置模块（MBM）增强节点嵌入以捕捉多类问题先验，再结合问题条件参数生成器提升泛化能力。实验表明，URS在110种VRP变体（含99种未见变体）上持续输出高质量解，并可扩展至7000节点规模的大规模实例，是首个单模型处理超百种VRP变体的神经求解器。
StakeBench: Evaluating Language Understanding Grounded in Market Commitment
Authors: Yunhua Pei, Jingyu Hu, Yiwei Shi, Hongnan Ma, Weiru Liu, John Cartlidge
First: 2026-05-25T17:38:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:38:30+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 2 figures, 20 tables. Preprint. Dataset and evaluation code included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing financial NLP benchmarks often rely on labels supplied by outside observers, measuring how language is perceived rather than what speakers have committed to in the market. We introduce StakeBench, an evaluation framework for language understanding grounded in market commitment. StakeBench links 560,876 comments from 2,261 resolved markets to verified position, action, and market-odds records across Polymarket and Manifold. Supervision is derived from observable market behavior. Position sides, post-comment trading actions, and market-odds trajectories replace human annotation. Four diagnostic tasks test whether models detect market commitment, identify the revealed side, anticipate future action, and perform collective odds projection. Three commitment-aware metrics measure alignment with revealed preferences rather than perceived sentiment. Validity audits and explicit interpretation boundaries help distinguish observable commitment signals from latent belief and causal market-odds impact. Across 15 LLMs and 18 topics and platform settings, models partially recover position-side signals, with Directed Accuracy from 0.506 to 0.599, but show structural failures on later tasks. Ten of the fifteen models collapse to one or two action labels in future action anticipation, and no model consistently improves on the naive odds-direction baseline in collective odds projection. Model scale is not correlated with performance, finance-domain tuning does not improve revealed-side identification, and platform incentives strongly shape higher-order results. StakeBench is packaged with evaluation code and dataset under CC-BY 4.0.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StakeBench：基于市场承诺的语言理解评估框架
现有金融NLP基准通常依赖外部观察者提供的标签，衡量语言的感知而非说话者在市场中的实际承诺。我们引入StakeBench，一个基于市场承诺的语言理解评估框架。StakeBench将2261个已解决市场的560,876条评论与Polymarket和Manifold平台上的验证持仓、操作及市场赔率记录关联。监督信号源自可观测的市场行为。持仓方向、评论后交易行为及市场赔率轨迹替代人工标注。四个诊断任务检验模型是否能检测市场承诺、识别揭示的立场、预测未来行动及进行集体赔率预测。三个承诺感知指标衡量与揭示偏好而非感知情绪的对齐程度。有效性审计和明确解释边界有助于区分可观测承诺信号与潜在信念及因果市场赔率影响。在15个LLM和18个主题及平台设置下，模型部分恢复持仓方向信号，定向准确率从0.506至0.599，但在后续任务中表现出结构性缺陷。十五个模型中有十个在预测未来行动时坍缩为一至两个行动标签，且无模型在集体赔率预测中持续优于朴素赔率方向基线。模型规模与性能无相关性，金融领域调优未提升揭示立场识别，平台激励强烈塑造高阶结果。StakeBench在CC-BY 4.0许可下提供评估代码和数据集。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing financial NLP benchmarks that rely on external human labels rather than actual market behavior, StakeBench introduces a novel evaluation framework grounded in observable market commitments. It links 560,876 resolved market comments to verified trading positions, actions, and odds records from Polymarket and Manifold, deriving supervision from real market behavior instead of human annotations. Four diagnostic tasks assess whether models can detect commitment, identify revealed sides, anticipate future actions, and project collective odds. Results show that while models partially recover position-side signals with Directed Accuracy ranging from 0.506 to 0.599, they consistently fail on later tasks: ten out of fifteen models collapse to one or two action labels in future action prediction, and none outperform a naive odds-direction baseline in collective odds projection. Model scale, finance-domain tuning, and platform incentives show no consistent correlation with performance improvements.
为克服现有金融NLP基准依赖外部人工标注、而非真实市场行为的缺陷，StakeBench提出一种基于市场承诺的评估框架。该框架将56万条已解决市场的评论与Polymarket和Manifold平台的验证交易头寸、操作记录和赔率数据关联，以市场行为本身作为监督信号。四个诊断任务检验模型是否能识别承诺、判断揭示立场、预测未来操作及集体赔率预测。结果表明，模型虽部分恢复头寸信号（定向准确率0.506–0.599），但在后续任务中普遍失效——十款模型在预测未来操作时坍缩为一到两个标签，且无模型在集体赔率预测上超越朴素赔率方向基线。模型规模、金融领域微调及平台激励均未显著提升性能。
Active Query Synthesis for Preference Learning
Authors: Namrata Nadagouda, Nauman Ahad, Maegan Tucker, Mark A. Davenport
First: 2026-05-25T17:37:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:37:58+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficient learning of user preferences is crucial for many modern decision making systems but typically requires costly labeled data. Active learning reduces this cost, yet standard methods are computationally expensive due to pool-based evaluation. Further, most methods assume all query feedback is equally reliable, ignoring that pairwise queries between nearly identical or entirely dissimilar items yield ambiguous, low-confidence responses. To address the issue of feedback reliability, we introduce a novel confidence aware response model that explicitly accounts for these ambiguous comparisons. To overcome the computational bottleneck of pool-based evaluation, we propose an active query synthesis framework, Info-Synth that generates optimal queries by maximizing a mutual information-based objective within a continuous space. Moreover, we propose two strategies, Pair M-dist and Pair Opt-dist, that extend Info-Synth to select effective queries even when restricted to finite query pools. We demonstrate our framework's versatility and performance across synthetic preference learning, constrained text summary datasets, and subjective, continuous-space controller gain tuning for a simulated mobile robot.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于偏好学习的主动查询合成
高效学习用户偏好对许多现代决策系统至关重要，但通常需要昂贵的标注数据。主动学习可降低该成本，但标准方法因基于池的评估而计算开销大。此外，多数方法假设所有查询反馈可靠性相同，忽略了在几乎相同或完全不同的项目间进行成对查询时，反馈往往模糊且置信度低。为解决反馈可靠性问题，我们引入了一种新颖的置信度感知响应模型，显式考虑这些模糊比较。为克服基于池评估的计算瓶颈，我们提出主动查询合成框架Info-Synth，通过最大化基于互信息的目标函数在连续空间中生成最优查询。此外，我们提出两种策略——Pair M-dist和Pair Opt-dist，将Info-Synth扩展至在有限查询池中仍能选择有效查询。我们在合成偏好学习、受限文本摘要数据集以及模拟移动机器人主观连续空间控制器增益调优中，展示了框架的通用性和性能。
Summary / 总结
To reduce the costly labeled data needed for preference learning, this paper proposes Info-Synth, an active query synthesis framework that generates optimal queries by maximizing mutual information in a continuous space, avoiding computationally expensive pool-based evaluation. It introduces a confidence-aware response model to handle ambiguous feedback from nearly identical or dissimilar items, and two practical strategies—Pair M-dist and Pair Opt-dist—to adapt the framework to finite query pools. Experiments across synthetic data, text summarization, and robot controller tuning show that Info-Synth achieves superior preference learning efficiency and accuracy compared to baseline methods.
为降低偏好学习中标注数据的成本并提升效率，本文提出Info-Synth主动查询合成框架，通过最大化互信息在连续空间中生成最优查询，避免池式评估的高计算开销。同时引入置信度感知响应模型，以处理近似或完全不相似项间反馈的模糊性。实验结果表明，该框架在合成数据、受限文本摘要及机器人控制器调优任务中，相比基线方法在偏好准确率和查询效率上均有显著提升，尤其在有限查询池下，Pair M-dist与Pair Opt-dist策略表现尤为突出。
WhoSaidIt: Human-LLM Collaborative Annotation for Text-Based Multilingual Speaker-Attribute Classification
Authors: Lingyu Gao, Will Monroe, David Smith, Meghan Jemison, Jackie Lee
First: 2026-05-25T17:37:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:37:45+00:00
Comments: 16 pages in total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotating speaker attributes from text is inherently ambiguous, particularly in multilingual settings where demographic and social cues are implicit and culturally variable. We propose a human-large language model (LLM) collaborative re-annotation framework for stabilizing multilingual speaker-attribute labels under practical resource constraints. Starting from a noisy corpus, we use LLMs to surface recurring annotation rationales through iterative interaction with experts, and apply disagreement-focused sampling for targeted re-annotation. Using this framework, we construct WhoSaidIt, a multilingual dataset covering nine speaker-attribute labels. We quantify divergence between original and revised annotations, benchmark recent LLMs, and analyze the effect of explicit rationales on model behavior. Our results reveal substantial cross-lingual differences in annotation decisions and demonstrate both the strengths and limitations of LLMs in speaker-attribute classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WhoSaidIt：基于人机协作标注的文本多语言说话人属性分类
从文本中标注说话人属性固有模糊，尤其在多语言环境下，人口统计和社会线索隐含且文化差异大。我们提出一种人类与大语言模型（LLM）协作重标注框架，以在实际资源限制下稳定多语言说话人属性标签。从噪声语料出发，我们利用LLM通过与专家的迭代交互揭示重复标注理由，并采用分歧导向采样进行针对性重标注。基于此框架，我们构建了WhoSaidIt数据集，涵盖九类说话人属性标签。我们量化原始与修订标注间的差异，评估近期LLM性能，并分析显式理由对模型行为的影响。结果揭示跨语言标注决策的显著差异，同时展示LLM在说话人属性分类中的优势与局限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the ambiguity and cultural variability of speaker-attribute annotation in multilingual text, the authors propose a human-LLM collaborative framework to stabilize labels under resource constraints. The method iteratively uses LLMs to surface annotation rationales, which are then refined through expert feedback and disagreement-focused sampling for targeted re-annotation. The resulting WhoSaidIt dataset, covering nine attributes across multiple languages, shows significant cross-lingual divergence in annotation decisions and reveals that while LLMs can capture patterns, their performance varies substantially across languages and is sensitive to explicit rationale guidance.
为解决跨语言文本中说话人属性标注的模糊性和文化差异问题，作者提出一种人机协作重标注框架，在资源受限条件下提升标注稳定性。该方法通过LLM迭代提取标注理由，结合专家反馈进行优化，并采用分歧聚焦采样策略进行针对性重标注。实验基于新构建的多语言数据集WhoSaidIt（覆盖九类属性）表明，不同语言间的标注决策存在显著差异，且LLM在属性分类中表现因语言而异，其性能受显式理由引导影响明显。
Rethinking Weak Supervision in Anomaly Detection: A Comprehensive Benchmark
Authors: Xu Yao, Siyuan Zhou, Wu Zhenbo, Chaochuan Hou, Shuang Liang, Shiping wang, Hailiang Huang, Songqiao Han, Minqi Jiang
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-05-25T17:32:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:32:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted at KDD 2026 Datasets and Benchmarks Track (Cycle 2)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Weakly supervised anomaly detection (WSAD) has developed in three primary directions: incomplete, inexact, and inaccurate supervision. However, these directions remain isolated, lacking a unified framework to assess whether they address unique challenges or share fundamental mechanics. This paper introduces WSADBench, the first benchmark that unifies evaluation across distinct weakly supervised scenarios, benchmarking diverse approaches from specialized WSAD methods to advanced tabular foundation models. WSADBench establishes standardized protocols to evaluate 36 algorithms across 4 modalities by systematically varying label quantity, granularity, and quality, revealing the performance boundaries of various methods. Based on over 700K experiments, WSADBench reveals four critical insights: (i) Strong intrinsic correlations exist between these weak supervision scenarios, challenging the isolation of current research directions. (ii) Specialized WSAD algorithms excel only in extreme label-scarcity regimes but are quickly dominated by tabular foundation models and general classification methods as supervision increases or in OOD scenarios. (iii) Unlabeled data shows inconsistent utility across settings, with marginal gains compared to label refinement. (iv) Models exhibit asymmetric sensitivity to different types of label noise. We release WSADBench as an open-source benchmark with code and datasets to facilitate future WSAD research: https://github.com/SUFE-AILAB/WSADBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考异常检测中的弱监督：全面基准
弱监督异常检测（WSAD）主要发展出三个方向：不完整、不精确和不准确的监督。然而，这些方向仍相互孤立，缺乏统一框架来评估它们是否解决独特挑战或共享基本机制。本文提出了WSADBench，这是首个统一评估不同弱监督场景的基准，涵盖从专用WSAD方法到先进表格基础模型的多种方法。WSADBench通过系统性地变化标签数量、粒度和质量，为4种模态的36种算法建立标准化评估协议，揭示各类方法的性能边界。基于70多万次实验，WSADBench揭示四个关键洞见：(i) 这些弱监督场景间存在强内在相关性，挑战当前研究方向的孤立性。(ii) 专用WSAD算法仅在极端标签稀缺场景下表现优异，但随着监督增强或在OOD场景中，很快被表格基础模型和通用分类方法超越。(iii) 无标签数据在不同设置下效用不一致，与标签优化相比收益有限。(iv) 模型对不同类型标签噪声表现出非对称敏感性。我们开源发布WSADBench，包含代码和数据集，以促进未来WSAD研究：https://github.com/SUFE-AILAB/WSADBench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented state of weakly supervised anomaly detection research, this paper introduces WSADBench, the first unified benchmark that evaluates 36 algorithms across four modalities under varying levels of label quantity, granularity, and quality. By systematically testing methods from specialized WSAD models to tabular foundation models, the study reveals that weak supervision scenarios are more interrelated than previously assumed, and that general classification models often outperform specialized ones as supervision improves or in out-of-distribution settings. The benchmark also finds that unlabeled data offers limited benefit compared to label refinement and that models respond asymmetrically to different types of label noise, based on over 700K experiments.
为解决弱监督异常检测研究方向割裂的问题，本文提出WSADBench，这是首个统一评估框架，涵盖4种模态、36种算法，在标签数量、粒度与质量变化下系统测试。实验表明，不同弱监督场景间存在强内在关联，且在标签充足或分布外场景中，通用分类模型常优于专用方法；未标注数据收益有限，而模型对不同噪声类型敏感度不对称，所有结论基于70余万次实验验证。
Conditional KRR: Injecting Unpenalized Features into Kernel Methods with Applications to Kernel Thresholding
Authors: Rustem Takhanov, Zhenisbek Assylbekov
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-25T17:31:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:31:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conditionally positive definite (CPD) kernels are defined with respect to a function class $\mathcal{F}$. It is well known that such a kernel $K$ is associated with its native space (defined analogously to an RKHS), which in turn gives rise to a learning method -- called conditional kernel ridge regression (conditional KRR) due to its analogy with KRR -- where the estimated regression function is penalized by the square of its native space norm. This method is of interest because it can be viewed as classical linear regression, with features specified by $\mathcal{F}$, followed by the application of standard KRR to the residual (unexplained) component of the target variable. Methods of this type have recently attracted increasing attention. We study the statistical properties of this method by reducing its behavior to that of KRR with another fixed kernel, called the residual kernel. Our main theoretical result shows that such a reduction is indeed possible, at the cost of an additional term in the expected test risk, bounded by $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{N})$, where $N$ is the sample size and the hidden constant depends on the class $\mathcal{F}$ and the input distribution. This reduction enables us to analyze conditional KRR in the case where $K$ is positive definite and $\mathcal{F}$ is given by the first $k$ principal eigenfunctions in the Mercer decomposition of $K$. We also consider the setting where $\mathcal{F}$ consists of $k$ random features from a random feature representation of $K$. It turns out that these two settings are closely related. Both our theoretical analysis and experiments confirm that conditional KRR outperforms standard KRR in these cases whenever the $\mathcal{F}$-component of the regression function is more pronounced than the residual part.
中文标题/摘要
标题：条件KRR：将未惩罚特征注入核方法及其在核阈值中的应用
条件正定（CPD）核相对于函数类$\mathcal{F}$定义。众所周知，此类核$K$关联其原生空间（类比RKHS定义），进而产生一种学习方法——称为条件核岭回归（条件KRR），因其与KRR类比，其中估计回归函数以原生空间范数平方进行惩罚。该方法因其可视为经典线性回归（特征由$\mathcal{F}$指定），随后对目标变量残差部分应用标准KRR而具有研究价值。此类方法近期备受关注。 我们通过将其行为归约为另一固定核（称为残差核）的KRR行为，研究该方法的统计性质。主要理论结果表明，这种归约确实可行，代价是测试风险期望值中增加一项，其上界为$\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{N})$，其中$N$为样本量，隐含常数依赖于函数类$\mathcal{F}$和输入分布。 该归约使我们能够分析当$K$为正定核且$\mathcal{F}$为$K$的Mercer分解前$k$个主特征函数时的条件KRR。我们还考虑$\mathcal{F}$由$K$的随机特征表示中$k$个随机特征组成的情形。结果表明，这两种情形密切相关。理论分析与实验均证实，当回归函数的$\mathcal{F}$分量比残差部分更显著时，条件KRR优于标准KRR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to improve kernel methods by incorporating unpenalized features, this paper introduces conditional kernel ridge regression (conditional KRR), which decomposes the target variable into a component explained by a function class $\mathcal{F}$ and a residual component, applying KRR only to the latter. The method is analyzed theoretically by reducing its behavior to that of KRR with a residual kernel, introducing an additional test risk term bounded by $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{N})$. Experiments confirm that conditional KRR outperforms standard KRR when the regression function has a strong $\mathcal{F}$-component, particularly when $\mathcal{F}$ consists of the first $k$ principal eigenfunctions or random features from a kernel’s representation.
为提升核方法性能，本文提出条件核岭回归（conditional KRR），将目标变量分解为由函数类 $\mathcal{F}$ 解释的部分与残差部分，仅对残差部分应用标准核岭回归。通过将该方法行为归约为标准核岭回归与残差核的分析，理论证明其测试风险额外增加 $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{N})$。实验表明，当回归函数中 $\mathcal{F}$ 部分显著强于残差部分时，conditional KRR 在 $\mathcal{F}$ 由前 $k$ 个主特征函数或随机特征构成的情形下优于标准核岭回归。
JEPA-DNA: Grounding Genomic Foundation Models through Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures
Authors: Ariel Larey, Elay Dahan, Amit Bleiweiss, Raizy Kellerman, Guy Leib, Omri Nayshool, Dan Ofer, Tal Zinger, Dan Dominissini, Gideon Rechavi, Nicole Bussola, Simon Lee, Shane O'Connell, Dung Hoang, Marissa Wirth, Alexander W. Charney, Nati Daniel, Yoli Shavit
First: 2026-02-19T08:20:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:29:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Genomic Foundation Models (GFMs) typically rely on Masked Language Modeling (MLM) or Next-Token Prediction (NTP) to learn the "Laws of Nature". While effective at capturing local syntax, these generative paradigms prioritize token-level reconstruction over high-level functional context. We introduce JEPA-DNA, a model-agnostic continual training framework that integrates a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) with traditional generative objectives. By supervising global sequence embeddings in a latent space, JEPA-DNA forces models to predict the functional representations of masked genomic segments, shifting the learning signal from token recovery to semantic alignment. We evaluate JEPA-DNA on 17 diverse genomic benchmark tasks, demonstrating consistent gains in linear probing and zero-shot performance regardless of the underlying GFM architecture or generative objective. Our framework establishes a new state-of-the-art for GFMs, surpassing the best existing models by bridging generative precision with latent semantic grounding. Through extensive ablation studies, we further characterize the synergistic interplay between generative and latent objectives. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/NVIDIA-Digital-Bio/JEPA-DNA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：JEPA-DNA：通过联合嵌入预测架构为基因组基础模型提供语义锚定
基因组基础模型（GFMs）通常依赖掩码语言建模（MLM）或下一词预测（NTP）来学习“自然法则”。虽然这些生成范式擅长捕捉局部语法，但它们优先关注词元级重建，而非高层次功能上下文。我们提出了JEPA-DNA，一种模型无关的持续训练框架，将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）与传统生成目标相结合。通过在潜在空间中监督全局序列嵌入，JEPA-DNA迫使模型预测被掩码基因组片段的功能表示，从而将学习信号从词元恢复转向语义对齐。我们在17个多样化的基因组基准任务上评估了JEPA-DNA，无论底层GFM架构或生成目标如何，均在线性探测和零样本性能上持续提升。我们的框架为GFMs建立了新的最先进水平，通过结合生成精度与潜在语义锚定，超越了现有最佳模型。通过广泛的消融研究，我们进一步刻画了生成与潜在目标之间的协同作用。我们的代码已公开于https://github.com/NVIDIA-Digital-Bio/JEPA-DNA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of generative models in capturing high-level genomic function despite strong token-level reconstruction, JEPA-DNA introduces a model-agnostic continual training framework that combines a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with traditional generative objectives. It trains models to predict masked genomic segments by aligning global sequence embeddings in a latent space, shifting focus from token recovery to semantic grounding. Evaluated across 17 genomic benchmarks, JEPA-DNA consistently outperforms existing GFMs in both linear probing and zero-shot settings, establishing a new state-of-the-art by synergistically integrating generative precision with latent semantic understanding.
为解决现有生成模型在捕捉高阶基因组语义方面的不足，JEPA-DNA提出一种模型无关的持续训练框架，将联合嵌入预测架构与传统生成目标结合。该框架通过在潜在空间中监督全局序列嵌入，训练模型预测被掩码基因组片段的功能表示，从而将学习信号从词元级重建转向语义对齐。在17项基因组基准任务上，JEPA-DNA无论底层架构或生成目标如何，均在线性探测和零样本任务中持续优于现有模型，建立新SOTA，通过协同生成精度与潜在语义锚定实现性能突破。
A Closed-Form Persistence-Landmark Pipeline for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification
Authors: Sushovan Majhi, Atish Mitra, Žiga Virk, Pramita Bagchi
First: 2026-05-04T17:15:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:29:12+00:00
Comments: TMLR submission, https://openreview.net/forum?id=4kZxNlE5Ve. v2: variance-aware Pinelis-Bernstein certificate (radius iii) fires on 8/12 benchmarks (v1: not operational); MUTAG: empirical and population NC rules agree on 940/940 predictions. Matching-free nu-coherence replaces non-interference. Le Cam lower bound (Thm 3.2) recast PD-native, matching regime m<~R/D explicit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce PLACE (Persistence-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), a closed-form pipeline for classifying point clouds and graphs through their persistent-homology signatures. Three quantitative guarantees -- a margin-based excess-risk rate, a closed-form descriptor-selection rule, and a per-prediction certificate -- are derived from training labels alone, with no learned weights or held-out calibration. The embedding sums Mitra-Virk single-point coordinate functions over a sparse landmark grid; the closed-form weight rule $w_k^2 \propto (d_{k+1}^2 - d_k^2)/R_k^2$ maximizes the distortion slope in Mitra-Virk's affine certificate under $ν$-coherence. (i) An $O(kR/(Δ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ margin bound, driven by class-mean separation $Δ$ and embedding radius $R$, matched in the sample-starved regime $m \lesssim R/Δ$ by a Le Cam minimax lower bound. (ii) The Mahalanobis margin under Ledoit-Wolf-shrunk covariance is the strongest closed-form ranker on a 64-descriptor chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ= +0.56$ across 11 benchmarks, positive on 10 of 11); the isotropic surrogate $Δ/\sqrt{\ell}$ admits a closed-form selection-consistency rate on the homogeneous protein/social pools. (iii) A training-time-decided certificate, with no per-prediction overhead, in three concrete radii (Pinelis, Gaussian plug-in, and variance-aware Pinelis-Bernstein). Empirically, PLACE is the strongest diagram-based method on Orbit5k and matches the strongest topology-based baseline within statistical noise on MUTAG and COX2; remaining gaps fall into two diagnosable regimes (descriptor blindness on NCI1/NCI109; pool-coverage limits elsewhere). The Pinelis-Bernstein radius fires on 8 of the 12 benchmarks; on MUTAG the empirical and population nearest-centroid rules agree on every one of 940 held-out test predictions, validating the certificate's mechanism.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for certified, closed-form classification of point clouds and graphs using topological signatures, the paper introduces PLACE, a pipeline that leverages persistent homology and Mitra-Virk coordinate functions over sparse landmarks. It derives three theoretical guarantees from training labels alone: a margin-based risk bound, a descriptor-selection rule based on distortion slope maximization under ν-coherence, and a per-prediction certificate without calibration. Experiments show PLACE achieves the best diagram-based performance on Orbit5k and matches top topology-based baselines on MUTAG and COX2, with the Pinelis-Bernstein certificate validating predictions on 940 held-out test cases in MUTAG. Gaps remain in descriptor coverage (NCI1/NCI109) and pool limitations elsewhere, but PLACE’s theoretical guarantees and empirical consistency demonstrate robustness without learned weights.
为实现点云与图的可认证闭式分类，该文提出PLACE方法，通过稀疏地标网格与Mitra-Virk坐标函数构建嵌入，权重由持久同调图推导以最大化仿射证书的畸变斜率。方法提供三项量化保证：基于类间分离与嵌入半径的边际界、基于Ledoit-Wolf收缩协方差的马哈拉诺比斯排序器（在64个化学图描述符上平均Spearman相关系数达+0.56），以及无需每预测开销的训练时证书。实验表明，PLACE在Orbit5k上超越其他图谱方法，在MUTAG和COX2上与最优拓扑基线持平，性能差距归因于描述符盲区或池覆盖不足；Pinelis-Bernstein半径在12个基准中的8个有效激活，且在MUTAG上经验与群体最近质心规则在940个测试预测上完全一致，验证了证书机制的有效性。
Paris 2.0: A Decentralized Diffusion Model for Video Generation
Authors: Ali Rouzbayani, Bidhan Roy, Marcos Villagra, Zhiying Jiang
First: 2026-05-25T17:27:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:27:22+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Paris 2.0, the first video generation model pre-trained through decentralized computation. Its training recipe builds upon Paris 1.0 (arXiv:2510.03434), the first ever open-weight Decentralized Diffusion Model (DDM), which showed that image generation can be trained without a monolithic GPU cluster. However, temporally coherent video generation had remained an open problem under decentralized training, and Paris 2.0 closes it. In low-resolution text-to-video training, against a monolithic model trained on the same data under a matched total compute budget, Paris 2.0 cuts Frechet Video Distance (FVD) from 561.04 to 279.01, a ~2.0x improvement, and lifts CLIP text-video similarity and aesthetic score.
中文标题/摘要
标题：巴黎2.0：一种用于视频生成的去中心化扩散模型
我们提出了巴黎2.0，这是首个通过去中心化计算预训练的视频生成模型。其训练方法基于巴黎1.0（arXiv:2510.03434），这是首个开源权重的去中心化扩散模型（DDM），证明了图像生成无需单一大型GPU集群即可训练。然而，在去中心化训练下，时序一致的视频生成一直是一个开放性问题，而巴黎2.0解决了这一问题。在低分辨率文本到视频训练中，与在相同数据上、总计算预算匹配的单体模型相比，巴黎2.0将Frechet视频距离（FVD）从561.04降至279.01，提升约2.0倍，并提升了CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable video generation under decentralized training without relying on centralized GPU clusters, Paris 2.0 extends the decentralized diffusion framework from images to videos. It builds on Paris 1.0’s architecture and training methodology, introducing temporal coherence mechanisms to handle video’s sequential nature. Evaluated against a monolithic model under identical compute budgets, Paris 2.0 achieves a 2.0x reduction in Frechet Video Distance (from 561.04 to 279.01), while also improving CLIP text-video similarity and aesthetic scores.
为实现无需集中式GPU集群的视频生成，Paris 2.0在图像扩散模型基础上扩展至视频领域，引入时序一致性机制以处理视频的序列特性。在与单体模型相同计算预算下，其Frechet Video Distance从561.04降至279.01（提升约2.0倍），同时CLIP图文相似度与美学评分也显著提高。
RCTs & Human Uplift Studies: Methodological Challenges and Practical Solutions for Frontier AI Evaluation
Authors: Patricia Paskov, Kevin Wei, Shen Zhou Hong, Dan Bateyko, Xavier Roberts-Gaal, Carson Ezell, Gailius Praninskas, Valerie Chen, Umang Bhatt, Ella Guest
First: 2026-03-11T17:25:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:25:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human uplift studies, or studies that measure the effects of AI access on human performance via randomized controlled trials (RCT) or similar methodologies, increasingly inform frontier AI governance and deployment decisions. While RCT methods are robust in other fields, their interaction with the distinctive properties of frontier AI systems remains underexamined, particularly when results are used to inform high-stakes decisions. We present findings from interviews with 16 expert practitioners with experience conducting human uplift studies in domains including biosecurity, cybersecurity, education, and labor. Across interviews, experts described a recurring tension between the standard causal inference assumptions upon which human uplift studies rely and the object of study itself. Rapidly evolving AI systems, shifting baselines, heterogeneous and changing user proficiency, and porous real-world settings strain assumptions underlying internal, external, and construct validity, complicating the interpretation and appropriate use of uplift evidence. We contribute (1) a synthesis of methodological challenges in human uplift studies, mapped to risks to study validity and classified by their degree of specificity to large language model (LLM) systems, and (2) a mapping from challenges to proposed solutions. By collating expert-identified challenges and solutions, we seek to clarify the interpretive limits and appropriate uses of human uplift evidence, to align evaluation practice with the decisions it informs, and to support more coordinated methodological foundations for AI governance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机对照试验与人类提升研究：前沿人工智能评估中的方法论挑战与实践解决方案
人类提升研究，即通过随机对照试验（RCT）或类似方法衡量人工智能接入对人类表现影响的研究，正日益影响前沿人工智能的治理与部署决策。尽管RCT方法在其他领域稳健可靠，但其与前沿AI系统的独特属性之间的互动仍缺乏充分探讨，尤其当研究结果用于指导高风险决策时。我们通过对16位在生物安全、网络安全、教育和劳动等领域开展人类提升研究的专家进行访谈，总结出一个反复出现的张力：人类提升研究依赖的标准因果推断假设与研究对象本身存在冲突。快速演进的AI系统、不断变化的基准、异质且变化的用户熟练度，以及现实世界中模糊的环境，削弱了内部效度、外部效度和构念效度的基础假设，使提升证据的解读与恰当使用变得复杂。我们贡献了两点：（1）对人类提升研究方法论挑战的综合梳理，按其对研究效度构成的风险分类，并区分其对大语言模型（LLM）系统的特异性程度；（2）将挑战映射至所提出的解决方案。通过汇集专家识别的挑战与解决方案，我们旨在厘清人类提升证据的解释边界与适用场景，使评估实践与所支持的决策相一致，并为人工智能治理提供更协调的方法论基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of human uplift studies to guide high-stakes AI governance, this paper examines methodological challenges arising from applying randomized controlled trials to frontier AI systems. Through interviews with 16 experts across domains like biosecurity and education, the study identifies tensions between standard causal inference assumptions and the dynamic nature of AI systems, including rapidly evolving models, shifting baselines, and heterogeneous user proficiency. These factors undermine internal, external, and construct validity, complicating the interpretation of uplift results. The paper synthesizes these challenges, categorizing them by their specificity to large language models, and maps them to practical solutions, aiming to clarify the appropriate use of uplift evidence and support more robust methodological foundations for AI evaluation.
鉴于人类提升研究日益用于指导前沿AI治理决策，本研究探讨了前沿AI系统独特属性引发的方法论挑战。通过对16位来自生物安全、教育等领域的专家进行访谈，研究发现标准因果推断假设与现实AI部署环境存在矛盾，如模型快速迭代、基线漂移和用户能力异质性，这些因素削弱了研究的内部、外部和构念效度，使提升证据的解读复杂化。论文系统梳理了这些挑战，按其对大语言模型的特异性进行分类，并提出对应解决方案，旨在明确提升证据的适用边界，推动评估实践与治理决策的对齐，并为AI评估方法论奠定更协调的基础。
Look Both Ways Before You Cross: Lifting Cross Fields From 2D Visual Priors
Authors: Dale Decatur, Jacob Serfaty, Oded Stein, Amir Vaxman, Rana Hanocka
First: 2026-05-25T17:23:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:23:23+00:00
Comments: Project page at: https://crosslift.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present CrossLift, a technique for computing cross fields on meshes guided by visual features in images. We leverage powerful text-to-image priors that are capable of synthesizing images of feature-aligned quad meshes in 2D. We extract this signal as explicit per-pixel directions in the 2D images, which we then back-project to the mesh surface. We aggregate these candidate surface directions by performing two smooth interpolations on the mesh surface (first within each view and second across multiple views). We propose custom confidence-based weights for the candidate directions in each interpolation that allow us to resolve conflicts between candidates on the same face and smoothly interpolate our field to occluded faces. Our method is modular and can be used with many different 2D visual priors. We show additional applications to texture-aligned quad meshing as well as interactive cross-field design using coarse, user-drawn lines as signal. We demonstrate the effectiveness of CrossLift on a diverse set of both organic and mechanical shapes and produce quad meshes that exhibit superior semantic alignment as compared to existing methods. Project page at: https://crosslift.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：过马路前请左右张望：从2D视觉先验中提升交叉场
我们提出了CrossLift，一种基于图像视觉特征引导的网格交叉场计算技术。我们利用强大的文本到图像先验，能够合成特征对齐的四边形网格2D图像。我们将该信号提取为2D图像中的每像素显式方向，再将其反投影到网格表面。我们在网格表面执行两次平滑插值（首先在每个视图内，其次在多个视图间）来聚合这些候选表面方向。我们为每次插值中的候选方向提出自定义置信度加权机制，以解决同一面上候选方向的冲突，并平滑插值至被遮挡面。该方法模块化，可与多种2D视觉先验结合使用。我们还展示了其在纹理对齐四边形网格化及使用用户手绘粗线进行交互式交叉场设计中的应用。我们在多样化的有机与机械形状上验证了CrossLift的有效性，生成的四边形网格相比现有方法展现出更优的语义对齐效果。项目页面：https://crosslift.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate quad meshes with semantic alignment to visual features, the authors introduce CrossLift, a method that leverages 2D text-to-image priors to guide cross field computation on 3D meshes. It extracts per-pixel directional signals from synthesized images and back-projects them onto the mesh surface, then aggregates these directions through two smooth interpolations—first within each view and then across multiple views—using confidence-based weights to resolve conflicts and handle occlusions. Experimental results show CrossLift produces quad meshes with superior semantic alignment compared to existing methods across diverse organic and mechanical shapes, and it supports interactive design using user-drawn lines.
为实现与视觉特征语义对齐的四边形网格生成，作者提出CrossLift方法，利用2D文本到图像先验引导3D网格上的交叉场计算。该方法从合成图像中提取每像素方向信号并投影到网格表面，通过两次平滑插值（先按视图内、再跨视图）聚合方向，采用置信度加权融合解决冲突并处理遮挡。实验表明，CrossLift在有机与机械形状上生成的四边形网格相比现有方法具有更优的语义对齐效果，且支持用户手绘线条驱动的交互式设计。
Neuronal Stochastic Attention Circuit (NSAC) for Probabilistic Representation Learning
Authors: Waleed Razzaq, Yun-Bo Zhao
First: 2026-05-25T17:19:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:19:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable quantification of uncertainty estimates in continuous-time (CT) representation learning remains nascent, particularly within CT attention architectures. We introduce the Neuronal Stochastic Attention Circuit (NSAC), a novel biologically-inspired CT attention architecture that reformulates attention logit computation as the solution of an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck stochastic differential equation modulated by input-dependent, nonlinear interlinked gates derived from repurposed C.elegans Neuronal Circuit Policies (NCPs) wiring mechanism. It induces Gaussian distribution over logits that propagates principled stochasticity through logistic-normal distribution over attention weights to yield probabilistic output. A two-term objective function combining Gaussian negative log-likelihood with an epistemic-separation regularizer enforces higher predictive variance and enables joint quantification of aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty. Empirically, we implement NSAC in a diverse set of learning tasks including: (i) irregular CT function approximation; (ii) multivariate regression; (iii) long-range forecasting; (iv) Industry 4.0; and (v) the lane-keeping of autonomous vehicles. We observe that the NSAC remains competitive against several baselines in terms of accuracy and produces reasonably well-calibrated uncertainty estimates while being interpretable at the neuronal cell level.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于概率表示学习的神经元随机注意电路（NSAC）
在连续时间（CT）表示学习中，对不确定性估计的可靠量化仍处于初级阶段，尤其是在CT注意架构中。我们提出了神经元随机注意电路（NSAC），这是一种新颖的生物启发式CT注意架构，将注意力logit计算重构为受输入依赖的非线性互联门控机制调制的奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克随机微分方程的解，该门控机制源自改造的秀丽隐杆线虫神经元电路策略（NCPs）布线机制。它在logit上诱导高斯分布，并通过逻辑正态分布传播规范的随机性，从而生成概率输出。一个结合高斯负对数似然与认知分离正则化项的双项目标函数，强制提高预测方差，并实现对偶然性和认知不确定性联合量化。实证上，我们在多种学习任务中实现了NSAC，包括：（i）不规则CT函数逼近；（ii）多元回归；（iii）长程预测；（iv）工业4.0；以及（v）自动驾驶车辆车道保持。我们观察到，NSAC在准确率上与多个基线方法保持竞争力，同时生成合理校准的不确定性估计，并在神经元细胞层面具备可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of reliable uncertainty quantification in continuous-time attention models, the paper proposes NSAC, a biologically inspired architecture that models attention logit computation via an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck stochastic differential equation, modulated by nonlinear gates derived from C.elegans neuronal wiring. This design induces Gaussian logit distributions and propagates stochasticity through logistic-normal attention weights to generate probabilistic outputs. The model is trained with a two-term objective combining Gaussian negative log-likelihood and an epistemic-separation regularizer to encourage higher predictive variance and joint uncertainty estimation. Experiments across five tasks—including function approximation, regression, forecasting, Industry 4.0 applications, and autonomous vehicle lane-keeping—show NSAC achieves competitive accuracy while producing well-calibrated uncertainty estimates and offering interpretability at the neuronal level.
为解决连续时间注意力模型中不确定性量化不足的问题，本文提出NSAC架构，其将注意力logit建模为受输入依赖非线性门控调节的奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克随机微分方程解，该门控机制源自C.elegans神经电路策略。该设计使logit呈高斯分布，并通过逻辑正态分布的注意力权重传播随机性，生成概率输出。模型采用结合高斯负对数似然与先验分离正则化的双目标函数，以增强预测方差并联合量化偶然与认知不确定性。在包括函数逼近、多元回归、长程预测、工业4.0及自动驾驶车道保持在内的五个任务中，NSAC在精度上与多个基线方法相当，同时输出合理校准的不确定性估计，并具备神经元层面的可解释性。
Accelerating Bayesian inverse design in computational fluid dynamics using neural operators
Authors: Bipin Tiwari, Omer San
Venue: Mach. Learn. Comput. Sci. Eng 2, 14 (2026)
First: 2026-05-25T17:18:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:18:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian inverse design provides a principled framework for inferring aerodynamic geometries from sparse flow observations while quantifying uncertainty. However, its practical use in computational fluid dynamics (CFD) is severely limited by the cost of repeated high-fidelity simulations required for gradient-based Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling. While surrogate models are commonly proposed to reduce this cost, their effect on posterior geometry and uncertainty, especially for shock-dominated flows, remains poorly understood. In this work, we demonstrate that neural operator surrogates can be embedded directly within the MCMC inference loop while preserving posterior structure. Using a fully Bayesian inverse formulation of quasi-one-dimensional nozzle flow, we demonstrate that geometry parameterization plays a decisive role in identifiability and posterior conditioning, with cubic B-splines yielding stable and physically meaningful uncertainty estimates. Building on this formulation, a Deep Operator Network trained on CFD-generated data is substituted for the CFD solver within a No-U-Turn Sampler, while keeping the likelihood model, priors, and sampling configuration unchanged. Across sparse to fully observed regimes, surrogate-based inference reproduces the posterior geometry and uncertainty trends of the CFD reference. As a result of surrogate integration, total inference time is reduced to under one second, corresponding to a speedup exceeding three orders of magnitude. In addition, a direct inverse neural operator is examined as a deterministic alternative for inverse design, enabling single-shot geometry reconstruction without posterior sampling. These results demonstrate that neural operator-accelerated Bayesian inference enables practical, uncertainty-aware inverse design workflows for aerodynamic applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用神经算子加速计算流体力学中的贝叶斯逆设计
贝叶斯逆设计提供了一种从稀疏流场观测中推断气动几何并量化不确定性的原理性框架。然而，其在计算流体力学（CFD）中的实际应用严重受限于梯度MCMC采样所需的重复高保真模拟成本。虽然常提出代理模型以降低该成本，但其对后验几何和不确定性的具体影响，特别是在激波主导流动中，仍不甚明了。本文证明，神经算子代理可直接嵌入MCMC推断循环中，同时保持后验结构。基于准一维喷管流动的全贝叶斯逆设计框架，我们表明几何参数化对可识别性和后验条件具有决定性作用，其中三次B样条能产生稳定且物理意义明确的不确定性估计。在此基础上，将训练于CFD生成数据的深度算子网络替代CFD求解器，嵌入No-U-Turn采样器中，同时保持似然模型、先验和采样配置不变。在从稀疏到完全观测的不同场景下，基于代理的推断重现了CFD基准的后验几何与不确定性趋势。由于代理集成，总推断时间缩短至不到一秒，速度提升超过三个数量级。此外，还考察了直接逆向神经算子作为确定性替代方案，实现无需后验采样的单次几何重建。这些结果表明，神经算子加速的贝叶斯推断可实现气动应用中实用且具备不确定性感知的逆设计工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of Bayesian inverse design in CFD, this work proposes embedding neural operator surrogates directly into MCMC sampling loops to accelerate inference while preserving posterior structure. Using a quasi-one-dimensional nozzle flow model, the study shows that cubic B-splines enable stable geometry parameterization and meaningful uncertainty quantification. A Deep Operator Network trained on CFD data replaces the original solver in a No-U-Turn Sampler, yielding posterior results consistent with high-fidelity simulations across sparse to full observation regimes, while reducing total inference time to under one second—a three-order-of-magnitude speedup. Additionally, a direct inverse neural operator enables single-shot geometry reconstruction without sampling, demonstrating the feasibility of practical, uncertainty-aware inverse design.
为解决CFD中贝叶斯逆设计因重复高保真模拟导致的计算成本过高问题，本文提出将神经算子代理模型直接嵌入MCMC采样循环，以加速推理同时保持后验结构。在准一维喷管流模型中，研究发现三次B样条参数化对可识别性和后验条件至关重要，能稳定输出物理意义明确的不确定性。通过训练的Deep Operator Network替代CFD求解器，嵌入No-U-Turn采样器，保持似然、先验和采样配置不变，推理时间缩短至不足一秒，提速超过三个数量级。此外，直接逆向神经算子可实现单次确定性几何重建，验证了神经算子加速贝叶斯推理在气动应用中实现实用化、不确定性感知逆设计的可行性。
Controllable Radar Simulation with Waveform Parameter Embedding
Authors: Weiqing Xiao, Hao Huang, Chonghao Zhong, Yujie Lin, Nan Wang, Xiaoxue Chen, Zhaoxi Chen, Saining Zhang, Shuocheng Yang, Pierre Merriaux, Lei Lei, Hao Zhao
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-06-03T17:58:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:12:50+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Findings: Code: https://github.com/zhuxing0/SA-Radar Project page: https://zhuxing0.github.io/projects/SA-Radar
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Autonomous driving simulators still lack high-fidelity radar, even though radar is critical for robust perception in adverse weather. A key obstacle is that raw radar point clouds are extremely sparse and stochastic, making it difficult to model; we argue that simulating the full range-azimuth-Doppler cube is a more principled target. Existing radar cube simulators either rely purely on neural generators, which are opaque and offer little control over sensor attributes, or on detailed electromagnetic pipelines, which are slow, require proprietary hardware specifications, and still struggle to capture real-world complexity. We introduce Ctrl-RS, a controllable radar cube simulation framework that combines the strengths of both worlds. First, we build an environment reflection tensor from diverse sensor sources (including LiDAR, monocular cameras, and existing radar). Second, we abstract radar physics into a compact set of waveform parameters that characterize the 3D point spread function, yielding an intuitive embedding of radar attributes such as range resolution, Doppler broadening, and azimuth beam shape. Third, we train a WARP-Net on a large mixed dataset that fuses real, analytically synthesized, and simulator-generated radar cubes to cover a wide distribution of radar attributes. Ctrl-RS supports viewpoint changes, actor removal, and attribute editing. Experiments on RADDet, Carrada, and nuScenes show that our simulated data can match or surpass real radar in 2D detection and semantic segmentation, and consistently boosts performance in 3D detection when combined with real data. The Project is available at https://github.com/zhuxing0/Ctrl-RS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于波形参数嵌入的可控雷达仿真
尽管雷达在恶劣天气下的鲁棒感知中至关重要，但自动驾驶仿真器仍缺乏高保真度的雷达。主要障碍在于原始雷达点云极度稀疏且随机，难以建模；我们认为模拟完整的距离-方位-多普勒立方体是更合理的靶标。现有雷达立方体仿真器要么完全依赖神经生成器，其过程不透明且难以控制传感器属性，要么依赖详细的电磁仿真流程，速度慢、需专有硬件规格，仍难以捕捉真实世界复杂性。我们提出Ctrl-RS，一种结合两者优势的可控雷达立方体仿真框架。首先，我们从多种传感器源（包括激光雷达、单目相机和现有雷达）构建环境反射张量。其次，我们将雷达物理抽象为一组紧凑的波形参数，表征三维点扩散函数，从而直观嵌入雷达属性如距离分辨率、多普勒展宽和方位波束形状。第三，我们在融合真实、分析合成及仿真生成雷达立方体的大规模混合数据集上训练WARP-Net，以覆盖广泛的雷达属性分布。Ctrl-RS支持视角变换、目标移除和属性编辑。在RADDet、Carrada和nuScenes上的实验表明，我们的仿真数据在2D检测和语义分割中可匹配或超越真实雷达，并在与真实数据结合时持续提升3D检测性能。项目代码见https://github.com/zhuxing0/Ctrl-RS。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity radar simulation in autonomous driving, this work addresses the challenge of modeling sparse and stochastic radar point clouds by targeting the full range-azimuth-Doppler cube. It introduces Ctrl-RS, a controllable simulation framework that combines neural generation with physics-based modeling: it constructs an environment reflection tensor from multi-modal sensors, abstracts radar physics into controllable waveform parameters, and trains a WARP-Net on a mixed dataset of real, synthetic, and simulated radar cubes. Experiments on RADDet, Carrada, and nuScenes demonstrate that Ctrl-RS-generated data matches or exceeds real radar in 2D detection and segmentation, and significantly improves 3D detection when blended with real data.
为解决自动驾驶仿真中雷达模拟精度不足的问题，本文提出Ctrl-RS框架，结合神经生成与物理建模优势。该框架从多源传感器构建环境反射张量，并将雷达物理特性抽象为波形参数嵌入，以直观表征距离分辨率、多普勒展宽等关键属性。基于混合真实、合成与仿真雷达数据训练的WARP-Net可生成可控雷达立方体。在RADDet、Carrada和nuScenes数据集上的实验表明，Ctrl-RS生成的数据在2D检测与分割任务中可媲美或超越真实雷达数据，并在与真实数据结合时持续提升3D检测性能。
Retrying vs Resampling in AI Control
Authors: James Lucassen, Adam Kaufman
First: 2026-05-25T17:10:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:10:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI coding scaffolds like Claude Code and Codex use \textit{retrying}: blocking actions flagged as risky and continuing the trajectory. We study retrying from an AI control perspective, which treats the model as potentially adversarial. We find that while retrying reduces honest suspicion scores, the untrusted model can exploit monitor rationale to construct sneakier attacks, negating safety gains. We also study \textit{resampling}: drawing multiple samples from the same context, which does not leak exploitable information. We disentangle design choices that previous work on resampling had bundled together. In BashArena, with Claude Opus 4.6 as the untrusted model and MiMo-V2-Flash as the trusted monitor, drawing five samples per step and auditing on the maximum suspicion score raises safety from 61\% to 71\% at a 0.3\% audit budget, at no cost to usefulness. Selectively resampling only the steps that look suspicious on the first draw recovers 6.2 percentage points of the gain while drawing only 10\% as many extra samples. Two of our findings in this setting contradict earlier work on resampling. The first is that auditing based on the maximum across resampled suspicion scores outperforms using the minimum, which is the opposite of what Ctrl-Z found. The second is that executing the least suspicious sample, which is the central mechanism in earlier defer-to-resample protocols, gives only a small empirical safety gain in our setting (+3.9 pp, with the confidence interval overlapping zero).
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI控制中的重试与重采样比较
AI编码辅助工具如Claude Code和Codex采用\textit{重试}：对标记为高风险的动作进行阻断并继续执行轨迹。我们从AI控制视角研究重试，将模型视为潜在对抗方。研究发现，虽然重试降低了诚实怀疑分数，但不可信模型可利用监控理由构建更隐蔽的攻击，抵消安全收益。我们还研究\textit{重采样}：从同一上下文抽取多个样本，不会泄露可被利用的信息。我们拆分了此前重采样研究中捆绑在一起的设计选择。在BashArena中，以Claude Opus 4.6为不可信模型、MiMo-V2-Flash为可信监控器，每步抽取五个样本并基于最大怀疑分数审计，安全率从61%提升至71%，审计预算为0.3%，且无用处损失。仅对首次抽样中看似可疑的步骤进行重采样，可恢复6.2个百分点的安全增益，同时仅额外抽取10%的样本。在该设置下，我们的两项发现与此前重采样研究结论相悖：一是基于重采样怀疑分数最大值的审计优于最小值（与Ctrl-Z研究相反）；二是执行最不可疑样本（此前“重采样退让”协议的核心机制）在本设置下仅带来微小实证安全增益（+3.9个百分点，置信区间与零重叠）
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve safety in AI control systems, this study compares retrying and resampling strategies for managing untrusted models. Retry involves blocking risky actions and continuing the trajectory, while resampling draws multiple samples from the same context to avoid leaking exploitable information. The research finds that retrying reduces honest suspicion scores but allows untrusted models to exploit monitor rationales for sneakier attacks. In contrast, resampling with five samples per step and auditing based on the maximum suspicion score boosts safety from 61% to 71% at a 0.3% audit budget, with no loss in usefulness. Selective resampling of only suspicious steps recovers 6.2 percentage points of the gain while using only 10% of the extra samples. Two key findings contradict prior work: maximum suspicion auditing outperforms minimum, and executing the least suspicious sample yields only a small safety gain (+3.9 pp, with confidence interval overlapping zero).
为提升AI控制系统安全性，本研究对比了重试与重采样两种策略。重试虽降低诚实怀疑分数，但允许不可信模型利用监控推理发起更隐蔽攻击。重采样通过从同一上下文抽样多个输出，避免信息泄露，安全效果更优：每步抽样五次并以最大怀疑分数审计，安全率从61%提升至71%，且不影响实用性。仅对首次抽样可疑步骤重采样，可恢复6.2个百分点安全增益，且采样量仅为原方案的10%。两项发现与先前研究相悖：最大怀疑分数审计优于最小值，且执行最不怀疑样本仅带来微弱安全增益（+3.9个百分点，置信区间包含零）。
Rapid mixing in positively weighted restricted Boltzmann machines
Authors: Weiming Feng, Heng Guo, Minji Yang
First: 2026-04-01T14:38:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:09:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We show polylogarithmic mixing time bounds for the alternating-scan sampler for positively weighted restricted Boltzmann machines. This is done via analysing the same chain and the Glauber dynamics for ferromagnetic two-spin systems, where we obtain new mixing time bounds up to the critical thresholds.

BlitzRank: Principled Zero-shot Ranking Agents with Tournament Graphs
Authors: Sheshansh Agrawal, Thien Hang Nguyen, Douwe Kiela
Venue: ICML 2026 spotlight
First: 2026-02-05T08:41:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:09:21+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 spotlight
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Selecting the top $m$ from $n$ items via expensive $k$-wise comparisons is central to settings ranging from LLM-based document reranking to crowdsourced evaluation and tournament design. Existing methods either rely on heuristics that discard comparison information, or exploit it at prohibitive cost. We introduce a tournament graph framework that provides a principled foundation for $k$-wise ranking. Our key observation is that each $k$-item comparison reveals an induced tournament of $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise preferences; aggregating these into a global preference graph and computing its transitive closure yields many additional orderings without further oracle calls. We formalize when the current top-$m$ output is certifiably determined and design a greedy query schedule that maximizes information gain towards identifying the top-$m$ items. The framework also gracefully handles non-transitive preferences -- cycles induced by real-world oracles -- by collapsing them into equivalence classes that yield principled tiered rankings. Applied to LLM reranking across 14 benchmarks and 5 models, BlitzRank achieves Pareto dominance over existing approaches: matching or exceeding accuracy while requiring 25--40% fewer tokens than comparable methods; against pairwise reranking, it achieves near-identical quality with 7$\times$ fewer tokens. Code available at https://github.com/ContextualAI/BlitzRank.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BlitzRank：基于锦标赛图的原理性零样本排序代理
从n个物品中通过昂贵的k元比较选出前m个物品，是LLM文档重排序、众包评估和锦标赛设计等场景的核心问题。现有方法要么依赖丢弃比较信息的启发式方法，要么以高昂代价利用比较信息。我们引入锦标赛图框架，为k元排序提供原理性基础。关键观察是，每次k元比较可揭示一个包含C(k,2)对偏好关系的诱导锦标赛；将这些关系聚合为全局偏好图并计算其传递闭包，可在无需进一步查询的情况下获得大量额外排序。我们形式化了当前前m个输出可被确证确定的条件，并设计了一种贪心查询调度策略，以最大化信息增益，从而识别前m个物品。该框架还能优雅处理非传递偏好——由现实世界查询诱导的环路——通过将其归并为等价类，生成具有原理性分层排序的排名。在14个基准和5个模型上的LLM重排序实验表明，BlitzRank在帕累托优势上超越现有方法：在保持或超越准确率的同时，所需token数量比同类方法减少25%-40%；与成对重排序相比，其质量几乎相同，但token数量减少7倍。代码见https://github.com/ContextualAI/BlitzRank。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently rank top-m items from n candidates using costly k-wise comparisons, BlitzRank introduces a tournament graph framework that systematically captures pairwise preferences from each k-item comparison and aggregates them into a global preference graph whose transitive closure reveals additional orderings without further queries. The method employs a greedy query scheduling strategy to maximize information gain toward identifying the top-m items and handles non-transitive preferences by collapsing cycles into equivalence classes for tiered rankings. Experiments across 14 LLM reranking benchmarks and 5 models show that BlitzRank matches or exceeds accuracy of existing methods while using 25–40% fewer tokens, and achieves near-identical quality to pairwise reranking with 7× fewer tokens.
为高效解决在昂贵的k次比较下从n个物品中选出前m个物品的问题，BlitzRank提出基于锦标赛图的框架，系统性地捕获并聚合每次k物品比较所揭示的成对偏好。通过计算聚合偏好图的传递闭包，无需额外查询即可推断出更多排序关系，并采用贪心策略最大化识别前m项的信息增益。在14个LLM重排序基准测试中，该方法实现帕累托优势：在保持或超越精度的同时，比现有方法节省25–40%的token；与成对重排序相比，其质量相近但节省7倍token。
When Gradients Collide: Failure Modes of Multi-Objective Prompt Optimization for LLM Judges
Authors: Parth Darshan, Abhishek Divekar
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-25T17:08:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:08:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL 2026 CustomNLP4U Workshop. Code, prompts and data available at https://github.com/ARDivekar/PromptMOO
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Customizing an LLM judge to a specific task or domain often involves optimizing its prompt across multiple evaluation criteria simultaneously. Textual gradient methods automate this for a single judge criterion, however they produce natural-language critiques, not numerical vectors. Thus, the conflict-resolution toolkit of multi-task learning (PCGrad, MGDA) doesn't apply to the multi-objective textual gradient setting. We test five decomposition modes of textual gradient optimizers by varying how much cross-task information the loss, gradient and optimizer LLMs share. In 6 of 10 configurations, we observe that optimization never improves over the initial prompt. Gradient specificity drops by 59% (from 9.0 to 3.7) when the gradient LLM processes multiple criteria jointly. Separately, we observe that naively combining per-task instructions into a single prompt degrades Spearman's rho by -5.3%. These results identify two separable failure modes: optimization-time gradient dilution and inference-time instruction interference, which together constrain the design space for multi-objective judge customization using textual feedback.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当梯度碰撞时：多目标提示优化用于大语言模型裁判的失败模式
将大语言模型裁判定制化到特定任务或领域时，通常需同时优化其提示以满足多个评估标准。文本梯度方法可自动化处理单一裁判标准，但生成的是自然语言批评而非数值向量，因此多任务学习中的冲突解决工具包（如PCGrad、MGDA）无法直接应用于多目标文本梯度场景。我们通过调整损失、梯度和优化器LLM之间共享的跨任务信息量，测试了五种文本梯度优化器的分解模式。在10种配置中的6种情况下，优化从未优于初始提示。当梯度LLM同时处理多个标准时，梯度特异性下降59%（从9.0降至3.7）。此外，将各任务指令简单合并为单个提示会使斯皮尔曼相关系数下降-5.3%。这些结果揭示了两种可分离的失败模式：优化阶段梯度稀释与推理阶段指令干扰，二者共同限制了使用文本反馈进行多目标裁判定制的设计空间。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the challenges in optimizing LLM judges using multi-objective textual gradients, where traditional gradient-based optimization methods from multi-task learning fail due to the non-vector nature of textual critiques. The authors test five decomposition strategies by varying how shared information flows between loss, gradient, and optimizer LLMs across tasks, finding that in six out of ten configurations, optimization yields no improvement over the initial prompt. They observe a 59% drop in gradient specificity when gradients are computed jointly across tasks, and a -5.3% decline in Spearman’s rho when per-task instructions are naively merged into a single prompt. These findings reveal two distinct failure modes: gradient dilution during optimization and instruction interference during inference, which together limit viable approaches for multi-objective prompt customization.
该研究探讨了在多目标评估准则下优化大语言模型裁判提示时所面临的挑战。由于文本梯度方法无法提供数值梯度，现有多任务学习中的梯度冲突解决工具（如PCGrad、MGDA）无法直接应用。研究通过调整损失、梯度和优化器LLM在多任务间共享信息的方式，测试了五种分解策略。在十种配置中，六种情况下优化未能提升初始提示效果。当梯度LLM同时处理多个目标时，梯度特异性下降59%（从9.0降至3.7），而将各任务指令合并为单一提示则使Spearman相关系数下降5.3%。研究揭示了两种独立的失败模式：优化阶段的梯度稀释和推理阶段的指令干扰，二者共同限制了多目标裁判提示定制的设计空间。
Confidence and Calibration of Activation Oracles for Reliable Interpretation of Language Model Internals
Authors: Federico Torrielli, Peter Schneider-Kamp, Lukas Galke Poech
First: 2026-05-25T17:08:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:08:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Activation oracles aim to make the activations of other models legible to humans and yield promising results compared to white-box interpretability techniques. However, uncertainty quantification (UQ) for the natural-language outputs of such activation oracles is so far understudied. Here, we investigate 6 different methods for estimating the confidence of activation oracles and evaluate how well-calibrated their confidence scores are. Our experiments on 6,000 samples per oracle (varying verbalizer and context prompts) reveal that bootstrap mode frequency is the best-calibrated method among those tested (ECE 5.7% vs. 25.5% for the answer-word log-probability on Qwen3-8B; 10.3% vs. 13.1% on Qwen3.6-27B), and that the log-prob baseline can serve as a fast triage signal at a fraction of the cost. Code and the patched trainer are available at https://github.com/federicotorrielli/probabilistic_activation_oracles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：激活预言机的置信度与校准：为语言模型内部可靠解释提供支持
激活预言机旨在使其他模型的激活状态对人类可理解，并在与白盒可解释性技术相比时表现出良好效果。然而，目前对这类激活预言机自然语言输出的不确定性量化（UQ）研究尚不充分。本文研究了6种评估激活预言机置信度的方法，并评估其置信分数的校准程度。在每个预言机上对6,000个样本（不同语义化器和上下文提示）的实验表明，自助法频率是所测试方法中校准效果最好的（在Qwen3-8B上，ECE为5.7% vs. 25.5%；在Qwen3.6-27B上，ECE为10.3% vs. 13.1%），而对数概率基线可作为低成本的快速筛选信号。 代码及修补后的训练器可在 https://github.com/federicotorrielli/probabilistic_activation_oracles 获取。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of uncertainty quantification in activation oracles, which are tools designed to make language model activations interpretable to humans. The authors evaluate six methods for estimating confidence in these oracles' outputs across 6,000 samples per oracle, varying verbalizers and prompts. Their results show that bootstrap mode frequency yields the most reliable calibration (ECE 5.7% on Qwen3-8B and 10.3% on Qwen3.6-27B), significantly outperforming alternatives like answer-word log-probability (ECE 25.5% and 13.1%). They also find that the log-prob baseline offers a computationally efficient triage option for quick confidence assessment.
该研究针对激活预言机缺乏不确定性量化的问题，评估了六种置信度估计方法，发现基于抽样众数频率的方法在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3.6-27B模型上校准效果最佳（ECE分别为5.7%和10.3%），远优于如答案词对数概率等方法（ECE分别为25.5%和13.1%）。同时，对数概率基线也被证明是一种计算成本更低的快速置信度筛查工具。
Smart Timing for Mining: A Deep Learning Framework for Bitcoin Hardware ROI Prediction
Authors: Sithumi Wickramasinghe, Bikramjit Das, Dorien Herremans
First: 2025-12-05T03:47:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:08:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bitcoin mining hardware acquisition requires strategic timing due to volatile markets, rapid technological obsolescence, and protocol-driven revenue cycles. Despite mining's evolution into a capital-intensive industry, there is little guidance on when to purchase new Application-Specific Integrated Circuit (ASIC) hardware, and no prior computational frameworks address this decision problem. We address this gap by formulating hardware acquisition as a time series classification task, predicting whether purchasing ASIC machines yields profitable (Return on Investment (ROI) >= 1), marginal (0 < ROI < 1), or unprofitable (ROI <= 0) returns within one year. We propose MineROI-Net, an open-source Transformer-based architecture designed to capture multi-scale temporal patterns in mining profitability. Evaluated on data from 20 ASIC miners released between 2015 and 2024 across diverse market regimes, MineROI-Net outperforms recurrent, convolutional, and attention-based baselines, achieving 83.2% accuracy and 83.5% macro F1-score. The model demonstrates strong economic relevance, achieving 97.8% precision in detecting unprofitable periods and 81.5% precision in detecting profitable ones, while avoiding misclassifying profitable scenarios as unprofitable and vice versa. These results indicate that MineROI-Net offers a practical, data-driven tool for timing mining hardware acquisitions, potentially reducing financial risk in capital-intensive mining operations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：智能时机采矿：一种用于比特币硬件投资回报率预测的深度学习框架
由于市场波动、技术快速过时和协议驱动的收益周期，比特币挖矿硬件采购需要战略性时机选择。尽管挖矿已演变为资本密集型行业，但目前缺乏关于何时购买新专用集成电路（ASIC）硬件的指导，也无先前计算框架解决此决策问题。我们通过将硬件采购建模为时间序列分类任务，预测在一年内购买ASIC设备是否能获得盈利（投资回报率ROI≥1）、边际收益（0<ROI<1）或亏损（ROI≤0）。我们提出MineROI-Net，一种开源的基于Transformer的架构，旨在捕捉挖矿盈利能力的多尺度时间模式。在涵盖2015至2024年间发布、跨越多种市场环境的20款ASIC矿机数据上评估，MineROI-Net优于循环、卷积和注意力机制基线模型，准确率达83.2%，宏F1分数达83.5%。该模型具有显著经济相关性，在识别亏损期时精度达97.8%，识别盈利期时精度达81.5%，且避免将盈利情况误判为亏损或反之。结果表明，MineROI-Net提供了一种实用、数据驱动的工具，用于优化挖矿硬件采购时机，有望降低资本密集型挖矿运营的财务风险。

Driven by the need to optimize Bitcoin mining hardware purchases amid market volatility and rapid technological change, this study introduces MineROI-Net, a Transformer-based deep learning model that classifies ASIC hardware acquisition timing as profitable, marginal, or unprofitable based on one-year ROI.
