Dynamic Representational Synchrony through Collective Predictive Coding: A Computational Model of Parent-Infant Homeostatic Co-Regulation
Authors: Yushi Tsubamoto, Takato Horii
First: 2026-05-08T09:54:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:07:53+00:00
Comments: 11pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inter-brain synchrony (IBS) observed in real-time dyadic interactions, including parent-infant exchanges, suggests that two agents can align their internal representations through interaction. Yet computational accounts of how such alignment can arise between agents that have only local sensory access and asymmetric internal knowledge remain underdeveloped. We propose a constructive model of parent-infant homeostatic co-regulation that integrates a POMDP formulation of active interoceptive inference with the Metropolis-Hastings Naming Game (MHNG) derived from the Collective Predictive Coding (CPC) hypothesis. In our model, the parent and infant agents agree on homeostatic regulatory actions for the infant's visceral state through a shared communicative variable generated by a locally computable Metropolis-Hastings probability. The parent observes the infant through body-generated exteroceptive cues, whereas the infant directly senses its own visceral state through interoception. This difference in access modality is implemented as asymmetric generative-model knowledge: the parent knows how actions transform visceral states but must learn what the infant's bodily cues indicate, whereas the infant perceives its visceral state directly but must learn how actions affect it. We quantify the degree of representational alignment using the Jensen-Shannon divergence between the two agents' latent representations. Notably, this synchrony emerged far earlier than the generative-model convergence and was maintained despite heterogeneous generative-model knowledge, indicating that it does not require fully shared world models. These findings support CPC as a candidate computational framework for explaining how dynamic representational synchrony relevant to IBS can emerge through local interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过集体预测编码实现动态表征同步：亲子稳态共调节的计算模型
在实时双人互动（包括亲子互动）中观察到的脑间同步（IBS）表明，两个主体可通过互动对齐其内部表征。然而，关于如何在仅具有局部感官访问权限且内部知识不对称的主体间实现这种对齐的计算解释仍不充分。我们提出一个亲子稳态共调节的建构性模型，该模型整合了主动内感受推断的POMDP表述与源自集体预测编码（CPC）假说的Metropolis-Hastings命名游戏（MHNG）。在本模型中，亲子双方通过由局部可计算的Metropolis-Hastings概率生成的共享通信变量达成对婴儿内脏状态稳态调节行动的一致。父母通过身体产生的外感受线索观察婴儿，而婴儿则通过内感受直接感知自身内脏状态。这种感知模态差异被实现为不对称生成模型知识：父母知晓行动如何改变内脏状态，但需学习婴儿身体线索的含义；而婴儿直接感知内脏状态，但需学习行动如何影响它。我们使用两个主体潜在表征间的Jensen-Shannon散度量化表征对齐程度。值得注意的是，这种同步性在生成模型收敛之前就已出现，并在生成模型知识异质性的情况下得以维持，表明其无需完全共享的世界模型。这些发现支持CPC作为解释通过局部互动如何产生与IBS相关的动态表征同步性的候选计算框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to explain how inter-brain synchrony arises despite asymmetric sensory access and differing internal models, this study proposes a computational model integrating active interoceptive inference via POMDPs with the Metropolis-Hastings Naming Game from Collective Predictive Coding. The model simulates parent-infant dyads where the parent infers the infant’s visceral state from exteroceptive cues while the infant directly senses its own state, each updating beliefs through locally computable Metropolis-Hastings probabilities. Representational alignment is measured via Jensen-Shannon divergence between latent states, revealing that synchrony emerges early and persists even before full generative-model convergence, suggesting it does not require complete model agreement.
为解释亲子互动中观察到的脑间同步现象，本研究提出一个计算模型，说明感官访问不对称的两个主体如何实现表征同步。该模型结合基于POMDP的主动内感受推断与集体预测编码中的Metropolis-Hastings命名游戏，使父母与婴儿通过局部可计算的共享通信变量协调内稳态行为。父母通过身体生成的外感受线索推断婴儿状态，婴儿则直接感知自身内感受状态，但双方均通过局部计算学习对方的生成模型。实验结果表明，通过Jensen-Shannon散度衡量的表征同步在生成模型完全收敛前即已出现并持续存在，说明同步无需完全共享的世界模型。
L2IR: Revealing Latent Intent in Graph Fraud Detection
Authors: Jinsheng Guo, Zhenhao Weng, Yibo Liu, Yan Qiao, Meng Li
First: 2026-05-25T17:06:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:06:13+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph fraud detection has long depended on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to propagate and aggregate information across relational data. A critical obstacle in practice, however, is that fraudsters frequently disguise themselves by forging numerous connections with benign users, causing fraud signals to be progressively diluted during neighborhood aggregation and undermining detection reliability. While recent efforts have used Large Language Models (LLMs) to provide rich semantic cues for fraud detection, the underlying intent behind suspicious connections remains insufficiently explored. Compounding this issue, the scarcity of annotated fraud samples makes it difficult to train detectors that remain robust under heavy camouflage. To address these gaps, we propose L2IR, an LLM-driven Latent Intent Revealing framework for graph fraud detection. By uncovering latent intent from both user behaviors and suspicious connections, L2IR extracts intent-aware representations from raw behavioral traces and reasons about the true purpose behind individual connections, effectively distinguishing supportive links from misleading ones. It further incorporates adaptive self-training to enhance robustness under limited supervision. Evaluations on two real-world datasets characterized by pervasive camouflage demonstrate that L2IR surpasses strong baselines and can function as a plug-in enhancement for a range of GNN-based detectors, improving AUPRC by up to 8.27%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：L2IR：揭示图欺诈检测中的潜在意图
图欺诈检测长期依赖图神经网络（GNN）在关系数据中传播和聚合信息。然而，实践中一个关键障碍是欺诈者常通过伪造大量与良性用户的关系来伪装自己，导致欺诈信号在邻居聚合过程中逐步稀释，从而削弱检测可靠性。尽管近期研究尝试利用大语言模型（LLM）为欺诈检测提供丰富的语义线索，但可疑连接背后的潜在意图仍被探索不足。此外，标注欺诈样本的稀缺性也使得训练在强伪装下仍保持鲁棒性的检测器变得困难。为解决这些问题，我们提出L2IR，一种基于LLM的潜在意图揭示框架，用于图欺诈检测。L2IR从用户行为和可疑连接中挖掘潜在意图，从原始行为轨迹中提取意图感知表示，并推理每个连接的真实目的，有效区分支持性链接与误导性链接。同时，它还结合自适应自训练以提升在有限监督下的鲁棒性。在两个具有广泛伪装特征的真实世界数据集上的评估表明，L2IR超越了多个强基线方法，可作为多种GNN检测器的插件增强模块，最高提升AUPRC达8.27%。

Motivated by the challenge of fraudsters diluting signals through camouflage connections in graph data, L2IR introduces an LLM-driven framework to uncover latent intent behind suspicious links. It extracts intent-aware representations from user behaviors and reasons about the purpose of individual connections to distinguish genuine from misleading ties, then enhances robustness via adaptive self-training. Experiments on real-world datasets show L2IR outperforms strong baselines, boosting AUPRC by up to 8.27% when integrated with GNN detectors.
为应对欺诈者通过与良性用户建立虚假连接稀释检测信号的问题，本文提出L2IR框架，利用大语言模型挖掘图中可疑连接背后的潜在意图，从用户行为中提取意图感知表示，区分支持性与误导性连接，并结合自适应自训练提升小样本下的鲁棒性。在两个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，L2IR显著优于现有基线方法，AUPRC提升最高达8.27%，可作为GNN检测器的插件增强模块。
DRScaffold: Boosting Dense-Scene Reasoning in Lightweight Vision Language Models
Authors: Xinrui Shi, Kai Liu, Ziqing Zhang, Jianze Li, Anqi Li, Yulun Zhang
First: 2026-05-25T17:05:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:05:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Lightweight vision-language models perform competitively on standard benchmarks yet fail systematically in dense-scene reasoning, where multiple objects, attributes, and relations must be jointly grounded and resolved through multi-step inference. Such capability is critical for real-world applications where models must reliably interpret cluttered environments. Yet existing training signals provide no explicit grounding between reasoning steps and the underlying visual entities and relations, leaving lightweight models free to generate fluent but visually unanchored reasoning chains. To address this gap, we first introduce DRBench, a benchmark of 14,573 questions across 2,943 images, organized into five task categories spanning three progressive reasoning layers. Building on DRBench, we propose DRScaffold, a supervised fine-tuning framework that decomposes the supervision target into four causally ordered stages, enforcing grounded reasoning without architectural modification. Experiments on three lightweight VLMs demonstrate substantial gains on DRBench while preserving or improving performance on general-purpose benchmarks. Notably, Qwen2.5-VL-3B trained with DRScaffold surpasses the frozen Qwen2.5-VL-32B on DRBench, demonstrating that structured supervision can substitute for a significant portion of model scale in dense-scene reasoning. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/irene-shi/DRScaffold .
中文标题/摘要
标题：DRScaffold：提升轻量级视觉语言模型在密集场景推理中的表现
轻量级视觉-语言模型在标准基准测试中表现优异，但在需要联合定位多个物体、属性和关系并通过多步推理解决的密集场景推理任务中系统性失败。这种能力对现实世界应用至关重要，因为模型必须可靠地理解杂乱环境。然而，现有训练信号未提供推理步骤与底层视觉实体和关系之间的显式锚定，导致轻量级模型可生成流畅但缺乏视觉锚定的推理链。为解决此问题，我们首先提出DRBench，包含2943张图像上的14573个问题，按五个任务类别组织，覆盖三个渐进式推理层次。基于DRBench，我们提出DRScaffold，一种监督微调框架，将监督目标分解为四个因果有序阶段，强制执行锚定推理，无需修改模型架构。在三个轻量级VLM上的实验表明，DRScaffold在DRBench上显著提升性能，同时保持或改善通用基准表现。值得注意的是，使用DRScaffold训练的Qwen2.5-VL-3B在DRBench上超越冻结的Qwen2.5-VL-32B，证明结构化监督可替代密集场景推理中相当一部分模型规模。我们的代码和模型可在https://github.com/irene-shi/DRScaffold获取。

Experiments on three lightweight VLMs show significant performance gains on DRBench while maintaining or improving general benchmarks; notably, Qwen2.5-VL-3B with DRScaffold outperforms the frozen Qwen2.5-VL-32B, indicating structured supervision can compensate for model scale in dense-scene reasoning.
为提升轻量级视觉语言模型在复杂密集场景中的多步推理能力，作者构建了包含14,573个问题和2,943张图像的DRBench基准，按五类任务分层组织。他们提出DRScaffold方法，通过将推理分解为四个因果有序阶段进行监督微调，强制视觉锚定而无需修改模型结构。实验表明，该方法在DRBench上显著提升性能，同时保持或增强通用基准表现，其中Qwen2.5-VL-3B微调后超越冻结的Qwen2.5-VL-32B，说明结构化监督可部分替代模型规模以提升密集场景推理能力。
Bridging the Semantic-Action Gap in Visual Token Pruning for Efficient VLA Inference
Authors: Ziyan Liu, Yeqiu Chen, Hongyi Cai, Tao Lin, Shuo Yang, Zheng Liu, Bo Zhao
First: 2025-11-20T15:16:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:05:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown great potential for embodied AI by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and action execution. In real-time deployment, these models must process continuous visual streams, incurring substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning -- a mainstream technique for accelerating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) by retaining salient tokens while discarding redundant ones -- offers a natural candidate solution to this challenge. However, directly applying VLM-oriented pruning methods to VLA inference can cause severe degradation in manipulation performance. Our analysis attributes this degradation to a key mismatch: VLA inference exhibits distinct attention patterns between the vision-language prefill stage and the action-decode stage, so pruning based only on context-prefill semantic salience is biased toward semantic cues and may remove action-critical visual tokens. Motivated by this observation, we propose VLA-Pruner, an effective plug-and-play token pruning method grounded in the visual requirements of VLA inference, further exploiting the temporal continuity of robot manipulation. Specifically, VLA-Pruner estimates visual-token importance from both semantic prefilling and temporally smoothed action relevance, and then applies a Combine-then-Filter strategy to retain compact, non-redundant tokens under the compute budget. Experiments show that VLA-Pruner outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across multiple VLA architectures, achieving up to 1.99x speedup with comparable manipulation quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉标记剪枝中语义-动作鸿沟的弥合：面向高效VLA推理
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通过融合视觉感知、语言理解和动作执行，在具身AI中展现出巨大潜力。在实时部署中，这些模型需处理连续视觉流，带来显著计算开销。视觉标记剪枝——主流加速视觉-语言模型（VLM）的技术，通过保留显著标记并丢弃冗余标记，是解决该挑战的自然候选方案。然而，直接将面向VLM的剪枝方法应用于VLA推理会导致操作性能严重下降。我们的分析指出，该退化源于关键不匹配：VLA推理在视觉-语言预填充阶段与动作解码阶段表现出不同的注意力模式，仅基于上下文预填充语义显著性进行剪枝会偏向语义线索，可能移除动作关键的视觉标记。为此，我们提出VLA-Pruner，一种基于VLA推理视觉需求的高效即插即用标记剪枝方法，进一步利用机器人操作的时间连续性。具体而言，VLA-Pruner从语义预填充和时序平滑的动作相关性两方面评估视觉标记重要性，再采用“合并后过滤”策略，在计算预算内保留紧凑且非冗余的标记。实验表明，VLA-Pruner在多种VLA架构上超越现有最先进方法，最高实现1.99倍加速，同时保持相当的操作质量。

To address the inefficiency of real-time VLA inference caused by continuous visual processing, the authors propose VLA-Pruner, a token pruning method tailored for Vision-Language-Action models. Unlike standard VLM pruning that prioritizes semantic salience during prefill, VLA-Pruner integrates both semantic relevance and temporally smoothed action relevance to preserve critical visual tokens for manipulation. Evaluated across multiple VLA architectures, VLA-Pruner achieves up to 1.99x speedup while maintaining comparable manipulation performance, demonstrating its effectiveness in bridging the semantic-action gap.
为解决视觉语言动作模型在实时推理中因连续视觉处理导致的计算效率低下问题，本文提出VLA-Pruner，一种面向VLA推理的视觉令牌剪枝方法。研究发现，传统视觉语言模型剪枝方法因忽视动作关键视觉令牌而降低操作性能。VLA-Pruner结合语义预填充阶段的视觉重要性与时间平滑的动作相关性，采用先合并后过滤策略保留高效非冗余令牌。实验表明，该方法在多个VLA架构上实现最高1.99倍加速，同时保持相当的操作质量。
Peak-Then-Collapse and the Four Interface Channels of Knowledge-Graph Tool Use
Authors: Tianda Sun, Dimitar Kazakov
First: 2026-05-25T17:05:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:05:35+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We test the standard RLVR tool-use recipe -- GRPO on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct -- on a deliberately minimal knowledge-graph tool API: four Freebase navigation verbs over Complex WebQuestions. Under a self-verifiable retrieval reward, the policy's tool-grounded answer rate climbs from $3.8\%$ to $9.6\%$ over 250 steps, then collapses to $0\%$ within a single 50-step window -- a \emph{peak-then-collapse} pattern replicated across four seeds. Across seven reward designs, we find four recurring failure modes: adding denser or more targeted proxy rewards shifts the failure mode rather than eliminating it. We argue that a key difference from Python interpreters, web search, and JSON APIs is interface feedback: their failures often leak natural-language signal the model saw in pretraining. A Python traceback names the failing line; an empty Freebase result \texttt{[]} does not. Stripping away that surface exposes a degradation regime that same-family reward redesigns do not fix. A direct oracle ablation rules out relation selection: injecting gold relations at every retrieval call lifts exact-match accuracy by only $+0.20$~pp, and $95.4\%$ of retrieval-dependent errors are retrieval-composition failures rather than answer-extraction failures. As a mitigation, one-iteration self-distillation reaches $40.0\%$ EM at 7B and is capacity-invariant: doubling capacity to 14B improves EM by only $0.25$~pp, and initialization barely matters -- the ceiling appears interface-bound within the 7B--14B range tested.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先高峰后崩溃：知识图谱工具使用的四种接口通道
我们在一个刻意简化的知识图谱工具API（四个Freebase导航动词，用于Complex WebQuestions）上，测试标准的RLVR工具使用方案——在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上使用GRPO。在自验证检索奖励下，策略的工具锚定回答率从3.8%上升至9.6%（250步），随后在单个50步窗口内骤降至0%——这一‘先高峰后崩溃’模式在四个种子中均重现。在七种奖励设计中，我们发现四种反复出现的失败模式：增加更密集或更精准的代理奖励仅改变失败模式，而非消除它。我们认为，与Python解释器、网络搜索和JSON API的关键区别在于接口反馈：它们的失败往往泄露模型在预训练中见过的自然语言信号。Python回溯会指出失败行号；而空的Freebase结果[]则不会。剥离这一表层后，暴露的是同家族奖励重设计无法修复的退化机制。直接Oracle消融实验排除了关系选择问题：在每次检索调用中注入黄金关系仅提升精确匹配准确率0.20个百分点，且95.4%的检索依赖性错误源于检索组合失败而非答案提取失败。作为缓解措施，单轮自蒸馏在7B模型上达到40.0%的EM，且容量无关：将容量从7B提升至14B仅提升EM 0.25个百分点，初始化几乎无影响——在测试的7B至14B范围内，天花板似乎受接口限制。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the performance of the GRPO reinforcement learning method applied to a minimal knowledge-graph tool API using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, revealing a 'peak-then-collapse' pattern where tool-grounded answer accuracy rises to 9.6% over 250 steps before dropping to 0% within 50 steps, consistently across four seeds. Across seven reward designs, four recurring failure modes were identified, none of which were resolved by denser or more targeted proxy rewards, suggesting that the issue stems from the lack of natural-language feedback in tool interface failures (e.g., empty Freebase results vs. Python tracebacks). Oracle ablation showed that relation selection is not the core problem, as injecting gold relations only improved exact-match accuracy by 0.20pp, and 95.4% of retrieval-dependent errors were due to retrieval-composition failures. A self-distillation mitigation achieved 40.0% EM accuracy at 7B, with minimal gains from scaling to 14B or changing initialization, indicating performance is interface-bound rather than capacity-limited.
为探究强化学习结合检索（RLVR）在工具使用中的持续性问题，该研究在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上测试GRPO算法，使用仅含四个Freebase导航动词的极简知识图谱API。模型在250步内工具接地回答率从3.8%提升至9.6%，随后在50步内骤降至0%——四组种子实验均呈现“峰值后崩溃”模式。七种奖励设计揭示四种反复出现的失败模式，密集或定向代理奖励无法根除问题，表明界面反馈缺失（如无类似Python错误提示的自然语言信号）是根本原因。消融实验显示关系选择非瓶颈：注入黄金关系仅提升0.20pp准确率，95.4%错误源于检索组合而非答案提取。单轮自蒸馏在7B模型上达到40.0%精确匹配率，容量翻倍至14B仅提升0.25pp，初始化影响微弱，表明性能上限受界面约束。
KAPLAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Prognostic Learnable Activation Networks for Survival Analysis
Authors: Stelios Boulitsakis Logothetis, Angela Wood, Pietro Liò
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-05-21T22:30:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:03:47+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 3 figures, 13 supplementary pages. Submitted to NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Survival analysis aims to model how covariates and time jointly shape the time-to-event distribution under right censoring. Classical methods such as the Cox model and generalised additive models (GAMs) require interactions and time-varying effects to be manually specified, which is increasingly impractical on rich clinical datasets. We introduce KAPLAN-HR, a B-spline Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) for nonparametric estimation of the conditional hazard as a joint function of covariates and time. A single-layer KAPLAN-HR model recovers a GAM, while deeper architectures capture interactions and time-varying effects through composition. We establish a convergence rate for the nonparametric KAN hazard estimator that depends only on the smoothness of the underlying KAN representation and not on the covariate dimension, thereby mitigating the curse of dimensionality for KAN-representable targets. In evaluations over six clinical benchmark datasets, KAPLAN-HR matches or exceeds the predictive performance of established statistical and deep learning survival methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KAPLAN：用于生存分析的 Kolmogorov-Arnold 可学习激活网络
生存分析旨在建模协变量与时间如何在右删失条件下共同塑造事件发生时间分布。经典方法如Cox模型和广义加性模型（GAMs）要求手动指定交互作用和时变效应，这在丰富的临床数据集上日益不切实际。我们引入KAPLAN-HR，一种基于B样条的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN），用于非参数估计协变量与时间的联合条件风险函数。单层KAPLAN-HR模型可恢复GAM，而更深的架构通过组合捕捉交互作用与时变效应。我们建立了非参数KAN风险估计量的收敛速率，该速率仅依赖于底层KAN表示的平滑性，而非协变量维度，从而缓解了KAN可表示目标的维度灾难问题。在六个临床基准数据集上的评估表明，KAPLAN-HR的预测性能与现有统计和深度学习生存方法相当或更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of classical survival models that require manual specification of interactions and time-varying effects, the authors propose KAPLAN-HR, a B-spline Kolmogorov-Arnold Network designed to nonparametrically estimate the conditional hazard as a joint function of covariates and time. The model leverages the compositional structure of KANs to capture complex interactions and time-dependent effects, with single-layer variants recovering generalized additive models. Theoretical analysis establishes a convergence rate dependent only on the smoothness of the underlying function, not covariate dimension, alleviating the curse of dimensionality. Empirical results across six clinical datasets show KAPLAN-HR matches or surpasses performance of established statistical and deep learning survival methods.
为了解决传统生存分析模型需手动指定交互项和时变效应的局限性，作者提出了KAPLAN-HR，一种基于B样条的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络，用于非参数估计协变量与时间的联合条件风险函数。该方法通过网络深度实现复杂交互与时变效应的自动建模，单层结构可还原广义加性模型。理论分析表明，其收敛速度仅依赖于目标函数的光滑性，而非协变量维度，有效缓解了维度灾难。在六个临床数据集上的实验显示，KAPLAN-HR在预测性能上达到或超越现有统计与深度学习生存分析方法。
CITYREP: A Unified Benchmark for Urban Representations Across Cities, Tasks, and Modalities
Authors: Junyuan Liu, Xinglei Wang, Zichao Zeng, Jiazhuang Feng, Quan Qin, Ilya Ilyankou, Guangsheng Dong, Tao Cheng
First: 2026-05-25T17:03:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:03:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urban representation learning encodes complex urban environments into general-purpose embeddings for diverse downstream tasks and emerging urban foundation models. However, current evaluations are limited, typically focusing on one or two cities and tasks and relying on random splits that introduce spatial leakage, leading to inflated performance and weak support for cross-location generalization and fair comparison. To address this, we propose CityRep, a unified benchmark that evaluates urban representations across data modalities, cities, and tasks using spatially structured splits. CityRep consists of three key components: (1) a spatial unit-agnostic evaluation framework that supports heterogeneous urban representations through a standardized alignment module; (2) a unified evaluation protocol using block-based spatial splits to mitigate spatial leakage and enable rigorous model comparison; and (3) an extensible multi-city, multi-task benchmark suite spanning 8 cities and 8 tasks across regression, classification, and distribution prediction. We evaluate 11 representative urban representation models. Results show that performance is highly sensitive to the split protocol, with random splits inflating scores and altering model rankings. We also observe substantial variability across cities and tasks, underscoring the need for generalization-aware evaluation. CityRep is released as a reproducible benchmark with datasets, evaluation pipelines, and diagnostic tools to facilitate fair comparison and support future research in urban representation learning towards urban foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CITYREP：面向城市、任务与模态的统一城市表征基准
城市表征学习将复杂的城市场景编码为通用嵌入，以支持多样化的下游任务和新兴的城市基础模型。然而，当前评估方法受限，通常仅聚焦于一到两个城市和任务，并依赖随机分割导致空间泄漏，从而夸大性能并削弱跨区域泛化能力与公平比较。为此，我们提出CityRep，一个通过空间结构化分割评估城市表征的统一基准，涵盖多模态、多城市与多任务。CityRep包含三个核心组件：（1）空间单元无关的评估框架，通过标准化对齐模块支持异构城市表征；（2）基于区块的空间分割统一评估协议，以缓解空间泄漏并实现严谨模型比较；（3）可扩展的多城市、多任务基准套件，覆盖8个城市和8项任务（回归、分类与分布预测）。我们评估了11个代表性城市表征模型。结果表明，分割协议对性能影响显著，随机分割会虚高分数并改变模型排名。同时，城市与任务间存在显著差异，凸显泛化感知评估的必要性。CityRep作为可复现基准发布，包含数据集、评估流程与诊断工具，以促进公平比较并支持城市表征学习向城市基础模型发展的未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized, spatially fair evaluations for urban representation models, this paper introduces CityRep, a unified benchmark that assesses urban embeddings across multiple cities, tasks, and modalities using block-based spatial splits to prevent spatial leakage. The method includes a unit-agnostic evaluation framework with a standardized alignment module, a rigorous evaluation protocol, and an extensible benchmark suite covering eight cities and eight tasks. Experimental results across eleven models reveal that random splits inflate performance and distort model rankings, while significant variability across cities and tasks highlights the need for generalization-aware evaluation.
为解决当前城市表征学习评估缺乏标准化和空间公平性的问题，本文提出CityRep统一基准，通过块状空间分割评估城市表征在多城市、多任务和多模态下的表现，以消除空间泄漏。该方法包含单元无关评估框架、标准化对齐模块、严谨评估协议及覆盖八个城市和八项任务的可扩展基准套件。实验表明，随机分割会虚高性能并扭曲模型排名，而不同城市和任务间表现差异显著，凸显了构建泛化感知评估基准的必要性。
Length Generalization with Log-Depth Recurrent Units
Authors: Charles Pert, Dalal Alrajeh, Alessandra Russo
First: 2026-05-25T17:02:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:02:29+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Length generalization remains a persistent challenge for neural networks: recurrent models tend to suffer from positional biases, while transformers are constrained by fixed computational depth. Regular languages provide a frequently used testbed for evaluating length generalization, as label prediction can be checked for any sequence length. We propose MLP-LDRU, a type of Log-Depth Recurrent Unit, which captures a class of associativity-biased operators designed to approximate recurrence through parallel reduction. We evaluate MLP-LDRU on 21 regular-language tasks, consisting of standard benchmarks and new prefix languages, where it achieves 100% out-of-distribution accuracy on 18 tasks and at least 99.9% on the remaining 3 when increasing max training length, outperforming comparable recurrent and attention-based models. We further evaluate MLP-LDRU beyond regular languages on ListOps and NLP classification benchmarks, where it performs competitively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用对数深度循环单元实现长度泛化
长度泛化仍是神经网络面临的持续挑战：循环模型易受位置偏差影响，而Transformer则受限于固定计算深度。正则语言常被用作评估长度泛化的测试平台，因为标签预测可在任意序列长度下进行验证。我们提出MLP-LDRU，一种对数深度循环单元，其捕捉一类具有结合性偏置的操作符，旨在通过并行约简近似递归。我们在21个正则语言任务上评估MLP-LDRU，包括标准基准和新前缀语言，当增加最大训练长度时，其在18个任务上达到100%的分布外准确率，在其余3个任务上至少达到99.9%，优于同类循环和注意力模型。我们进一步在ListOps和NLP分类基准上评估MLP-LDRU，其表现具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty neural networks face in generalizing to varying sequence lengths, this paper introduces MLP-LDRU, a Log-Depth Recurrent Unit that approximates recurrence via parallel reduction using associativity-biased operators. Evaluated on 21 regular-language tasks, including standard benchmarks and novel prefix languages, MLP-LDRU achieves 100% out-of-distribution accuracy on 18 tasks and at least 99.9% on the remaining three when training length is extended, surpassing comparable recurrent and attention-based models. Its performance is also competitive on non-regular benchmarks like ListOps and NLP classification tasks.
为解决神经网络在不同序列长度下泛化能力不足的问题，本文提出MLP-LDRU，一种通过并行归约模拟递归的对数深度循环单元，其核心是建模具有结合性偏置的运算。在21个正则语言任务上，当训练最大长度增加时，MLP-LDRU在18个任务上达到100%准确率，在其余3个任务上至少达到99.9%，优于同类循环与注意力模型；在ListOps和NLP分类等非正则任务上亦表现竞争力。
Everything at Every Scale: Scale-Invariant Diffusion with Continuous Super-Resolution
Authors: Zixin Jessie Chen, Zhuo Chen, Archer Wang, Jeff Gore, William T. Freeman, Congyue Deng, Marin Soljačić
First: 2026-05-25T17:01:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T17:01:21+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Creating images from noise is image generation; reconstructing fine details from coarse inputs is super-resolution. Despite their practical differences, both can be understood as reversing information loss across scales. We introduce $\textbf{SKILD}$, a $\textbf{S}$cale-invariant $\textbf{K}$-Space $\textbf{I}$mage $\textbf{L}$earning $\textbf{D}$iffusion model that unifies generation and continuous super-resolution within a single unconditional framework. Both natural images and critical physical systems exhibit scale invariance, and we leverage it to design a forward process that attenuates image content from fine to coarse scales while injecting spectrum-matched Gaussian noise, making scale an explicit coordinate of the diffusion dynamics. The same trained reverse process performs generation and continuous super-resolution by varying only the starting timestep: $\textit{no task-specific architecture, no conditioning branch, no classifier-free guidance, no retraining per scale factor}$. Empirically, SKILD reaches FID $2.65$ and Inception Score $9.63$ on unconditional CIFAR-10, performs $2\times$--$8\times$ super-resolution on ImageNet from a single unconditional checkpoint while outperforming conditional models across perceptual metrics, and reconstructs critical Ising models whose connected four-point correlations closely track the ground truth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：各尺度下的万物：具有连续超分辨率的尺度不变扩散模型
从噪声生成图像属于图像生成；从粗略输入重建细粒度细节属于超分辨率。尽管二者在实际应用上存在差异，但均可理解为跨尺度信息损失的逆向过程。我们提出了SKILD（尺度不变K空间图像学习扩散模型），它在一个无条件框架内统一了图像生成与连续超分辨率。自然图像和关键物理系统均表现出尺度不变性，我们利用这一特性设计了一个前向过程：从细尺度到粗尺度逐步衰减图像内容，同时注入频谱匹配的高斯噪声，使尺度成为扩散动态的显式坐标。同一训练好的逆向过程仅通过调整起始时间步即可实现图像生成与连续超分辨率：无需任务特定架构、无需条件分支、无需分类器自由引导、无需针对不同尺度因子重新训练。实验证明，SKILD在无条件CIFAR-10上达到FID 2.65、Inception Score 9.63；在ImageNet上从单一无条件模型实现2倍至8倍超分辨率，且在感知指标上超越条件模型；并成功重建关键的伊辛模型，其关联四点相关性与真实值高度吻合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the shared underlying principle of reversing scale-dependent information loss in image generation and super-resolution, the authors propose SKILD, a scale-invariant diffusion model that operates in k-space to unify both tasks within a single unconditional framework. The model’s forward process attenuates fine-scale content while injecting spectrum-matched noise, treating scale as an explicit diffusion coordinate. The reverse process, trained once, generates images or performs continuous super-resolution simply by adjusting the starting timestep, without requiring task-specific architectures or retraining. Experimentally, SKILD achieves FID 2.65 and Inception Score 9.63 on CIFAR-10, delivers 2x–8x super-resolution on ImageNet without conditioning, outperforming conditional models in perceptual metrics, and accurately reconstructs critical Ising models with ground-truth-like four-point correlations.
受图像生成与超分辨率任务在尺度信息损失反转上的共性启发，作者提出SKILD模型，将二者统一于单个无条件扩散框架中。该方法设计了一种前向扩散过程，逐步衰减图像细尺度内容并注入频谱匹配的高斯噪声，使尺度成为扩散动力学的显式坐标。同一训练好的逆过程通过仅调整起始时间步即可实现图像生成或连续超分辨率，无需任务特定架构或重新训练。实验表明，SKILD在CIFAR-10上达到FID 2.65和Inception Score 9.63，对ImageNet实现2x–8x超分辨率且无需条件输入，感知指标优于条件模型，并能准确重建临界伊辛模型，其四点关联函数接近真实值。
Fine-Tuning Causal LLMs for Text Classification: Embedding-Based vs. Instruction-Based Approaches
Authors: Amirhossein Yousefiramandi, Ciaran Cooney
First: 2025-12-14T13:02:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:57:18+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We explore efficient strategies to fine-tune decoder-only Large Language Models (LLMs) for downstream text classification under resource constraints. Two approaches are investigated: (1) attaching a classification head to a pretrained causal LLM and fine-tuning it on the task, using the LLM's final-token embedding as a sequence representation, and (2) instruction-tuning the LLM in a prompt-to-response format for classification. To enable single-GPU fine-tuning of models up to 8B parameters, we combine 4-bit model quantization with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient training. Experiments on two patent benchmarks, a 5-class single-label internal corpus and the public WIPO-Alpha multi-label dataset with 14 categories, show that the embedding-head approach matches or exceeds fine-tuned BERT baselines on single-label classification while training 10-30x fewer parameters. Instruction-tuning is competitive only in the multi-label regime, and only with substantially larger trainable budgets of at least 100M parameters. These results demonstrate that directly leveraging the internal representations of causal LLMs, together with efficient fine-tuning techniques, yields strong classification performance under limited computational resources. We discuss the advantages of each approach and outline practical guidelines and future directions for optimizing LLM fine-tuning in classification scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于嵌入与指令微调因果LLM用于文本分类
我们在资源受限条件下探索高效微调解码器型大语言模型（LLM）用于下游文本分类的策略。研究两种方法：(1) 在预训练因果LLM上附加分类头并微调，利用LLM的最终token嵌入作为序列表示；(2) 以提示-响应格式对LLM进行指令微调以实现分类。为实现单GPU下对8B参数模型的微调，我们结合4位模型量化与低秩自适应（LoRA）进行参数高效训练。在两个专利基准数据集上的实验表明，嵌入头方法在单标签分类任务中性能可媲美或超越微调BERT基线，且训练参数量少10-30倍；指令微调仅在多标签场景下具有竞争力，且需至少100M参数的更大可训练预算。结果表明，直接利用因果LLM内部表示并结合高效微调技术，可在计算资源受限条件下获得强分类性能。我们讨论了各方法的优势，并提出实用指南与未来优化方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient text classification under resource constraints, this study compares embedding-based and instruction-based fine-tuning methods for causal LLMs. The embedding approach attaches a classification head to a pretrained LLM and uses its final-token embedding as input, while instruction-tuning adapts the model via prompt-response pairs. Both methods employ 4-bit quantization and LoRA to enable single-GPU training of up to 8B-parameter models. Experiments on two patent datasets show the embedding method achieves performance matching or surpassing fine-tuned BERT with 10-30x fewer trainable parameters in single-label tasks, while instruction-tuning only performs competitively in multi-label settings with at least 100M parameters. The findings suggest that leveraging internal LLM representations with parameter-efficient techniques is effective for classification under limited computational resources.
为在资源受限条件下实现高效的文本分类，本研究对比了基于嵌入和基于指令的因果LLM微调方法。嵌入方法将分类头连接至LLM的最终token嵌入，而指令微调采用提示-响应格式。两种方法均结合4位量化与LoRA技术，支持单GPU训练高达8B参数模型。在单标签任务中，嵌入方法使用10-30倍更少参数即可达到或超越BERT基线性能。指令微调仅在多标签任务且具备至少100M可训练参数时表现可比，凸显嵌入方法在受限环境中的效率优势。
CausaLab: A Scalable Environment for Interactive Causal Discovery Toward AI Scientists
Authors: Junlin Yang, Dylan Zhang, Xiangchen Song, Qirun Dai, Xiao Liu, Yuen Chen, Aniket Vashishtha, Jing Shi, Chenhao Tan, Hao Peng
First: 2026-05-25T16:57:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:57:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce CausaLab, a scalable environment for evaluating interactive causal discovery by LLM agents. Unlike prior evaluations, CausaLab evaluates both whether an agent can solve a problem using causal evidence and whether its answer is supported by a correct hypothesis about the underlying causal mechanism. Each episode places an agent in a synthetic laboratory: it receives prior measurement records, intervenes on a manipulator crystal, and predicts the resonance frequency of a held-out reactor crystal governed by the same mechanism. The hidden data-generating process is a randomly sampled structural causal model (SCM), so success requires recovering both a causal graph and structural equations rather than recalling prior knowledge. CausaLab also includes a domain-specific language that records the agent's evolving SCM hypothesis, making trajectories inspectable and comparable with ground truth. Experiments show a persistent gap between prediction and mechanism recovery: in the purely observational 6-node setting, GPT-5.2-high reaches 92% task accuracy but only 0.471 all-edge $F_1$. This observation further motivates our exploration of different interaction strategies: Mixed observation--intervention strategies improve structural fidelity: in the mixed 6-node setting, GPT-5.2-high achieves 80% on both task accuracy and all-edge $F_1$. Yet even strong agents struggle to design informative interventions, as pure intervention strategies perform poorly on both task accuracy and all-edge $F_1$. We identify premature stopping as a major weakness of agents, and show that asking the model to verify the consistency between its hypothesis and past data can help mitigate this issue. CausaLab therefore separates predictive success from causal understanding and exposes current LLM agents' limits as experimental causal reasoners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CausaLab：面向AI科学家的可扩展交互因果发现环境
我们引入CausaLab，一个用于评估大语言模型代理交互式因果发现能力的可扩展环境。与以往评估不同，CausaLab不仅评估代理能否利用因果证据解决问题，还评估其答案是否基于对底层因果机制的正确假设。每个实验回合将代理置于一个合成实验室中：代理接收先前测量记录，操作一个操纵晶体，并预测受相同机制支配的未见反应器晶体的共振频率。隐藏的数据生成过程是一个随机采样的结构因果模型（SCM），因此成功需要恢复因果图和结构方程，而非依赖先验知识。CausaLab还包含一种领域特定语言，记录代理不断演化的SCM假设，使轨迹可检视并与真实值比较。实验表明，预测与机制恢复之间存在持续差距：在纯观测6节点设置中，GPT-5.2-high达到92%任务准确率，但仅0.471全边F1值。这一观察进一步促使我们探索不同交互策略：混合观测-干预策略可提升结构保真度：在混合6节点设置中，GPT-5.2-high在任务准确率和全边F1值上均达80%。然而，即使强代理也难以设计信息性干预，纯干预策略在任务准确率和全边F1值上表现不佳。我们识别出代理的过早停止是主要弱点，并表明要求模型验证其假设与过往数据的一致性有助于缓解此问题。因此，CausaLab将预测成功与因果理解分离，揭示当前大语言模型代理作为实验因果推理者的局限性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLM agents' true causal reasoning capabilities beyond mere prediction, CausaLab introduces a scalable synthetic environment where agents must recover hidden causal mechanisms to solve tasks. Agents interact with a simulated lab, receiving observational data, performing interventions, and predicting outcomes governed by a randomly sampled structural causal model. The system tracks evolving hypotheses in a domain-specific language, enabling comparison with ground truth. Experiments reveal that while agents like GPT-5.2-high achieve high task accuracy (92% in observational setting), their causal structure recovery remains poor (F1=0.471), highlighting a gap between prediction and understanding. Mixed observation-intervention strategies improve both accuracy and structural fidelity (80% each), but pure intervention strategies underperform. The study identifies premature stopping as a key limitation and demonstrates that hypothesis verification helps mitigate it, underscoring LLMs' current inability to act as robust experimental causal reasoners.
为评估大语言模型在因果推理中的真实能力，CausaLab构建了一个可扩展的合成实验室环境，要求代理通过观测与干预数据恢复隐藏的因果机制以完成预测任务。代理使用领域特定语言构建和迭代结构因果模型（SCM），其性能基于随机采样的SCM。实验表明，尽管如GPT-5.2-high在纯观测场景下任务准确率高达92%，但因果结构恢复能力极弱（F1=0.471），揭示预测与理解能力的脱节。混合观测-干预策略可同时提升准确率与因果结构精度（均为80%），但纯干预策略表现不佳，凸显代理设计有效干预的困难。研究还发现代理常过早停止推理，通过要求其验证假设与数据一致性可缓解此问题。
Multi-Pair Temporal Sentence Grounding via Multi-Thread Knowledge Transfer Network
Authors: Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Changshuo Wang, Daizong Liu, Keke Tang, Jianfeng Dong, Pan Zhou, Beibei Li
Venue: AAAI 2025
First: 2024-12-20T08:50:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:54:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted by AAAI 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Given some video-query pairs with untrimmed videos and sentence queries, temporal sentence grounding (TSG) aims to locate query-relevant segments in these videos. Although previous respectable TSG methods have achieved remarkable success, they train each video-query pair separately and ignore the relationship between different pairs. We observe that the similar video/query content not only helps the TSG model better understand and generalize the cross-modal representation but also assists the model in locating some complex video-query pairs. Previous methods follow a single-thread framework that cannot co-train different pairs and usually spends much time re-obtaining redundant knowledge, limiting their real-world applications. To this end, in this paper, we pose a brand-new setting: Multi-Pair TSG, which aims to co-train these pairs. In particular, we propose a novel video-query co-training approach, Multi-Thread Knowledge Transfer Network, to locate a variety of video-query pairs effectively and efficiently. Firstly, we mine the spatial and temporal semantics across different queries to cooperate with each other. To learn intra- and inter-modal representations simultaneously, we design a cross-modal contrast module to explore the semantic consistency by a self-supervised strategy. To fully align visual and textual representations between different pairs, we design a prototype alignment strategy to 1) match object prototypes and phrase prototypes for spatial alignment, and 2) align activity prototypes and sentence prototypes for temporal alignment. Finally, we develop an adaptive negative selection module to adaptively generate a threshold for cross-modal matching. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness and efficiency of our proposed method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多线程知识迁移网络实现多对时序句子定位
给定包含未修剪视频和句子查询的视频-查询对，时序句子定位（TSG）旨在定位这些视频中与查询相关的片段。尽管先前的优秀TSG方法已取得显著成功，但它们分别训练每个视频-查询对，忽略了不同对之间的关系。我们观察到，相似的视频/查询内容不仅有助于TSG模型更好地理解并泛化跨模态表示，还能帮助模型定位一些复杂的视频-查询对。先前方法采用单线程框架，无法协同训练不同对，通常需耗费大量时间重新获取冗余知识，限制了其实际应用。为此，本文提出全新设定：多对TSG，旨在协同训练这些对。特别地，我们提出一种新颖的视频-查询协同训练方法——多线程知识迁移网络，以高效准确地定位多种视频-查询对。首先，我们挖掘不同查询间的空间与时间语义以相互协作。为同时学习模态内与模态间表示，我们设计跨模态对比模块，通过自监督策略探索语义一致性。为充分对齐不同对间的视觉与文本表示，我们设计原型对齐策略：1）匹配对象原型与短语原型以实现空间对齐，2）对齐活动原型与句子原型以实现时间对齐。最后，我们开发自适应负样本选择模块，自适应生成跨模态匹配阈值。大量实验表明，所提方法有效且高效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training video-query pairs in isolation and the missed opportunities for cross-pair knowledge transfer, this paper introduces Multi-Pair Temporal Sentence Grounding (TSG) and proposes a Multi-Thread Knowledge Transfer Network to co-train multiple video-query pairs simultaneously. The method mines spatial-temporal semantics across queries, employs a cross-modal contrast module for self-supervised intra- and inter-modal alignment, and uses prototype alignment to match visual and textual representations at both spatial and temporal levels. An adaptive negative selection module further refines cross-modal matching thresholds. Experiments demonstrate that this approach significantly improves grounding accuracy and computational efficiency compared to single-thread baselines.
为解决传统方法孤立训练视频-查询对、忽略跨对知识共享的问题，本文提出多对时序句子定位方法，通过多线程知识迁移网络实现多对联合训练。该方法通过挖掘不同查询间的时空语义、设计跨模态对比模块进行自监督表征学习，并通过原型对齐策略实现视觉与文本特征的空间和时序对齐，最后引入自适应负样本选择模块优化跨模态匹配阈值。实验表明，该方法在准确率和效率上均优于单线程基线方法。
A Multimodal 3D Foundation Model for Light Sheet Fluorescence Microscopy Enables Few-Shot Segmentation, Classification, and Deblurring
Authors: Adina Scheinfeld, Haotan Zhang, Shang Mu, Rudolf L. M. van Herten, Lucas Stoffl, Ali Erturk, Zhuhao Wu, Johannes C. Paetzold
First: 2026-05-25T16:50:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:50:58+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Light sheet fluorescence microscopy (LSM) enables high-resolution, three-dimensional (3D) imaging of biological specimens, providing rich volumetric data for studying cellular organization, pathology, and vascular networks. However, the size, dimensionality, and annotation burden of LSM data make supervised deep learning approaches costly and difficult to scale. Additionally, despite the abundance of unannotated LSM volumes, foundation models for this modality remain underexplored due to computational challenges and the complexity of volumetric representation learning. In this work, we introduce a 3D foundation model for LSM data, pretrained on a large curated collection of 3D images spanning multiple organisms, stains, and imaging protocols. We learn transferable volumetric representations by jointly optimizing for masked reconstruction and image-text alignment. The pretrained backbone drastically reduces the annotation burden, enabling efficient, few-shot adaptation for varied downstream tasks. We evaluate this approach on downstream segmentation, classification, and deblurring. Our results demonstrate consistent improvements over baselines, (1) when measured using standard evaluation metrics and (2) when rigorously assessed by domain experts. This highlights the potential of foundation model pretraining to reduce annotation requirements while improving performance across diverse LSM analysis tasks. Pretrained model weights and code for pretraining and finetuning are publicly available: https://github.com/AdinaScheinfeld/lsm_fm_public_repo.git.
中文标题/摘要

The model learns transferable volumetric representations through joint optimization of masked reconstruction and image-text alignment, enabling few-shot adaptation for segmentation, classification, and deblurring tasks. Experimental results show consistent performance improvements over baselines both in standard metrics and expert evaluations, demonstrating the model’s effectiveness in reducing annotation burden while enhancing downstream task performance.
为应对光片荧光显微镜（LSM）数据规模大、维度高且标注成本高的问题，本研究提出一种3D基础模型，基于跨物种、染色和成像协议的大量LSM数据进行预训练。该模型通过联合优化掩码重建与图像-文本对齐，学习可迁移的体数据表示，支持少样本下游任务如分割、分类与去模糊。实验表明，该模型在标准评估指标和领域专家评估中均优于基线方法，验证了预训练基础模型在降低标注需求的同时提升LSM分析任务性能的潜力。
HiGraph: A Large-Scale Hierarchical Graph Dataset for Malware Analysis
Authors: Han Chen, Hanchen Wang, Hongmei Chen, Ying Zhang, Lu Qin, Wenjie Zhang
First: 2025-09-02T09:10:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:45:37+00:00
Comments: updated dataset statistics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The advancement of graph-based malware analysis is critically limited by the absence of large-scale datasets that capture the inherent hierarchical structure of software. Existing methods often oversimplify programs into single level graphs, failing to model the crucial semantic relationship between high-level functional interactions and low-level instruction logic. To bridge this gap, we introduce \dataset, the largest public hierarchical graph dataset for malware analysis, comprising over \textbf{200M} Control Flow Graphs (CFGs) nested within \textbf{595K} Function Call Graphs (FCGs). This two-level representation preserves structural semantics essential for building robust detectors resilient to code obfuscation and malware evolution. We demonstrate HiGraph's utility through a large-scale analysis that reveals distinct structural properties of benign and malicious software, establishing it as a foundational benchmark for the community. The dataset and tools are publicly available at https://higraph.org.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiGraph：用于恶意软件分析的大规模层次图数据集
基于图的恶意软件分析进展受限于缺乏能捕捉软件固有层次结构的大规模数据集。现有方法常将程序简化为单层图，未能建模高层功能交互与底层指令逻辑之间的关键语义关系。为弥合这一差距，我们引入HiGraph——目前最大的公开层次图数据集，包含超过20000万个控制流图（CFGs），嵌套于59.5万个函数调用图（FCGs）中。该双层表示保留了构建抗代码混淆和恶意软件演化的稳健检测器所必需的结构语义。我们通过大规模分析展示了HiGraph的实用性，揭示了良性与恶意软件在结构特性上的差异，确立其为社区的基础基准。数据集和工具可在https://higraph.org公开获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of large-scale hierarchical graph datasets for malware analysis, this work introduces HiGraph, a two-level graph dataset comprising over 200 million Control Flow Graphs nested within 595,000 Function Call Graphs, designed to preserve semantic structure across code levels. The method constructs a hierarchical representation that captures both high-level functional interactions and low-level instruction logic, enabling more robust detection models resilient to obfuscation. Experimental results show distinct structural patterns between benign and malicious software, validating HiGraph as a foundational benchmark for future research in malware analysis.
为提升基于图的恶意软件检测能力，HiGraph构建了包含2亿多个控制流图嵌套于5.95万个函数调用图中的两级层次图数据集，保留了代码不同层级间的语义结构。该方法克服了传统单层图模型忽略功能与指令级关系的缺陷。实验表明，良性与恶意软件在结构特征上存在显著差异，验证了HiGraph作为抗混淆与演化对抗检测模型评估基准的有效性。
Relative Translation Invariant Wasserstein Distance
Authors: Binshuai Wang, Qiwei Di, Ming Yin, Mengdi Wang, Quanquan Gu, Peng Wei
First: 2024-09-04T03:41:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:44:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR). Final accepted version. The implementation is publicly available at \url{https://github.com/DRKWang/rw_metric}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Motivated by the Bures distance, we introduce a new family of distances, \emph{relative translation invariant Wasserstein distances}, denoted by $RW_p$, as an extension of the classical Wasserstein distances $W_p$ for $p \in [1, +\infty)$. We establish that $RW_p$ defines a valid metric and demonstrate that this type of metric is more intrinsic than the classical Wasserstein distance. A bi-level algorithm is designed to compute the general $RW_p$ distance between arbitrary discrete distributions. Moreover, when $p = 2$, we show that the optimal coupling matrix is invariant under distributional translation in the discrete setting, and we further propose two algorithms, the $\mathrm{RW}_2$-LP algorithm and the $\mathrm{RW}_2$-Sinkhorn algorithm, to improve the numerical stability of computing $W_2$ distance and the optimal coupling matrix solutions. Finally, we conduct three experiments to validate our theoretical results and algorithms. The first two experiments report that the $\mathrm{RW}_2$-LP algorithm and the $\mathrm{RW}_2$-Sinkhorn algorithm, both with and without normalization, can significantly reduce the numerical errors compared to standard algorithms. The third experiment shows that $RW_p$ algorithms are computationally scalable and applicable to the retrieval of similar thunderstorm patterns in practical applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相对平移不变的Wasserstein距离
受Bures距离启发，我们引入了一类新距离——相对平移不变的Wasserstein距离（记为$RW_p$），作为经典Wasserstein距离$W_p$（$p \in [1, +\infty)$）的扩展。我们证明$RW_p$构成一个有效度量，并表明此类度量比经典Wasserstein距离更具内在性。设计了一种双层算法，用于计算任意离散分布间的通用$RW_p$距离。当$p=2$时，我们证明在离散情形下最优耦合矩阵在分布平移下保持不变，并进一步提出$\mathrm{RW}_2$-LP算法和$\mathrm{RW}_2$-Sinkhorn算法，以提升计算$W_2$距离及最优耦合矩阵解的数值稳定性。最后，通过三个实验验证理论结果与算法：前两个实验表明，$\mathrm{RW}_2$-LP算法和$\mathrm{RW}_2$-Sinkhorn算法（含或不含归一化）相比标准算法可显著降低数值误差；第三个实验表明$RW_p$算法具有良好的计算可扩展性，适用于实际应用中相似雷暴模式的检索。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the Bures distance, this work introduces relative translation invariant Wasserstein distances (RW_p) as a generalization of classical Wasserstein distances, aiming to provide a more intrinsic metric for comparing probability distributions. The authors design a bi-level algorithm to compute RW_p for arbitrary discrete distributions and, for p=2, prove that the optimal coupling matrix is translation-invariant, leading to two specialized algorithms—RW_2-LP and RW_2-Sinkhorn—to enhance numerical stability in computing W_2. Experimental results show that both RW_2 algorithms significantly reduce numerical errors compared to standard methods, and RW_p is computationally scalable, successfully applied to retrieve similar thunderstorm patterns in real-world scenarios.
受Bures距离启发，本文提出相对平移不变Wasserstein距离（RW_p），作为经典Wasserstein距离的扩展，旨在通过平移不变性提升其内在性。作者设计了用于计算任意离散分布间RW_p距离的双层算法，并针对p=2情形提出RW_2-LP和RW_2-Sinkhorn两种算法以提高数值稳定性。实验表明，两种RW_2算法在有无归一化情况下均显著降低数值误差，且RW_p框架具备良好的计算可扩展性，成功应用于实际雷暴模式相似性检索任务。
From Next Token Prediction to (STRIPS) World Models
Authors: Carlos Núñez-Molina, Vicenç Gómez, Hector Geffner
First: 2025-09-16T14:03:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:41:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study whether next-token prediction can yield world models that truly support planning, in a controlled symbolic setting where propositional STRIPS action models are learned from action traces alone and correctness can be evaluated exactly. We introduce two architectures. The first is the STRIPS Transformer, a symbolically aligned model grounded in theoretical results linking transformers and the formal language structure of STRIPS domains. The second is a standard transformer architecture without explicit symbolic structure built in, for which we study different positional encoding schemes and attention aggregation mechanisms. We evaluate both architectures on five classical planning domains, measuring training accuracy, generalization, and planning performance across domains and problem sizes. Interestingly, both approaches can be used to produce models that support planning with off-the-shelf STRIPS planners over exponentially many unseen initial states and goals. Although the STRIPS Transformer incorporates a strong symbolic inductive bias, it is harder to optimize and requires larger datasets to generalize reliably. In contrast, a standard transformer with stick-breaking attention achieves near-perfect training accuracy and strong generalization. Finally, standard transformers without stick-breaking attention do not generalize to long traces, whereas a symbolic STRIPS model extracted from a transformer trained on shorter traces does.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从下一个词预测到（STRIPS）世界模型
我们在一个受控的符号环境中研究，下一个词预测是否能生成真正支持规划的世界模型，该环境中的命题STRIPS动作模型仅从动作轨迹中学习，且正确性可精确评估。我们引入两种架构：第一种是STRIPS Transformer，其符号对齐建模基于将Transformer与STRIPS领域形式语言结构关联的理论结果；第二种是无显式符号结构的标准Transformer，我们研究其不同位置编码方案和注意力聚合机制。我们在五个经典规划领域评估两种架构，测量训练准确率、泛化能力及跨领域和问题规模的规划表现。有趣的是，两种方法均可生成支持规划的模型，可处理指数级数量的未见初始状态和目标，且使用现成的STRIPS规划器。尽管STRIPS Transformer具有强符号归纳偏置，但优化难度更大，需更大数据集才能可靠泛化；相比之下，采用stick-breaking注意力的标准Transformer实现近乎完美的训练准确率和强泛化能力。最后，无stick-breaking注意力的标准Transformer无法泛化到长轨迹，而从短轨迹训练的Transformer提取的符号STRIPS模型则可实现泛化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether next-token prediction can generate world models capable of supporting planning, the authors investigate this in a symbolic STRIPS setting where action models are learned from traces and correctness is verifiable. They propose two architectures: the STRIPS Transformer, which embeds symbolic structure grounded in formal language theory, and a standard transformer with varied positional encodings and attention mechanisms. Experiments across five planning domains show both models can support planning with off-the-shelf STRIPS planners over exponentially many unseen states and goals. The STRIPS Transformer, despite its symbolic bias, is harder to optimize and needs larger datasets for reliable generalization, while a standard transformer with stick-breaking attention achieves near-perfect training accuracy and strong generalization. Without stick-breaking, standard transformers fail to generalize to long traces, whereas symbolic models extracted from short-trace-trained transformers still generalize well.
为探究基于下一个token预测是否能生成支持规划的世界模型，研究在符号化的STRIPS环境中进行，其中动作模型仅从轨迹中学习，且正确性可精确评估。论文提出两种架构：STRIPS Transformer，其嵌入符号结构并基于形式语言理论；以及标准Transformer，搭配不同位置编码与注意力机制。在五个规划领域实验中，两种模型均能支持对指数级未见初始状态与目标的规划。STRIPS Transformer虽具符号归纳偏置，但优化困难且需更大数据集才能可靠泛化；而采用stick-breaking注意力的标准Transformer训练准确率近完美且泛化能力强。无stick-breaking的标准Transformer无法泛化至长轨迹，而从短轨迹训练的Transformer提取的符号STRIPS模型表现良好。
Retrieval-Augmented Detection of Potentially Abusive Clauses in Chilean Terms of Service
Authors: Christoffer Loeffler, Tomás Rey Pizarro, Daniel Ignacio Miranda Vásquez, Andrea Martínez Freile
First: 2026-05-25T16:38:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:38:10+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 6 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Online Terms of Service often function as contracts of adhesion, creating asymmetries that may expose consumers to potentially abusive clauses. In Chile, assessing such clauses is legally challenging because some provisions clearly violate mandatory consumer law, whereas others depend on broader standards such as good faith and contractual imbalance. We present a retrieval-augmented generation framework for the automated detection and classification of potentially abusive clauses in Chilean Terms of Service. Designed for local execution, it combines efficient clause detection, hybrid dense--sparse retrieval, reranking, and prompt augmentation to support medium-sized open-weight language models. We also introduce the Chilean Abusive Terms of Service Extended corpus, comprising 100 contracts and 10,029 annotated clauses in 24 legally grounded categories spanning illegal, dark, and gray clauses. Experiments comparing commercial and open-weight language models, fine-tuned encoders, and traditional baselines show that retrieval-augmented prompting substantially improves performance and enables local models to approach larger cloud-based systems at lower computational and token cost. The study also contributes a refined legal annotation scheme and a practical design for AI-assisted consumer contract review.

标题：基于检索增强的智利服务条款中潜在滥用条款检测
在线服务条款常作为附从合同，造成不对称性，可能使消费者面临潜在滥用条款。在智利，评估此类条款具有法律挑战性：部分条款明显违反强制性消费者法，而其他条款则依赖更广泛的标准，如诚信原则和合同失衡。我们提出一种检索增强生成框架，用于自动检测和分类智利服务条款中的潜在滥用条款。该框架专为本地执行设计，结合高效条款检测、混合稠密-稀疏检索、重排序和提示增强，支持中等规模开源语言模型。我们还引入了智利服务条款滥用扩展语料库，包含100份合同和10,029个标注条款，涵盖24个法律依据明确的类别，包括非法、暗黑和灰色条款。实验对比商业和开源语言模型、微调编码器及传统基线方法，表明检索增强提示显著提升性能，使本地模型在更低计算和token成本下接近云端大型系统。本研究还贡献了精炼的法律标注方案和AI辅助消费者合同审查的实用设计。

为应对智利服务条款中识别滥用条款的法律复杂性——部分条款明显违法，部分则依赖模糊的诚信与合同失衡标准——本文提出一种检索增强生成框架，结合条款检测、混合检索、重排和提示增强，使中等规模开源模型能有效执行。在包含10,029条标注条款、覆盖24类法律标准的新数据集上，与商用模型、微调编码器及传统方法对比，该方法显著提升性能，使本地模型以更低计算和token成本接近云端大模型效果。研究同时贡献了精细化法律标注体系与实用的AI辅助合同审查方案。
STORM: Internalized Modeling for Spatial-Temporal Reasoning in Video-Language Models
Authors: Yiming Liang, Yixiao Chen, Yiyang Zhou, Yixuan Wang, Shoubin Yu, Andong Deng, Fuxiao Liu, Qin Zhang, Chen Chen, Mohit Bansal, Huaxiu Yao
First: 2026-05-25T16:33:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:33:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many video reasoning tasks require tracking motion, temporal order, and evolving visual states across frames. Existing methods built on large vision-language models (LVLMs) often address this challenge by externalizing reasoning through textual chain-of-thought (CoT), keyframe selection, repeated frame reinsertion, or external tool use. While effective, such pipelines increase inference-time latency and engineering complexity, and they force temporal-visual evidence to be serialized into text or repeatedly re-encoded from frames. Inspired by the intuition that visual reasoning can occur implicitly before verbalization, we propose STORMS (Spatial-Temporal reasOning via inteRnalized Modeling), a two-stage framework that teaches LVLMs to reason through bounded continuous latent trajectories instead of explicit textual CoT. In Stage I, STORMS aligns latent tokens with thought-video representations derived from generated videos, grounding the latent states in dynamic visual evidence. In Stage II, the model is further trained with answer-only supervision, encouraging the reasoning process to be internalized without step-by-step annotations. Generated thought videos are used only during training; at inference, STORMS performs a bounded latent rollout without regenerating videos, reinserting frames, or invoking external visual tools. Experiments on VideoMME, MVBench, TempCompass, and MMVU show that STORMS improves video reasoning accuracy while substantially reducing inference overhead compared with tool or video-generation-based reasoning pipelines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STORM：面向视频-语言模型的空间-时间推理的内化建模
许多视频推理任务需要跟踪运动、时间顺序及帧间演变的视觉状态。现有基于大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）的方法常通过外部化推理（如文本链式思维、关键帧选择、重复帧重插入或外部工具调用）应对这一挑战。虽然有效，但此类流程增加了推理延迟和工程复杂度，并迫使时空视觉证据被序列化为文本或反复从帧中重编码。受视觉推理可在语言表达前隐式发生的直觉启发，我们提出STORMS（通过内化建模实现空间-时间推理），一种两阶段框架，训练LVLM通过有界连续潜在轨迹而非显式文本链式思维进行推理。第一阶段，STORMS将潜在标记与由生成视频导出的思维-视频表示对齐，使潜在状态扎根于动态视觉证据。第二阶段，模型在仅答案监督下进一步训练，鼓励推理过程内化而不依赖逐步骤标注。训练时使用生成的思维视频；推理时，STORMS执行有界潜在轨迹展开，无需重新生成视频、重插入帧或调用外部视觉工具。在VideoMME、MVBench、TempCompass和MMVU上的实验表明，STORMS在提升视频推理准确率的同时，显著降低了与工具或视频生成相关推理流程相比的推理开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of externalizing video reasoning through text or repeated frame processing, STORMS proposes an internalized modeling approach that enables video-language models to reason via continuous latent trajectories instead of explicit chain-of-thought. In Stage I, latent tokens are aligned with thought-video representations generated during training to ground reasoning in dynamic visual evidence; in Stage II, the model is trained with answer-only supervision to internalize reasoning without step-by-step annotations. Experiments on VideoMME, MVBench, TempCompass, and MMVU demonstrate that STORMS achieves higher video reasoning accuracy while significantly reducing inference latency compared to methods relying on external tools or video generation.
为解决现有方法通过文本链式推理或重复帧处理导致推理延迟高、工程复杂的问题，STORMS提出一种内部建模方法，使视频-语言模型通过连续潜在轨迹进行空间-时间推理，而非显式文本推理。第一阶段通过生成的思维视频对潜在标记进行对齐，将推理与动态视觉证据绑定；第二阶段采用仅答案监督训练，促使推理过程内化，无需步骤标注。在VideoMME、MVBench、TempCompass和MMVU上的实验表明，STORMS在提升视频推理准确率的同时，显著降低了推理开销和复杂度，优于依赖外部工具或视频生成的方法。
Nonconvex Decentralized Stochastic Bilevel Optimization under Heavy-Tailed Noise
Authors: Xinwen Zhang, Yihan Zhang, Heng Liang, Hongchang Gao
First: 2025-09-19T02:51:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:32:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing decentralized stochastic optimization methods assume the lower-level loss function is strongly convex and the stochastic gradient noise has finite variance. These strong assumptions typically are not satisfied in real-world machine learning models. For example, learning on language data typically leads to heavy-tailed gradient. To address these limitations, we develop a novel decentralized stochastic bilevel optimization algorithm for the nonconvex bilevel optimization problem under heavy-tailed noise. Specifically, we develop a normalized stochastic variance-reduced bilevel gradient descent algorithm, which does not rely on any clipping operation. Moreover, we establish its convergence rate by innovatively bounding interdependent gradient sequences under heavy-tailed noise for nonconvex decentralized bilevel optimization problems. As far as we know, this is the first decentralized bilevel optimization algorithm with rigorous theoretical guarantees under heavy-tailed noise. The extensive experimental results confirm the effectiveness of our algorithm in handling heavy-tailed noise.

标题：重尾噪声下非凸分布式随机双层优化
现有分布式随机优化方法假设下层损失函数强凸且随机梯度噪声具有有限方差，这些强假设在现实世界机器学习模型中通常不成立。例如，语言数据学习通常导致梯度呈现重尾特性。为解决此问题，我们开发了一种新型分布式随机双层优化算法，用于处理重尾噪声下的非凸双层优化问题。具体而言，我们提出了一种归一化随机方差缩减双层梯度下降算法，无需任何裁剪操作。此外，我们通过创新性地在重尾噪声下对非凸分布式双层优化问题中的相互依赖梯度序列进行界估计，建立了该算法的收敛速率。据我们所知，这是首个在重尾噪声下具有严格理论保证的分布式双层优化算法。大量实验结果验证了该算法在处理重尾噪声方面的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of existing decentralized stochastic optimization methods under heavy-tailed gradient noise common in real-world machine learning, such as language modeling, this paper proposes a novel decentralized stochastic bilevel optimization algorithm for nonconvex problems. The method, a normalized stochastic variance-reduced bilevel gradient descent, avoids clipping operations and establishes theoretical convergence guarantees by innovatively bounding interdependent gradient sequences under heavy-tailed noise. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness in handling such noise, marking the first decentralized bilevel algorithm with rigorous theoretical guarantees under heavy-tailed conditions.
为应对现有分布式随机优化方法在现实机器学习任务（如语言建模）中常遇的重尾梯度噪声问题，本文提出一种面向非凸双层优化问题的新型分布式随机算法。该方法采用归一化随机方差缩减双层梯度下降，无需裁剪操作，并通过创新性地约束重尾噪声下相互依赖的梯度序列，建立了收敛率理论。实验结果验证了其在重尾噪声下的有效性，是首个在该场景下具备严格理论保证的分布式双层优化算法。
AdvantageFlow: Advantage-Weighted Least Squares for RL in Flow Models
Authors: Branislav Kveton, Anup Rao, Subhojyoti Mukherjee, Krishna Kumar Singh, Viet Dac Lai
First: 2026-05-25T16:32:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:32:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce AdvantageFlow, a forward-process reinforcement learning algorithm for rectified flow models. Unlike Flow-GRPO, which optimizes the reverse process, we optimize an advantage-weighted forward-process prediction loss. This optimization problem is unstable when advantages are negative and the loss becomes non-convex. We stabilize it by rollout policy regularization, which reduces variance and arises from fitting a local reward-improving target distribution. We evaluate AdvantageFlow on image generation tasks with Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium. It outperforms both Flow-GRPO and a state-of-the-art forward-process RL baseline based on negative-aware fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdvantageFlow：用于流模型的基于优势加权最小二乘的强化学习

To stabilize training when advantages are negative and the loss becomes non-convex, they introduce rollout policy regularization, which reduces variance by fitting a local reward-improving target distribution. Experimental results on image generation with Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium show that AdvantageFlow outperforms both Flow-GRPO and a state-of-the-art forward-process RL baseline using negative-aware fine-tuning.
为了解决流模型中逆向过程优化不稳定的问题，作者提出了AdvantageFlow，一种基于优势加权最小二乘法的前向过程强化学习算法，用于训练修正流模型。为缓解负优势导致的非凸损失问题，引入了 rollout 政策正则化，通过拟合局部奖励改进的目标分布来降低方差。在 Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 上的图像生成实验表明，AdvantageFlow 在性能上优于 Flow-GRPO 和当前最先进的前向过程 RL 基线（基于负优势微调）
Train-Free Segmentation in MRI with Cubical Persistent Homology
Authors: Anton François, Raphaël Tinarrage
Venue: Journal of Mathematical Imaging and Vision 68, 20 (2026)
First: 2024-01-02T11:43:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:31:45+00:00
Comments: Similar to the published version. 22 pages, 11 figures, 3 tables. For associated code, see https://github.com/antonfrancois/gliomaSegmentation_TDA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We investigate a framework for train-free MRI segmentation based on Topological Data Analysis. The pipeline proceeds in three steps, first identifying the whole object to segment via automatic thresholding, then detecting a distinctive subset whose topology is known in advance, and finally deducing the various components of the segmentation. A key ingredient is the extraction of approximate representative cycles from persistence diagrams, which provides an interpretable link between persistent features and anatomical components. To clarify the method's scope, we make the underlying topological and intensity assumptions explicit, quantify when they hold on real data, and analyze typical failure modes. We evaluate the approach on glioblastoma and on fetal cortical plate segmentation, with comparisons to unsupervised and deep-learning references. By operating without large annotated datasets, the method is well suited to scarce-data settings and provides an interpretable baseline and practical initialization for expert refinement or learning-based pipelines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于立方体持久同调的无训练MRI分割
我们研究了一种基于拓扑数据分析的无训练MRI分割框架。该流程分为三步：首先通过自动阈值识别待分割的整体对象；然后检测一个拓扑结构已知的显著子集；最后推断分割的各个组成部分。关键步骤是从持久图中提取近似代表性环，从而建立持久特征与解剖结构之间的可解释联系。为明确方法适用范围，我们明确说明了其底层拓扑与强度假设，量化其在真实数据中的适用性，并分析典型失效模式。我们在胶质母细胞瘤和胎儿皮层板分割任务中评估该方法，并与无监督和深度学习方法进行比较。由于无需大量标注数据，该方法适用于数据稀缺场景，可作为专家优化或学习型流程的可解释基线和实用初始化。

Motivated by the need for segmentation methods that avoid large annotated datasets in MRI, this work proposes a train-free approach using Cubical Persistent Homology from Topological Data Analysis. The method first segments the whole object via automatic thresholding, then identifies a topologically known subset, and finally infers segmentation components using approximate representative cycles extracted from persistence diagrams.
