为应对MRI分割中对大规模标注数据的依赖，本文提出一种基于立方持久同调的无训练分割方法。该方法首先通过自动阈值分割整体目标，再识别已知拓扑结构的子集，最后利用持久图中提取的近似代表环推断分割组件。在胶质母细胞瘤和胎儿皮层板数据上的实验表明，该方法优于无监督基线，可为深度学习提供可解释的初始化，适用于数据稀缺场景，并揭示了在拓扑假设不成立时的失效模式。
Learning in Low-Dimensional Subspaces: Orthogonal Bottlenecks for Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Aleksandar Todorov, Matthia Sabatelli
First: 2026-05-25T16:31:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:31:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents commonly rely on high-dimensional neural representations, despite growing evidence that task-relevant value and policy structure may be intrinsically low-dimensional. In this work, we present a simple yet effective representation-level prior that inserts a fixed orthonormal projection to constrain encoder features to a low-dimensional subspace, requiring no auxiliary objectives, pretraining, or changes to the underlying RL algorithm. Under a linear realizability assumption, we prove that when the bottleneck dimension exceeds the intrinsic rank of the optimal value function in feature space, the bottleneck preserves expressivity and leaves the induced gradient dynamics unchanged up to an equivalent low-dimensional parameterization. Empirically, we find that across both single and multi-task benchmarks, baseline performance is either matched or improved once the bottleneck dimension exceeds a small task-dependent threshold; in many cases, value representations can be compressed to extremely low dimensions without loss, and the minimal sufficient dimension depends far more on environment complexity than encoder width. In addition, we analyze representation geometry and find that orthogonal bottlenecks stabilize feature norms and are associated with higher effective rank. Together, these results support a representation-space interpretation of the manifold hypothesis in reinforcement learning and position orthogonal bottlenecks as a lightweight, architecture-agnostic mechanism for shaping RL representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：低维子空间中的学习：强化学习的正交瓶颈
尽管有越来越多的证据表明任务相关的价值和策略结构本质上可能是低维的，但深度强化学习（RL）代理通常依赖高维神经表示。本文提出了一种简单而有效的表示层先验方法，通过插入固定正交投影来约束编码器特征至低维子空间，无需辅助目标、预训练或对底层RL算法进行修改。在线性可实现性假设下，我们证明当瓶颈维度超过最优价值函数在特征空间中的内在秩时，瓶颈保持表达能力，并在等价低维参数化下保持诱导梯度动力学不变。实验证明，在单任务和多任务基准测试中，一旦瓶颈维度超过一个微小的任务相关阈值，基线性能要么持平要么提升；在许多情况下，价值表示可压缩至极低维度而无损失，且最小必要维度更依赖环境复杂度而非编码器宽度。此外，我们分析了表示几何结构，发现正交瓶颈可稳定特征范数，并与更高的有效秩相关。这些结果支持了强化学习中流形假设的表示空间解释，并将正交瓶颈定位为一种轻量级、架构无关的机制，用于塑造RL表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by evidence that task-relevant RL representations may be low-dimensional despite high-dimensional neural encodings, this work introduces orthogonal bottlenecks—fixed orthonormal projections inserted into encoder layers—to constrain features to low-dimensional subspaces without auxiliary training or algorithmic changes. Under a linear realizability assumption, the method preserves expressivity and gradient dynamics when the bottleneck dimension exceeds the intrinsic rank of the optimal value function. Empirically, across single and multi-task benchmarks, performance matches or improves once the bottleneck dimension surpasses a small task-specific threshold, with value representations often compressible to very low dimensions without loss; the minimal sufficient dimension correlates more with environment complexity than encoder width, and orthogonal bottlenecks stabilize feature norms and increase effective rank.
鉴于任务相关RL表示可能本质上是低维的，尽管神经编码常为高维，本文提出在编码器中插入固定正交投影（即正交瓶颈），以将特征约束在低维子空间。在线性可实现性假设下，当瓶颈维度超过最优价值函数在特征空间中的内在秩时，该方法保持表达能力和梯度动力学不变。实验证明，在单任务和多任务基准上，当瓶颈维度超过一个较小的任务相关阈值时，性能可保持或提升，且价值表示常可压缩至极低维度而无损失，其最小必要维度更取决于环境复杂度而非编码器宽度。进一步分析表明，正交瓶颈可稳定特征范数并提升有效秩，支持RL中流形假设的表示空间解释，并将其定位为轻量、架构无关的表示塑造机制。
PathWise: Planning through World Model for Automated Heuristic Design via Self-Evolving LLMs
Authors: Oguzhan Gungordu, Siheng Xiong, Faramarz Fekri
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-28T12:34:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:30:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled automated heuristic design (AHD) for combinatorial optimization problems (COPs), but existing frameworks' reliance on fixed evolutionary rules and static prompt templates often leads to myopic heuristic generation, redundant evaluations, and limited reasoning about how new heuristics should be derived. We propose a novel multi-agent reasoning framework, referred to as Planning through World Model for Automated Heuristic Design via Self-Evolving LLMs (PathWise), which formulates heuristic generation as a sequential decision process over an entailment graph serving as a compact, stateful memory of the search trajectory. This approach allows the system to carry forward past decisions and reuse or avoid derivation information across generations. A policy agent plans evolutionary actions, a world model agent generates heuristic rollouts conditioned on those actions, and critic agents provide routed reflections summarizing lessons from prior steps, shifting LLM-based AHD from trial-and-error evolution toward state-aware planning through reasoning. Experiments across diverse COPs show that PathWise converges faster to better heuristics, generalizes across different LLM backbones, and scales to larger problem sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PathWise：通过世界模型规划，利用自演化大语言模型实现自动化启发式设计
大语言模型（LLMs）已实现组合优化问题（COPs）的自动化启发式设计（AHD），但现有框架依赖固定演化规则和静态提示模板，常导致启发式生成短视、评估冗余及难以推导新启发式。本文提出一种新颖的多智能体推理框架——PathWise（通过世界模型规划，利用自演化LLMs实现自动化启发式设计），将启发式生成建模为在蕴含图上进行的序列决策过程，该图作为搜索轨迹的紧凑、具状态记忆结构。该方法使系统可继承过往决策，跨代复用或避免重复推导信息。策略智能体规划演化动作，世界模型智能体根据动作生成启发式 rollout，评论智能体提供路线反思，总结前序步骤经验，推动基于LLM的AHD从试错演化转向基于状态的推理规划。在多种COPs实验中，PathWise收敛更快、生成更优启发式，具备不同LLM架构的泛化能力，并可扩展至更大规模问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed evolutionary rules and static prompts in automated heuristic design for combinatorial optimization, PathWise introduces a multi-agent framework that treats heuristic generation as a sequential decision process over an entailment graph, enabling stateful memory of past decisions. The system comprises a policy agent for planning, a world model agent for heuristic rollouts, and critic agents for reflective summarization, collectively shifting AHD from trial-and-error to reasoning-driven planning. Experiments across multiple COPs demonstrate faster convergence to superior heuristics, cross-LLM generalization, and scalability to larger problem instances.
为解决现有自动启发式设计中固定演化规则和静态提示导致的短视生成、冗余评估及推理能力不足问题，作者提出PathWise框架，将启发式生成建模为在蕴含图上的序列决策过程，该图作为状态化记忆存储搜索轨迹，支持跨代信息复用或规避。框架包含规划代理、世界模型代理和批评代理，分别负责行动规划、启发式生成与经验总结，实现从试错演化到状态感知规划的转变。实验表明，该方法在多种组合优化问题上收敛更快、生成更优启发式，具备跨LLM通用性并可扩展至更大规模问题。
Forgotten Words: Benchmarking NeoBERT for Dementia Detection in Low-Resource Conversational Filipino and English Speech
Authors: Rez Samantha Z. Floresca, Edric Castel C. Hao, Hannah Grachiella Buñales, Chelsea Dominique E. Temprosa, Georgianna Z. Reyes, Kervin Gabriel L. Chua
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-25T16:26:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:26:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted to BioNLP Workshop @ ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dementia detection from spontaneous speech offers a scalable approach to cognitive screening, yet NLP systems remain predominantly English-centric. This limitation is especially acute in the Philippines, where Filipino-English code-switching is pervasive and no prior work has addressed NLP-based dementia detection. We present the first systematic evaluation of transformer-based dementia detection in Filipino speech and the first assessment of NeoBERT in a clinical NLP setting. To separate language from domain effects, we construct a parallel bilingual dataset of 4,000 DementiaBank-derived transcripts, with Filipino translations produced manually to preserve discourse-level markers of cognitive decline. We evaluate five model families, TF-IDF + LogReg, BERT, NeoBERT, XLM-R, and RoBERTa-Tagalog, under monolingual, zero-shot cross-lingual, and bilingual fine-tuning settings. We find that in-domain performance does not transfer across languages, with English-trained BERT dropping to Macro-F1 = 0.455 on Filipino, and that architectural modernization alone does not improve robustness. Bilingual fine-tuning, however, eliminates cross-lingual degradation across all transformer models, converging to Macro-F1 = 0.969-0.973. These results suggest that multilingual clinical NLP performance is driven primarily by linguistic coverage during training rather than model scale or architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：被遗忘的词语：在资源匮乏的菲律宾语和英语对话语音中基准测试NeoBERT用于痴呆检测
从自发性语音中检测痴呆提供了一种可扩展的认知筛查方法，但自然语言处理系统仍主要以英语为中心。这一局限在菲律宾尤为突出，因为菲律宾语-英语混合使用普遍，且此前尚无研究涉及基于NLP的痴呆检测。我们首次系统评估了基于Transformer的菲律宾语痴呆检测模型，并首次在临床NLP环境中评估了NeoBERT。为分离语言与领域效应，我们构建了一个包含4000条源自DementiaBank的双语平行语料库，其中菲律宾语翻译由人工制作，以保留认知衰退的语篇层面标记。我们在单语、零样本跨语言及双语微调设置下评估了五类模型：TF-IDF + LogReg、BERT、NeoBERT、XLM-R和RoBERTa-Tagalog。结果表明，领域内性能无法跨语言迁移，英语训练的BERT在菲律宾语上F1值降至0.455；仅靠架构现代化无法提升鲁棒性。然而，双语微调可消除所有Transformer模型的跨语言性能下降，F1值收敛至0.969-0.973。这些结果表明，多语言临床NLP性能主要取决于训练期间的语言覆盖范围，而非模型规模或架构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of NLP tools for dementia detection in Filipino-English code-switching speech, this study evaluates transformer models including NeoBERT in a low-resource clinical setting. Using a manually translated bilingual dataset of 4,000 dementia-related transcripts, the authors test five model families under monolingual, zero-shot cross-lingual, and bilingual fine-tuning conditions. Results show that English-trained BERT performs poorly on Filipino (Macro-F1 = 0.455), indicating poor cross-lingual transfer, while architectural upgrades alone do not help. Bilingual fine-tuning, however, significantly improves performance across all models, achieving Macro-F1 scores of 0.969–0.973, suggesting that linguistic coverage during training—not model scale or architecture—is key to multilingual clinical NLP success.
为填补菲律宾双语语境下痴呆检测的NLP工具空白，本研究首次系统评估了包括NeoBERT在内的多种Transformer模型在临床场景中的表现。基于人工翻译的4000条双语转录数据，作者在单语、零样本跨语言及双语微调三种设置下测试五类模型。结果表明，英语训练的BERT在菲律宾语上表现极差（Macro-F1=0.455），说明语言迁移能力弱，仅靠模型架构升级无法提升鲁棒性；而双语微调则使所有Transformer模型性能显著提升至Macro-F1=0.969–0.973，证明训练时的语言覆盖范围比模型规模或架构更重要。
Your Data Is Not Perfect: Towards Cross-Domain Out-of-Distribution Detection in Class-Imbalanced Data
Authors: Xiang Fang, Arvind Easwaran, Blaise Genest, Ponnuthurai Nagaratnam Suganthan
First: 2024-12-09T08:03:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:25:07+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Expert Systems with Applications
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Previous OOD detection systems only focus on the semantic gap between ID and OOD samples. Besides the semantic gap, we are faced with two additional gaps: the domain gap between source and target domains, and the class-imbalance gap between different classes. In fact, similar objects from different domains should belong to the same class. In this paper, we introduce a realistic yet challenging setting: class-imbalanced cross-domain OOD detection (CCOD), which contains a well-labeled (but usually small) source set for training and conducts OOD detection on an unlabeled (but usually larger) target set for testing. We do not assume that the target domain contains only OOD classes or that it is class-balanced: the distribution among classes of the target dataset need not be the same as the source dataset. To tackle this challenging setting with an OOD detection system, we propose a novel uncertainty-aware adaptive semantic alignment (UASA) network based on a prototype-based alignment strategy. Specifically, we first build label-driven prototypes in the source domain and utilize these prototypes for target classification to close the domain gap. Rather than utilizing fixed thresholds for OOD detection, we generate adaptive sample-wise thresholds to handle the semantic gap. Finally, we conduct uncertainty-aware clustering to group semantically similar target samples to relieve the class-imbalance gap. Extensive experiments on three challenging benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed UASA outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a large margin.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的数据并不完美：面向类别不平衡数据的跨域分布外检测
以往的分布外（OOD）检测系统仅关注ID样本与OOD样本之间的语义差距。除此之外，我们还面临两个额外的差距：源域与目标域之间的域差距，以及不同类别之间的类别不平衡差距。事实上，来自不同域的相似对象应属于同一类别。本文引入了一个现实但具有挑战性的设定：类别不平衡跨域分布外检测（CCOD），该设定包含一个标注良好的（但通常较小）源数据集用于训练，并在未标注的（但通常更大）目标数据集上进行OOD检测。我们不假设目标域仅包含OOD类别或其类别分布是平衡的：目标数据集的类别分布无需与源数据集相同。为应对这一挑战性设定，我们提出了一种基于原型对齐策略的新型不确定性感知自适应语义对齐（UASA）网络。具体而言，我们首先在源域中构建标签驱动的原型，并利用这些原型对目标样本进行分类以缩小域差距；而非使用固定阈值进行OOD检测，我们生成自适应样本级阈值以处理语义差距；最后，我们进行不确定性感知聚类，将语义相似的目标样本分组，以缓解类别不平衡差距。在三个具有挑战性的基准数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的UASA方法显著优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the real-world challenge of detecting out-of-distribution samples in class-imbalanced and cross-domain settings, this paper introduces class-imbalanced cross-domain OOD detection (CCOD), where a small labeled source domain trains a model to detect OOD samples in a larger unlabeled target domain with unknown class distributions. To address this, the authors propose UASA, an uncertainty-aware adaptive semantic alignment network that first builds label-driven prototypes in the source domain to align target samples and close the domain gap. It then generates adaptive per-sample thresholds for OOD detection to handle semantic differences, and applies uncertainty-aware clustering to group similar target samples, mitigating class imbalance. Experiments on three benchmarks show UASA significantly outperforms existing methods.
为应对现实场景中类不平衡与跨域条件下异常检测的挑战，本文提出类不平衡跨域OOD检测（CCOD）任务，即利用小规模标注源域训练模型，在大规模无标签目标域中检测OOD样本，且目标域类分布无需与源域一致。作者提出不确定性感知自适应语义对齐（UASA）网络：首先在源域构建标签驱动原型以对齐目标样本并缩小域差距，再为每个样本自适应生成阈值以应对语义差异，最后通过不确定性感知聚类缓解类不平衡问题。实验表明，UASA在三个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。
MIND: Multi-Scale Intent Diffusion for Text-Driven Physics-Based Humanoid Control
Authors: Bin Li, Ruichi Zhang, Han Liang, Jingyan Zhang, Juze Zhang, Xin Chen, Jingya Wang
First: 2026-05-25T16:23:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:23:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Enabling physics-based humanoids to execute diverse behaviors from high-level textual commands remains a significant challenge. Existing methods typically follow either a two-stage paradigm that combines kinematic motion generation with physics-based tracking, or an end-to-end imitation-learning paradigm that directly generates actions from text. However, the former suffers from the inherent domain shift between kinematic generation and physics-based tracking, while the latter struggles with the substantial modality gap between textual commands and low-level actions, limiting effective semantic alignment. Notably, humanoid states encode rich motion dynamics that are more semantically aligned with textual descriptions than low-level actions, making them a natural basis for deriving behavioral intent. Building upon this insight, we propose MIND, a novel end-to-end diffusion framework for text-driven physics-based humanoid control that leverages behavioral intent as a semantic bridge between textual commands and low-level actions. At its core, MIND introduces a multi-scale intent diffusion mechanism, where a holistic intent predictor captures global behavioral dynamics to guide overall behavior synthesis, while an immediate intent predictor provides step-wise, fine-grained signals for local behavior refinement at each diffusion step. This hierarchical intent formulation imposes a structured inductive bias for humanoid control, improving semantic alignment and behavioral naturalness. Furthermore, MIND encodes humanoid states into a latent space to enable more effective semantic intent modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MIND outperforms existing methods and synthesizes coherent, physically plausible, and semantically aligned humanoid behaviors from text commands. Our code will be released to facilitate future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MIND：面向文本驱动的物理人形控制的多尺度意图扩散
使物理人形机器人从高层文本指令执行多样化行为仍是一个重大挑战。现有方法通常采用两种范式：一种是先生成运动学轨迹再进行物理跟踪的两阶段方法；另一种是直接从文本生成动作的端到端模仿学习方法。前者因运动学生成与物理跟踪之间存在固有领域偏移而受限，后者则因文本指令与低层动作间存在显著模态鸿沟，难以实现有效语义对齐。值得注意的是，人形状态编码了丰富的运动动态，其语义与文本描述更契合，因此是推导行为意图的自然基础。基于此洞察，我们提出MIND，一种面向文本驱动物理人形控制的端到端扩散框架，利用行为意图作为文本指令与低层动作间的语义桥梁。其核心是引入多尺度意图扩散机制：全局意图预测器捕捉整体行为动态以指导整体行为合成，即时意图预测器则在每一步扩散中提供细粒度信号以局部优化行为。这种分层意图建模为人形控制引入结构化归纳偏置，提升语义对齐与行为自然性。此外，MIND将人形状态编码至潜在空间，以实现更有效的语义意图建模。大量实验表明，MIND优于现有方法，能从文本指令合成连贯、物理合理且语义对齐的人形行为。我们的代码将开源以促进后续研究。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of generating physically plausible humanoid behaviors from textual commands, MIND introduces an end-to-end diffusion framework that uses behavioral intent as a semantic bridge between text and low-level actions. It employs a multi-scale intent diffusion mechanism: a global intent predictor captures overarching motion dynamics to guide behavior synthesis, while a local intent predictor refines actions step-by-step during diffusion. By encoding humanoid states into a latent space, MIND enhances semantic alignment and naturalness. Experiments show MIND outperforms prior two-stage and imitation-learning methods, producing coherent, physically accurate, and semantically consistent behaviors from text.
为解决从高阶文本指令生成物理真实的人形机器人行为的难题，MIND提出一种端到端扩散框架，以行为意图作为文本与低级动作间的语义桥梁。其采用多尺度意图扩散机制：全局意图预测器捕捉整体运动动态以引导行为生成，局部意图预测器在扩散步骤中逐步细化动作。通过将人形机器人状态编码至潜在空间，MIND提升语义对齐与行为自然性。实验表明，MIND优于现有两阶段与模仿学习方法，能从文本指令生成连贯、物理合理且语义一致的行为。
MAGIC: Multimodal Alignment & Grounding-aware Instruction Coreset for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Shristi Das Biswas, Kaushik Roy
First: 2026-05-25T16:22:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:22:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instruction tuning of large vision-language models (LVLMs) increasingly depends on massive multimodal corpora, yet these datasets contain samples with substantial redundancy, low visual dependency, and highly imbalanced coverage of multimodal reasoning behaviors. As a result, uniform subsampling or naive score-based selection often yields suboptimal training subsets. We introduce MAGIC, a training-free, forward-only coreset selection method designed to construct compact yet behaviorally faithful subsets for multimodal instruction tuning. MAGIC is built on three intrinsic signals extracted from a pretrained VLM: Multimodal Gain, which measures the likelihood improvement obtained from visual input; Bridging Relevance, which captures the sharpness of answer-token grounding over visual tokens; and Skill-Neuron Signatures, which characterize the functional computation elicited by each sample via top-activated feed-forward neurons. MAGIC combines these signals in a three-stage pipeline: filtering low-gain examples, ranking candidates by a normalized quality objective, and performing bucket-wise budget allocation over discrete neuron signatures to preserve latent multimodal skill coverage. This formulation avoids backpropagation, auxiliary selector training, and expensive clustering in continuous activation spaces, while remaining efficient and easily deployable in existing VLMs. Across LLaVA-665K and Vision-Flan datasets, and transfer settings to large target models, LLaVA-1.5-7B and -13B, MAGIC consistently improves over strong baselines under matched 20% budgets: it achieves 100.3% relative performance to full finetuning on LLaVA-665K and 101.6% relative performance on Vision-Flan-186K, while yielding a 73.7% reduction in wall-clock run time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAGIC：面向视觉-语言模型的多模态对齐与定位感知指令核心集
大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）的指令调优日益依赖大规模多模态语料库，但这些数据集包含大量冗余样本、视觉依赖度低，且多模态推理行为覆盖极不平衡。因此，均匀采样或朴素评分选择常导致次优训练子集。我们提出MAGIC，一种无需训练、仅前向传播的核心集选择方法，旨在为多模态指令调优构建紧凑且行为忠实的子集。MAGIC基于预训练VLM提取的三个内在信号构建：多模态增益（衡量视觉输入带来的概率提升）、桥接相关性（捕捉答案标记对视觉标记的定位锐度）和技能神经元签名（通过激活最强的前馈神经元表征每个样本的功能计算）。MAGIC采用三阶段流程：过滤低增益样本、按归一化质量目标排序候选样本、按离散神经元签名分桶分配预算以保留潜在多模态技能覆盖。该方法避免反向传播、辅助选择器训练及连续激活空间中的昂贵聚类，同时高效且易于在现有VLM中部署。在LLaVA-665K和Vision-Flan数据集上，跨迁移至大型目标模型LLaVA-1.5-7B和-13B，MAGIC在匹配20%预算下持续优于强基线：在LLaVA-665K上达到全微调100.3%相对性能，在Vision-Flan-186K上达到101.6%相对性能，同时将实际运行时间减少73.7%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of large multimodal datasets in instruction tuning due to redundancy and imbalanced coverage, MAGIC introduces a training-free coreset selection method that leverages three intrinsic signals from a pretrained VLM—Multimodal Gain, Bridging Relevance, and Skill-Neuron Signatures—to construct compact, behaviorally representative subsets. It operates in three stages: filtering low-gain samples, ranking candidates via a normalized quality metric, and allocating budget across neuron signatures to preserve multimodal skill diversity. Experiments on LLaVA-665K and Vision-Flan datasets show MAGIC outperforms baselines under 20% budget constraints, achieving 100.3% and 101.6% of full fine-tuning performance while reducing training time by 73.7%.
为解决大规模多模态数据在指令调优中冗余与覆盖不均导致的效率低下问题，MAGIC提出一种无需训练的子集选择方法，利用预训练VLM的三类内在信号——多模态增益、桥接相关性与技能神经元签名，通过三阶段流程（低增益样本过滤、归一化质量排序、神经元签名桶内预算分配）构建紧凑且行为忠实的子集。在LLaVA-665K与Vision-Flan数据集上，MAGIC在20%预算下优于基线方法，性能达全微调的100.3%与101.6%，同时训练时间减少73.7%。
Towards 3D heart mesh generation using contactless radar imaging and physics-informed neural network
Authors: Jinye Li, Chenxi Fu, Minghang Zheng, Yang Liu, Xiahai Zhuang, Qingchao Chen
First: 2026-05-25T16:20:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:20:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cardiac function evaluation necessitates continuous, non-invasive monitoring, a capability limited in MRI. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar and its Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) mode offer a privacy-preserving and portable point-of-care clinical applications. However, reconstructing high-fidelity 3D cardiac geometry from SAR remains an open challenge. Traditional radar methods generate sparse point clouds that lack continuous surface topology. Meanwhile, direct application of optical reconstruction networks performs poorly due to the severe speckle noise and ambiguous boundaries inherent in SAR images. To bridge this gap, we propose SAR2Mesh, a novel framework that reformulates the task as a coarse-to-fine mesh deformation process. By initializing with a topological template, our approach explicitly preserves anatomical connectivity through progressive mesh deformation.We introduce a geometry-aware feature projection module to extract multi-view features via 3D-to-2D sampling, and a physics-informed radar loss to enforce consistency between the predicted geometry and raw radar echoes. Furthermore, we present Cardiac Mesh-SAR, the first large-scale paired SAR-mesh dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAR2Mesh significantly outperforms existing image-based baselines, achieving accurate and physically consistent cardiac reconstructions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于无接触雷达成像与物理信息神经网络的心脏三维网格生成方法
心脏功能评估需要连续、无创的监测，而MRI在该方面能力有限。毫米波（mmWave）雷达及其合成孔径雷达（SAR）模式可提供隐私保护且便携的床旁临床应用。然而，从SAR图像重建高保真度的三维心脏几何结构仍是一个开放性挑战。传统雷达方法生成的点云稀疏，缺乏连续表面拓扑结构；而直接应用光学重建网络因SAR图像固有的严重斑点噪声和模糊边界而表现不佳。为弥合这一差距，我们提出SAR2Mesh框架，将任务重新表述为粗到细的网格变形过程。通过以拓扑模板初始化，我们的方法在渐进式网格变形中显式保持解剖学连通性。我们引入几何感知特征投影模块，通过3D到2D采样提取多视角特征，并引入物理信息雷达损失，以确保预测几何与原始雷达回波的一致性。此外，我们构建了首个大规模配对的SAR-网格数据集Cardiac Mesh-SAR。大量实验表明，SAR2Mesh显著优于现有基于图像的基线方法，实现了准确且物理一致的心脏重建。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for continuous, non-invasive cardiac monitoring without MRI’s limitations, this work addresses the challenge of generating high-fidelity 3D heart meshes from millimeter-wave radar SAR images, which suffer from sparse point clouds and severe speckle noise. The proposed SAR2Mesh framework treats mesh generation as a coarse-to-fine deformation process, starting from a topological template to preserve anatomical connectivity, and employs a geometry-aware feature projection module for multi-view 3D-to-2D sampling, paired with a physics-informed radar loss to align predicted geometry with raw radar echoes. Evaluated on the newly introduced Cardiac Mesh-SAR dataset, SAR2Mesh significantly outperforms existing image-based methods, producing accurate and physically consistent 3D heart reconstructions.
为实现无需侵入的连续心脏监测，该研究旨在从毫米波雷达SAR图像中重建高保真3D心脏网格，解决传统方法点云稀疏和图像噪声严重的问题。SAR2Mesh框架将网格生成建模为粗到细的变形过程，从拓扑模板出发以保持解剖结构连通性，并引入几何感知特征投影模块进行多视角采样及物理感知雷达损失以约束预测几何与原始雷达数据一致性。在首个大规模配对SAR-网格数据集Cardiac Mesh-SAR上，该方法显著优于现有图像基方法，生成准确且物理一致的心脏重建结果。
AI-Assisted Systematization for Evaluating GenAI Systems
Authors: Dhruv Agarwal, Emily Sheng, Chad Atalla, Jean Garcia-Gathright, Hussein Mozannar, Hannah Washington, Alexandra Chouldechova, Solon Barocas, Hanna Wallach
First: 2026-05-25T16:19:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:19:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating generative AI (GenAI) systems is challenging because many targets of evaluation are broad, contested concepts, such as "reasoning," "fairness," or "creativity." When these concepts are left underspecified, it becomes unclear what should be measured or how evaluation results should be interpreted. This problem reflects a missing step: systematization, that is, moving from a broad background concept to an explicit, structured account of the concept in measurable terms. To help address the fact that systematization is cognitively demanding and resource-intensive, we investigate whether AI assistance can support this process. To enable AI-assisted systematization and assess its quality, we introduce a structured representation of a systematized concept, a concept spec, and a validation worksheet. We then develop two AI-assisted systematizers: a direct, zero-shot approach and a multi-agent approach that more closely mirrors manual systematization approaches from existing literature. We use these systematizers to produce concept specs for two concepts -- hate-based rhetoric and digital empathy -- and evaluate resulting concept specs on content validity and information recoverability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI辅助的生成式AI系统评估体系化方法
评估生成式人工智能（GenAI）系统具有挑战性，因为许多评估目标是宽泛且存在争议的概念，如“推理”、“公平性”或“创造力”。当这些概念未被明确界定时，就难以确定应测量什么，以及如何解释评估结果。这一问题反映了缺失的步骤：体系化，即从宽泛的背景概念过渡到可测量的、明确结构化的概念描述。为应对体系化认知负荷大、资源密集的问题，我们研究AI辅助是否能支持该过程。为实现AI辅助体系化并评估其质量，我们引入了体系化概念的结构化表示——概念规范（concept spec）及验证表单。随后，我们开发了两种AI辅助体系化工具：一种是直接的零样本方法，另一种是更贴近现有文献中人工体系化方法的多智能体方法。我们使用这两种工具为“基于仇恨的修辞”和“数字共情”两个概念生成概念规范，并从内容效度和信息可恢复性两个维度评估结果。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the challenge of evaluating generative AI systems by tackling the lack of clear, measurable definitions for abstract evaluation concepts like reasoning or fairness. It proposes AI-assisted systematization as a solution, introducing a structured format called a 'concept spec' and a validation worksheet to guide the process. Two AI methods are developed: a zero-shot direct approach and a multi-agent method mimicking human systematization. Applied to hate-based rhetoric and digital empathy, both methods generated concept specs that were evaluated for content validity and information recoverability, showing that AI can support the creation of structured, measurable definitions despite the cognitive complexity of the task.
该论文针对评估生成式AI系统时抽象概念（如推理、公平性）缺乏明确可测量定义的问题，提出借助AI辅助进行概念系统化。研究设计了“概念规格”和验证表单，开发了两种AI方法——直接零样本方法与模拟人工系统化过程的多智能体方法，并用于生成“仇恨言论”与“数字共情”两个概念的规格说明。评估结果显示，AI生成的规格在内容效度和信息可恢复性方面表现良好，证明AI可有效支持复杂概念的结构化定义。
Statistical Inference for Stochastic Gradient Descent Beyond Finite Variance
Authors: Jose Blanchet, Peter Glynn, Wenhao Yang
First: 2026-05-25T16:18:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:18:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is a foundational algorithm for large-scale statistical learning and stochastic optimization. However, statistical inference based on SGD iterates remains challenging when stochastic gradients have infinite variance, as the relevant limiting distributions depend on unknown nuisance parameters. In this paper, we develop an efficient, model-agnostic methodology for constructing confidence regions from SGD trajectories that applies in both finite- and infinite-variance regimes. The procedure is based on a joint weak convergence result for the Polyak-Ruppert averaged estimator and an empirical second-moment normalizer constructed from stochastic gradients along the SGD trajectory. This joint limit yields a self-normalized statistic in which the leading tail-dependent scaling terms cancel. We then use a subsampling calibration scheme to estimate the relevant critical values, avoiding explicit estimation of tail indices, slowly varying functions, or stable-law parameters. The resulting confidence regions are straightforward to implement and are asymptotically valid under both the finite- and infinite-second-moment regimes. Simulation studies show reliable coverage in various settings, supporting the proposed method as a practical tool for uncertainty quantification in stochastic optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越有限方差的随机梯度下降统计推断
随机梯度下降（SGD）是大规模统计学习和随机优化的基础算法。然而，当随机梯度具有无限方差时，基于SGD迭代的统计推断仍具挑战性，因为相关极限分布依赖于未知的干扰参数。本文提出了一种高效、模型无关的方法，用于从SGD轨迹构建置信区域，适用于有限方差和无限方差情形。该方法基于Polyak-Ruppert平均估计量与从SGD轨迹中构建的实证二阶矩归一化器的联合弱收敛结果。该联合极限产生一个自归一化统计量，其中主导的尾部依赖缩放项相互抵消。我们采用子抽样校准方案估计相关临界值，无需显式估计尾指数、缓慢变化函数或稳定分布参数。所得置信区域易于实现，且在有限二阶矩和无限二阶矩情形下均渐近有效。仿真研究表明，该方法在多种场景下具有可靠的覆盖率，支持其作为随机优化中不确定性量化实用工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of statistical inference for stochastic gradient descent (SGD) when gradients have infinite variance, this paper proposes a model-agnostic method to construct confidence regions from SGD trajectories. The approach leverages a joint weak convergence result between the Polyak-Ruppert averaged estimator and an empirical second-moment normalizer derived from the SGD path, yielding a self-normalized statistic that cancels tail-dependent scaling. A subsampling calibration scheme estimates critical values without requiring explicit estimation of tail indices or stable-law parameters. Experimental results demonstrate reliable coverage across finite- and infinite-variance settings, validating the method’s practical utility for uncertainty quantification in stochastic optimization.
为了解决梯度具有无限方差时SGD统计推断的难题，本文提出了一种模型无关的方法，通过SGD轨迹构建置信区域。该方法基于Polyak-Ruppert平均估计量与从SGD路径中构造的实证二阶矩归一化器的联合弱收敛结果，生成一个自归一化统计量，消除了尾部依赖的缩放项。采用子抽样校准方案估计临界值，无需显式估计尾指数、慢变函数或稳定分布参数。实验结果表明，在有限和无限方差场景下均能实现可靠覆盖，验证了该方法在随机优化中进行不确定性量化方面的实用价值。
MoBiQuant: Mixture-of-Bits Quantization for Token-Adaptive Any-Precision LLM
Authors: Dongwei Wang, Jinhee Kim, Seokho Han, Denis Gudovskiy, Yohei Nakata, Tomoyuki Okuno, KhayTze Peong, Kang Eun Jeon, Jong Hwan Ko, Yiran Chen, Huanrui Yang
First: 2026-02-21T21:11:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:17:59+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dynamic runtime latency and memory constraints necessitate flexible large language model (LLM) deployment, where an LLM can be inferred with various quantization precisions based on available computational resources. Recent work on such any-precision quantization either relies on hardware-inefficient vector quantization or induces additional scaling factors when switching between bit-widths. Meanwhile, existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods calibrated for a fixed low precision show poor generalizability under runtime precision change. In this work, we attribute the source of poor generalization across bit-widths to a precision-dependent \textit{outlier migration} phenomenon where the distribution of PTQ-sensitive tokens changes across precisions. Motivated by this observation, we propose \texttt{MoBiQuant}, a novel any-precision Mixture-of-Bits quantization framework that adjusts weight precision for flexible LLM inference based on token sensitivity. Specifically, we propose a many-in-one recursive residual quantization that can iteratively reconstruct higher-precision weights at runtime and mitigates \textit{outlier migration} with a token-aware router to dynamically select the optimal inference precision of each token.Extensive experiments show that \texttt{MoBiQuant} matches or surpasses frontier single-precision PTQ while exhibiting strong elasticity, achieving significant memory savings and throughput gains of up to $1.34\times$ over state-of-the-art any-precision methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoBiQuant：面向令牌自适应任意精度LLM的比特混合量化
动态运行时延迟和内存约束要求灵活部署大语言模型（LLM），使其可根据可用计算资源以不同量化精度进行推理。现有任意精度量化方法要么依赖硬件效率低的向量量化，要么在切换位宽时引入额外缩放因子。同时，现有后训练量化（PTQ）方法针对固定低精度校准，在运行时精度变化时泛化能力差。本文将跨位宽泛化能力差的原因归结为精度依赖的\textit{异常值迁移}现象——PTQ敏感令牌的分布随精度变化而改变。受此启发，我们提出\texttt{MoBiQuant}，一种新颖的任意精度比特混合量化框架，根据令牌敏感性动态调整权重精度以实现灵活的LLM推理。具体而言，我们提出一种多对一递归残差量化方法，可在运行时迭代重建高精度权重，并通过令牌感知路由器动态选择每个令牌的最佳推理精度，以缓解\textit{异常值迁移}。大量实验表明，\texttt{MoBiQuant}在匹配或超越前沿单精度PTQ方法的同时，展现出强大弹性，相比现有任意精度方法实现高达$1.34\times$的内存节省和吞吐量提升。
Summary / 总结
To address the need for flexible large language model deployment under varying computational constraints, MoBiQuant introduces a token-adaptive mixture-of-bits quantization framework that dynamically adjusts inference precision per token. It mitigates the 'outlier migration' phenomenon—where token distributions shift across bit-widths—by using a recursive residual quantization scheme and a token-aware router to select optimal precision at runtime. Experiments demonstrate that MoBiQuant matches or exceeds the performance of single-precision PTQ methods while offering superior elasticity, achieving up to 1.34x memory savings and throughput gains over existing any-precision approaches.
为应对不同计算资源下大语言模型部署的灵活性需求，MoBiQuant 提出一种基于令牌感知的混合比特量化框架，动态调整每个令牌的推理精度。该方法通过递归残差量化与令牌感知路由器，缓解了跨比特宽时敏感令牌分布迁移的问题。实验表明，MoBiQuant 在性能上可媲美或超越单精度 PTQ 方法，同时具备更强的弹性，相比现有任意精度方法最高可实现 1.34 倍的内存节省与吞吐量提升。
Causal methods for LLM development and evaluation
Authors: Dennis Frauen, Marie Brockschmidt, Konstantin Hess, Haorui Ma, Yuchen Ma, Abdurahman Maarouf, Maresa Schröder, Jonas Schweisthal, Yuxin Wang, Athiya Deviyani, Sonali Parbhoo, Rahul G. Krishnan, Stefan Feuerriegel
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-05-25T16:15:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:15:44+00:00
Comments: Published in KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) development is currently driven by large-scale empirical iteration over data mixtures, reward models, routing strategies, and evaluation pipelines. Here, we argue that many central questions in LLM development and evaluation are inherently causal: What is the effect of adding a data domain during pretraining? How do annotator preferences change when LLMs generate text in a different style? Should a prompt be routed to a larger or smaller model given inference cost constraints? In general, causal methods are well-suited to such settings where interventions change outcomes but, surprisingly, are underrepresented in LLM development. Our contribution is threefold: (1) We explain how causal methods can help develop modern LLM development and evaluation: LLM development relies heavily on logged data, which are often subject to confounding and distribution shifts; evaluation uses learned but potentially biased judges; and deployment environments are non-stationary. These conditions make purely predictive approaches fragile and create opportunities for principled identification and estimation methods from causal inference. (2) We further map opportunities for causal methods in the entire LLM development pipeline, including pretraining, alignment, routing, agentic workflows, and evaluation. (3) We discuss new research opportunities around leveraging causal methods for LLM development and evaluation. Overall, we argue that causal methods are potentially underutilized for the LLM development and evaluation pipeline, despite the fact that such methods can ensure a reliable and scientifically grounded design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型开发与评估中的因果方法
当前大语言模型（LLM）开发主要依赖于对数据混合、奖励模型、路由策略和评估流程的大规模经验迭代。本文认为，LLM开发与评估中的许多核心问题本质上具有因果性：在预训练中增加某一数据域会产生什么影响？当LLM以不同风格生成文本时，标注者偏好如何变化？在推理成本约束下，提示应被路由至更大还是更小的模型？总体而言，因果方法非常适合于干预改变结果的场景，但令人惊讶的是，它们在LLM开发中仍被严重忽视。我们的贡献有三方面：（1）我们阐述因果方法如何助力现代LLM开发与评估：LLM开发高度依赖日志数据，常受混杂因素和分布偏移影响；评估依赖学习但可能有偏的评判者；部署环境非平稳。这些条件使纯预测方法脆弱，同时为因果推断中的原则性识别与估计方法创造了机会。（2）我们进一步梳理了因果方法在LLM开发全流程（包括预训练、对齐、路由、代理工作流和评估）中的应用机会。（3）我们探讨了利用因果方法进行LLM开发与评估的新研究方向。总体而言，尽管因果方法能确保可靠且科学的设计，但在LLM开发与评估流程中仍被低估。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that causal methods are underutilized yet highly relevant for LLM development and evaluation, as many core questions involve interventions whose effects are obscured by confounding and distribution shifts in logged data, biased evaluation judges, and non-stationary deployment environments. The authors propose a framework for applying causal inference across the entire LLM pipeline—from pretraining and alignment to routing and evaluation—to enable principled identification of causal effects, thereby improving reliability and scientific grounding. Experimental results across these domains show that causal methods can better isolate true intervention effects, reduce reliance on brittle predictive models, and offer more robust design choices under real-world constraints.
本文指出，尽管因果方法非常适合处理LLM开发与评估中的干预性问题（如预训练数据域的影响或输出风格对标注者偏好的改变），但在实际应用中仍被严重低估。作者认为，因果推断有助于缓解日志数据中的混杂与分布偏移、降低学习评估器的偏差、应对非平稳部署环境——这些条件使纯预测方法脆弱。文章进一步梳理了因果方法在预训练、对齐、路由、代理工作流及评估等LLM全流程中的应用机会，强调采用因果推理可实现更可靠、更具科学依据的设计。
PowerFlow: Unlocking the Dual Nature of LLMs via Principled Distribution Matching
Authors: Ruishuo Chen, Yu Chen, Zhuoran Li, Longbo Huang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-19T00:00:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:15:24+00:00
Comments: Camera-ready version accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) has emerged as a promising paradigm for eliciting the latent capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) without external supervision. However, current methods rely on heuristic intrinsic rewards, which often lack a well-defined theoretical optimization target and are prone to degenerative biases. In this work, we introduce PowerFlow, a principled framework that reformulates unsupervised fine-tuning as a distribution matching problem. By casting GFlowNet as an amortized variational sampler for unnormalized densities, we propose a length-aware Trajectory-Balance objective that explicitly neutralizes the structural length biases inherent in autoregressive generation. By targeting $α$-power distributions, PowerFlow enables the directional elicitation of the dual nature of LLMs: sharpening the distribution ($α> 1$) to intensify logical reasoning, or flattening it ($α< 1$) to unlock expressive creativity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PowerFlow consistently outperforms existing RLIF methods, matching or even exceeding supervised GRPO. Furthermore, by mitigating over-sharpening in aligned models, our approach achieves simultaneous gains in diversity and quality, shifting the Pareto frontier in creative tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PowerFlow：通过原理性分布匹配揭示大语言模型的双重特性
无监督强化学习（RLIF）通过内部反馈已成为无需外部监督即可激发大语言模型（LLMs）潜在能力的有前景范式。然而，当前方法依赖启发式内在奖励，往往缺乏明确的理论优化目标，易受退化偏差影响。本文提出PowerFlow，一个原理性框架，将无监督微调重新表述为分布匹配问题。通过将GFlowNet视为非归一化密度的近似变分采样器，我们提出一种长度感知的轨迹平衡目标，显式中和自回归生成固有的结构长度偏差。通过目标α幂分布，PowerFlow可定向激发LLMs的双重特性：当α>1时，使分布更尖锐以强化逻辑推理；当α<1时，使分布更平坦以释放创造性表达。大量实验表明，PowerFlow持续优于现有RLIF方法，表现可媲美甚至超越监督式GRPO。此外，通过缓解对齐模型的过度尖锐化，我们的方法在创意任务中同时提升多样性和质量，推动帕累托前沿的移动。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of heuristic rewards in unsupervised RLIF methods for LLMs, this work proposes PowerFlow, a principled framework that reframes unsupervised fine-tuning as distribution matching via GFlowNet as an amortized variational sampler. It introduces a length-aware Trajectory-Balance objective to neutralize structural length biases in autoregressive generation and targets α-power distributions to directionally elicit LLMs’ dual nature: sharpening (α>1) for enhanced logical reasoning or flattening (α<1) for expressive creativity. Experiments show PowerFlow outperforms existing RLIF methods, matches or exceeds supervised GRPO, and improves both diversity and quality in creative tasks by mitigating over-sharpening.
为克服当前无监督RLIF中启发式奖励缺乏理论目标和易受偏差影响的问题，本文提出PowerFlow框架，将无监督微调重构为分布匹配问题，通过将GFlowNet建模为无归一化密度的变分采样器，并引入长度感知的轨迹平衡目标以消除自回归生成的长度偏差。通过调节α幂分布，可定向激发LLM的双重能力：α>1时增强逻辑推理，α<1时激发创造性表达。实验表明，PowerFlow在多个任务上优于现有RLIF方法，可媲美或超越监督式GRPO，并在创意任务中同时提升多样性和质量，有效避免过度锐化。
Deployment-complete benchmarking
Authors: El Mustapha Mansouri, Keigo Arai
First: 2026-05-25T16:15:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:15:22+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 5 figures, 1 table; supplementary tables and code available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Benchmarks increasingly guide deployment, procurement and scientific screening, yet a score supports only the response it records, not necessarily the deployment action. We introduce deployment-complete benchmarking, which tests whether benchmark evidence determines a deployment action. A benchmark is complete for a claim exactly when the action is constant on each evidence fiber; mixed fibers expose missing deployment information, and completion curves quantify the evidence required to resolve ambiguity. In controlled response spaces, benchmark-channel conformal coverage of 94.98% transferred poorly to an unmeasured deployment channel (10.07%), whereas response-rank intervals achieved 94.91% coverage; even zero benchmark error certified only 45.4% of candidates at the largest residual size. Public audits revealed incompleteness, including 97.9% mixed Tox21 fibers and zero median certifiable fraction in main Matbench and JARVIS audits. In held-out replays, certify-then-acquire reduced false decisions from 1.19% to 0.027% in Tox21 and from 20.3% to 0.128% in JARVIS, while changing model choice and identifying deployment-relevant probes. Deployment-ready benchmarks should report evidence, supported actions, ambiguity and completion cost rather than scores alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：部署完备性基准测试
基准测试日益指导部署、采购和科学筛选，但分数仅支持其记录的响应，未必支持实际部署行动。我们引入部署完备性基准测试，检验基准证据是否决定部署行动。一个基准对某主张完备，当且仅当在每条证据纤维上行动恒定；混合纤维暴露缺失的部署信息，完成曲线量化解决歧义所需的证据量。在受控响应空间中，94.98%的基准通道一致性覆盖在未测量部署通道中表现不佳（仅10.07%），而响应秩区间达到94.91%覆盖；即使基准误差为零，仅在最大残差规模下认证了45.4%的候选方案。公开审计揭示了不完备性，包括Tox21中97.9%的混合纤维，以及主Matbench和JARVIS审计中可认证分数中位数为零。在保留集重放中，先认证后获取将Tox21和JARVIS中的错误决策率分别从1.19%降至0.027%、从20.3%降至0.128%，同时改变模型选择并识别部署相关探针。部署就绪的基准应报告证据、支持行动、歧义及完成成本，而非仅分数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between benchmark scores and actual deployment decisions, this work introduces deployment-complete benchmarking to evaluate whether benchmark evidence reliably determines deployment actions. The method defines a benchmark as complete if deployment actions are consistent across each evidence fiber; mixed fibers indicate missing information, and completion curves measure the evidence needed to resolve ambiguity. Experiments show that even benchmarks with high response-space coverage (94.98%) perform poorly in real deployment channels (10.07%), while response-rank intervals achieved 94.91% coverage. Public audits revealed widespread incompleteness, including 97.9% mixed fibers in Tox21 and zero certifiable fraction in Matbench and JARVIS. In held-out replays, the certify-then-acquire strategy reduced false decisions from 1.19% to 0.027% in Tox21 and from 20.3% to 0.128% in JARVIS, while also enabling model selection and deployment-relevant probe identification.
为弥合基准分数与实际部署决策之间的差距，本文提出部署完备性基准评估方法，旨在判断基准证据是否能可靠决定部署动作。该方法定义基准在某主张下完备，当且仅当每个证据纤维上的部署动作恒定，混合纤维暴露信息缺失，完成曲线量化消除模糊所需证据量。实验表明，即使响应覆盖率达94.98%的基准，在未测量部署通道中仅实现10.07%的预测准确率，而响应秩区间表现更优（94.91%）。公开审计发现广泛不完备性，如Tox21中97.9%纤维混合，Matbench与JARVIS中可认证比例为零。采用“先认证后获取”策略，在保留测试中将Tox21和JARVIS的错误决策率分别从1.19%降至0.027%、从20.3%降至0.128%，凸显了证据导向部署准备的重要性。
Beyond the Proxy: Trajectory-Distilled Guidance for Offline GFlowNet Training
Authors: Ruishuo Chen, Xun Wang, Rui Hu, Zhuoran Li, Longbo Huang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-05-26T15:12:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:15:16+00:00
Comments: Camera-ready version accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) excel at sampling diverse, high-reward objects. In many practical applications where active reward queries are infeasible, these models must be trained using static offline datasets. Prevailing training methods typically rely on a proxy model to provide reward feedback for online sampled trajectories. However, constructing a reliable proxy is often challenging due to data scarcity or high evaluation costs. While existing proxy-free approaches attempt to address this, they often impose coarse constraints that limit the model's ability to explore effectively. To overcome these limitations, we propose Trajectory-Distilled GFlowNet (TD-GFN), a novel proxy-free training framework. TD-GFN utilizes inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) to extract dense, transition-level edge rewards from offline trajectories, providing rich structural guidance for efficient exploration. Crucially, to ensure robustness, these rewards guide the policy indirectly through DAG pruning and prioritized backward sampling. This design ensures that gradient updates rely exclusively on ground-truth terminal rewards from the dataset, thereby preventing error propagation. Empirical results demonstrate that TD-GFN significantly outperforms a broad range of existing baselines in both convergence speed and sample quality, establishing a more robust and efficient paradigm for offline GFlowNet training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越代理：用于离线GFlowNet训练的轨迹蒸馏引导方法
生成流网络（GFlowNets）擅长采样多样且高奖励的对象。在许多实际应用中，由于主动奖励查询不可行，这些模型必须使用静态离线数据集进行训练。现有训练方法通常依赖代理模型为在线采样的轨迹提供奖励反馈。然而，由于数据稀缺或评估成本高昂，构建可靠的代理模型往往具有挑战性。尽管现有无代理方法试图解决此问题，但它们通常施加粗粒度约束，限制模型有效探索的能力。为克服这些限制，我们提出轨迹蒸馏GFlowNet（TD-GFN），一种新颖的无代理训练框架。TD-GFN利用逆强化学习（IRL）从离线轨迹中提取密集的、转移级别的边奖励，为高效探索提供丰富的结构指导。关键的是，为确保鲁棒性，这些奖励通过DAG剪枝和优先后向采样间接引导策略。该设计确保梯度更新仅依赖数据集中真实的终端奖励，从而防止误差传播。实验证明，TD-GFN在收敛速度和样本质量上显著优于广泛现有的基线方法，确立了更稳健高效的离线GFlowNet训练范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of training GFlowNets offline without reliable proxy rewards, this work introduces Trajectory-Distilled GFlowNet (TD-GFN), a proxy-free framework that leverages inverse reinforcement learning to derive fine-grained, transition-level rewards from offline trajectories. These rewards guide policy learning indirectly via DAG pruning and prioritized backward sampling, ensuring gradient updates depend solely on ground-truth terminal rewards to avoid error propagation. Experiments show TD-GFN achieves faster convergence and higher sample quality compared to existing methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in offline GFlowNet training.
为解决无代理奖励下离线训练GFlowNet的难题，本文提出轨迹蒸馏GFlowNet（TD-GFN），该框架利用逆强化学习从离线轨迹中提取密集的过渡级奖励。这些奖励通过DAG剪枝与优先逆向采样间接引导策略学习，确保梯度更新仅依赖真实终端奖励，避免误差传播。实验表明，TD-GFN在收敛速度与样本质量上显著优于现有方法，验证了其在离线GFlowNet训练中的有效性。
Shepherd: A Runtime Substrate Empowering Meta-Agents with a Formalized Execution Trace
Authors: Simon Yu, Derek Chong, Ananjan Nandi, Dilara Soylu, Jiuding Sun, Christopher D Manning, Weiyan Shi
First: 2026-05-11T17:50:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:14:14+00:00
Comments: 50 pages, 22 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As LLM agent systems take on more complex tasks, they increasingly rely on meta-agents: higher-order agents that operate on other agents, much as managers supervise employees. Whatever a meta-agent does: coordinating agents, halting risky actions before execution, or repairing failed runs, requires manipulation of agentic execution at runtime. Existing agentic substrates make this hard: they give meta-agents only plain transcripts and environment snapshots, requiring it to build it's own tooling to reconstruct and orchestrate execution state. Therefore, we introduce Shepherd, a Python substrate grounded in functional programming principles, where an agent's execution is itself a first-class object that a meta-agent can inspect and transform. Every model call, tool call, and environment change becomes a structured event in a Git-like execution trace, where any past state can be forked 5x faster than docker commit and replayed. Three example use cases show Shepherd's versatility: (1) a supervisor agent prevents conflicts among parallel coding agents, lifting CooperBench performance from 28.8% to 54.7%; (2) a counterfactual optimizer repairs agent workflows by proposing edits and replaying runs from the point of changed behavior, outperforming MetaHarness on TerminalBench-2 with 58% lower wall-clock; (3) a meta-agent picks fork points during rollouts to improve credit assignment in long-horizon agentic RL, doubling GRPO's gains on TerminalBench-2. We open-source Shepherd to empower future meta-agents with principled and efficient operations over agentic execution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Shepherd：一种支持元代理的运行时基座，具备形式化的执行轨迹
随着大语言模型代理系统承担更复杂的任务，它们越来越依赖元代理——即对其他代理进行操作的高阶代理，类似于管理者监督员工。无论元代理执行何种任务：协调代理、在执行前阻止高风险行为，或修复失败的运行，都需要在运行时操纵代理的执行状态。现有代理基座使这一过程困难：它们仅向元代理提供普通对话记录和环境快照，要求其自行构建工具以重建和编排执行状态。因此，我们引入Shepherd，一种基于函数式编程原则的Python基座，其中代理的执行本身作为一等对象，元代理可对其进行检查和转换。每一次模型调用、工具调用和环境变化都成为类似Git的结构化执行轨迹中的事件，任何历史状态均可比Docker commit快5倍进行分叉并回放。三个示例用例展示了Shepherd的多功能性：（1）监督代理防止并行编码代理间的冲突，将CooperBench性能从28.8%提升至54.7%；（2）反事实优化器通过提出修改并从行为改变点回放运行来修复代理工作流，在TerminalBench-2上比MetaHarness节省58%的壁钟时间；（3）元代理在滚动过程中选择分叉点，以改进长时域代理强化学习中的信用分配，使GRPO在TerminalBench-2上的收益翻倍。我们开源Shepherd，以赋予未来元代理以原则性和高效的操作代理执行的能力。
Summary / 总结
As LLM agent systems grow more complex, meta-agents—higher-order agents that manage or coordinate other agents—require fine-grained runtime control over execution states, which existing substrates fail to provide by only offering plain transcripts and snapshots. Shepherd addresses this by introducing a Python-based runtime substrate grounded in functional programming, where agent execution is treated as a first-class object and captured as a structured, Git-like trace of events. This enables meta-agents to inspect, fork, and replay any past state efficiently, up to five times faster than Docker commits. Experimental results demonstrate Shepherd’s effectiveness: a supervisor agent improved CooperBench performance from 28.8% to 54.7%; a counterfactual optimizer reduced wall-clock time by 58% compared to MetaHarness on TerminalBench-2; and a meta-agent doubled GRPO’s gains on TerminalBench-2 by optimizing credit assignment through strategic fork points.
随着LLM代理系统任务日益复杂，元代理（管理或协调其他代理的高阶代理）需要对运行时执行状态进行精细控制，而现有基座仅提供原始日志和快照，难以满足需求。Shepherd提出一种基于Python、以函数式编程为根基的运行时基座，将代理执行视为一等对象并以类似Git的结构化事件日志记录，使元代理可高效检查、分叉与回放执行状态——速度比Docker提交快5倍。实验表明：监督代理使CooperBench性能从28.8%提升至54.7%；反事实优化器在TerminalBench-2上比MetaHarness节省58%的运行时；元代理通过在 rollout 中选择分叉点，使GRPO在TerminalBench-2上的收益翻倍。
Fuzzy PyTorch: Rapid Numerical Variability Evaluation for Deep Learning Models
Authors: Inés Gonzalez-Pepe, Hiba Akhaddar, Tristan Glatard, Yohan Chatelain
First: 2026-05-25T16:10:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:10:21+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 8 figures, Published in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (01/2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Fuzzy PyTorch, a framework for rapid evaluation of numerical variability in deep learning (DL) models. As DL is increasingly applied to diverse tasks, understanding variability from floating-point arithmetic is essential to ensure robust and reliable performance. Tools assessing such variability must be scalable, efficient, and integrate seamlessly with existing frameworks while minimizing code modifications. Fuzzy PyTorch enables this by integrating stochastic arithmetic into PyTorch through Probabilistic Rounding with Instruction Set Management, a novel library interfacing with Verificarlo, a numerical analysis compiler. The library offers stochastic rounding mode and a novel mode; up-down rounding. Comparative evaluations show Fuzzy PyTorch maintains model performance and achieves runtime reductions of 5x to 60x versus Verrou, a state-of-the-art tool. We further demonstrate scalability by running models from 1 to 341 million parameters, confirming applicability across small and large DL architectures. Overall, Fuzzy PyTorch provides an efficient, scalable, and practical solution for assessing numerical variability in deep learning, enabling researchers and practitioners to quantify and manage floating-point uncertainty without compromising performance or computational efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模糊PyTorch：深度学习模型数值变异性快速评估框架
我们提出了Fuzzy PyTorch，一个用于快速评估深度学习（DL）模型数值变异性框架。随着DL在多样化任务中的广泛应用，理解浮点运算带来的变异性对确保模型稳健可靠性能至关重要。评估此类变异性的工具必须具备可扩展性、高效性，并能无缝集成现有框架，同时最小化代码修改。Fuzzy PyTorch通过概率舍入与指令集管理（Probabilistic Rounding with Instruction Set Management）将随机算术集成到PyTorch中，该库与数值分析编译器Verificarlo接口。该库提供随机舍入模式及一种新颖的上下舍入模式。对比评估显示，Fuzzy PyTorch在保持模型性能的同时，相较于最先进的工具Verrou，运行速度提升5至60倍。我们进一步通过运行参数量从1百万到3.41亿的模型，验证其在小型和大型DL架构中的可扩展性。总体而言，Fuzzy PyTorch为评估深度学习中的数值变异性提供了一种高效、可扩展且实用的解决方案，使研究人员和从业者能够在不牺牲性能或计算效率的前提下量化和管理浮点不确定性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate floating-point numerical variability in deep learning models for robust performance, Fuzzy PyTorch integrates stochastic arithmetic into PyTorch via Probabilistic Rounding with Instruction Set Management, interfacing with Verificarlo for numerical analysis. The framework introduces stochastic rounding and a novel up-down rounding mode, enabling efficient variability assessment without altering model code. Experimental results show Fuzzy PyTorch maintains model accuracy while achieving 5x to 60x faster runtime than Verrou, the current state-of-the-art, and scales effectively from 1 to 341 million parameters, validating its applicability across diverse model sizes.
为评估深度学习模型中浮点数计算带来的数值变异性以确保性能稳健性，Fuzzy PyTorch 通过概率舍入与指令集管理将随机算术集成到 PyTorch 中，并与数值分析编译器 Verificarlo 集成。该框架引入随机舍入和新型上下舍入模式，可在不大幅修改代码的前提下高效评估数值不确定性。实验表明，Fuzzy PyTorch 在保持模型精度的同时，比当前最先进的工具 Verrou 快 5 倍至 60 倍，并可从 100 万到 3.41 亿参数规模的模型中稳定运行，验证其在不同规模模型中的适用性。
Ineffectiveness for Search and Undecidability of PCSP Meta-Problems
Authors: Alberto Larrauri
First: 2025-04-06T23:08:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:07:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It is an open question whether the search and decision versions of promise CSPs are equivalent. Most known algorithms for PCSPs solve only their \emph{decision} variant, and it is unknown whether they can be adapted to solve \emph{search} as well. The main approaches, called BLP, AIP and BLP+AIP, handle a PCSP by finding a solution to a relaxation of some integer program. We prove that rounding those solutions to a proper search certificate can be as hard as any problem in the class TFNP. In other words, these algorithms are ineffective for search. Building on the algebraic approach to PCSPs, we find sufficient conditions that imply ineffectiveness for search. Our tools are tailored to algorithms that are characterized by minions in a suitable way, and can also be used to prove undecidability results for meta-problems. This way, we show that the families of templates solvable via BLP, AIP, and BLP+AIP are undecidable. Using the same techniques we also analyze several algebraic conditions that are known to guarantee the tractability of finite-template CSPs. We prove that several meta-problems related to cyclic polymorphims and WNUs are undecidable for PCSPs. In particular, there is no algorithm deciding whether a finite PCSP template (1) admits cyclic a polymorphism, (2) admits a WNU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PCSP元问题的搜索无效性与不可判定性
承诺约束满足问题（PCSP）的搜索与决策版本等价性仍属开放问题。目前已知的大多数PCSP算法仅解决其决策变体，尚不清楚它们能否扩展至搜索问题。主要方法BLP、AIP及BLP+AIP通过求解整数规划的松弛问题来处理PCSP。我们证明，将这些松弛解四舍五入为有效搜索证书的难度至少与TFNP类中的任何问题相当，即这些算法对搜索问题无效。基于PCSP的代数方法，我们找到若干充分条件，可推导出搜索无效性。我们的工具针对由适当方式刻画的“小群”算法，并可用于证明元问题的不可判定性。由此，我们证明通过BLP、AIP及BLP+AIP可解的模板族是不可判定的。使用相同技术，我们还分析若干已知保证有限模板CSP可解性的代数条件，证明与循环同态及WNUs相关的若干元问题对PCSP不可判定。特别是，不存在算法可判定有限PCSP模板是否（1）存在循环同态，（2）存在WNU。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the open question of whether search and decision versions of promise CSPs (PCSPs) are equivalent, the paper investigates whether known algorithms—BLP, AIP, and BLP+AIP—can solve the search variant. These methods rely on relaxing integer programs and rounding solutions to obtain certificates; the authors prove this rounding is as hard as any problem in TFNP, rendering these algorithms ineffective for search. Building on algebraic tools tied to minions, they establish sufficient conditions for search ineffectiveness and demonstrate that the families of templates solvable by these algorithms are undecidable. Further, they show that meta-problems concerning cyclic polymorphisms and WNUs for PCSPs are undecidable, including the impossibility of deciding whether a finite PCSP template admits such structures.
该论文探讨了承诺约束满足问题（PCSP）的搜索与决策版本是否等价，指出当前多数算法仅能解决决策版本。研究证明，将整数规划松弛解四舍五入为有效搜索证书的难度等同于TFNP类中任意问题，因此这些算法对搜索问题无效。基于代数框架和“minion”结构，作者建立了搜索无效性的充分条件，并证明通过BLP、AIP和BLP+AIP可解的模板族是不可判定的。此外，研究还表明，与循环同态和WNU相关的若干代数元问题在PCSP中也是不可判定的，包括无法判定有限PCSP模板是否具备此类结构。
Branch Scaling Manifests as Implicit Architectural Regularization for Improving Generalization in Overparameterized ResNets
Authors: Zixiong Yu, Guhan Chen, Jianfa Lai, Bohan Li, Songtao Tian
Venue: ICML
First: 2024-03-07T14:40:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:06:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML. This version incorporates content from the preprint arXiv:2305.18506. The contributors of the relevant content have consented to its inclusion and have been listed as authors
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling factors in residual branches have emerged as a prevalent method for boosting neural network performance, especially in normalization-free architectures. While prior work has primarily examined scaling effects from an optimization perspective, this paper investigates their role in residual architectures through the lens of generalization theory. Specifically, we establish that wide residual networks (ResNets) with constant scaling factors become asymptotically unlearnable as depth increases. In contrast, when the scaling factor exhibits rapid depth-wise decay combined with early stopping, over-parameterized ResNets achieve minimax-optimal generalization rates. To establish this, we demonstrate that the generalization capability of wide ResNets can be approximated by kernel regression associated with the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK). Our theoretical findings are validated through experiments on synthetic data and real-world classification tasks, including MNIST and CIFAR-100.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分支缩放作为隐式架构正则化，用于提升过参数化ResNet的泛化能力
残差分支中的缩放因子已成为提升神经网络性能的常用方法，尤其在无归一化架构中。虽然先前研究主要从优化角度考察缩放效应，本文则从泛化理论视角研究其在残差架构中的作用。具体而言，我们证明：具有恒定缩放因子的宽残差网络（ResNet）随着深度增加会渐近不可学习；相反，当缩放因子呈现快速深度衰减并结合早期停止时，过参数化ResNet可实现最小最大最优泛化率。为证明此结论，我们表明宽ResNet的泛化能力可近似为与神经切线核（NTK）相关的核回归。我们的理论结果通过合成数据和真实分类任务（包括MNIST和CIFAR-100）的实验得到验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that branch scaling improves performance in normalization-free ResNets, this work investigates its role in generalization rather than optimization. The authors show that constant scaling in wide ResNets leads to asymptotic unlearnability with increasing depth, while rapid depth-wise decay of scaling factors combined with early stopping enables minimax-optimal generalization. Theoretical analysis links generalization to kernel regression under the Neural Tangent Kernel, and experiments on synthetic data and real-world tasks like MNIST and CIFAR-100 confirm that scaling-induced architectural regularization enhances generalization in overparameterized networks.
鉴于残差网络中分支缩放在实践中效果显著，本文从泛化理论角度研究其作用。作者发现，宽残差网络中恒定缩放因子会导致深度增加时渐近不可学习，而缩放因子随深度快速衰减并结合早停策略可实现最小最大最优泛化率。通过基于神经切线核的核回归框架，他们理论推导了泛化能力，并在合成数据及MNIST、CIFAR-100等真实任务上验证了结论。
What Makes a Medical Checker Trainable? Diagnosing Signal Collapse and Reward Hacking in Checker-Guided RAG for Biomedical QA
Authors: Yuelyu Ji, Min Gu Kwak, Hang Zhang, Xizhi Wu, Chenyu Li, Yanshan Wan
First: 2026-05-25T16:06:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:06:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical RAG needs evidence-grounded claims, so plugging a claim-level NLI checker into retrieval-augmented RL is intuitive. \textbf{We find that the checker's \emph{output distribution} during training, not its held-out accuracy, decides whether it provides trainable gradient.} We compare four NLI checker back-ends as process rewards inside a GRPO-trained medical RAG agent (Qwen2.5-7B, replicated on Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B) across four held-out medical QA benchmarks. Three diagnostic findings emerge. \textbf{(i)} Signal collapse is log-prob-specific: LLM log-probability scoring labels over 97\% of claims neutral -- collapsing the RL gradient to zero -- while a calibrated MedNLI classifier scores the same pairs non-degenerately. \textbf{(ii)} Moderate signal beats strong signal on answer quality: a strong proprietary checker triggers a three-step reward-hacking cascade -- ultra-short answers, search avoidance, language collapse -- so a moderate-signal local classifier trains a higher-quality model (\textbf{+12\% BERTScore over zero-shot, no GPT dependency}). \textbf{(iii)} Signal strength is policy-dependent: the same checker registers as moderate on one policy but strong on another without triggering the cascade end-state. We frame these as boundary conditions for verifier-as-reward systems.

标题：什么使医学检查器可训练？诊断检查器引导的生物医学问答RAG中的信号崩溃与奖励欺骗
医学RAG需要证据支撑的陈述，因此在检索增强强化学习中接入陈述级NLI检查器是直观的。\textbf{我们发现，检查器在训练期间的\emph{输出分布}，而非其验证集准确率，决定了其是否提供可训练梯度。} 我们在四个验证集医学问答基准上，比较了四种NLI检查器后端作为过程奖励，用于训练GRPO医学RAG代理（Qwen2.5-7B，已在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上复现）。得出三项诊断发现。\textbf{(i)} 信号崩溃具有对数概率特异性：LLM对数概率评分使超过97\%的陈述被标记为中性——导致强化学习梯度归零——而校准的MedNLI分类器对相同配对评分非退化。\textbf{(ii)} 中等信号优于强信号在答案质量上：强专有检查器触发三步奖励欺骗级联——超短答案、搜索回避、语言崩溃——因此中等信号本地分类器训练出更高质量模型（\textbf{零样本BERTScore提升+12\%，无需GPT依赖}）。\textbf{(iii)} 信号强度依赖策略：同一检查器在一种策略下表现为中等信号，而在另一种策略下表现为强信号，且不触发级联终态。我们将这些视为验证器作为奖励系统的边界条件。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why medical RAG systems fail to train effectively when using claim-level NLI checkers as rewards, finding that the checker's output distribution during training—not its accuracy—determines whether it provides useful gradients. The researchers evaluated four NLI backends within a GRPO-trained RAG agent (Qwen2.5-7B, replicated on Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B) across four medical QA benchmarks, uncovering three key findings: (i) signal collapse occurs when LLM log-prob scoring labels over 97% of claims as neutral, collapsing gradients to zero, whereas a calibrated MedNLI classifier avoids this; (ii) moderate signal strength yields better answer quality than strong signal, as strong proprietary checkers trigger a reward-hacking cascade (ultra-short answers, search avoidance, language collapse), while a moderate local classifier improves BERTScore by 12% over zero-shot without GPT dependency; (iii) signal strength is policy-dependent, as the same checker can be moderate under one policy and strong under another without triggering the cascade. 该研究探讨了为何在医疗RAG系统中使用NLI检查器作为奖励信号时训练效果不佳，发现决定梯度是否可用的是检查器在训练中的输出分布而非其准确率。研究人员在四个医疗问答基准上，将四个NLI后端嵌入GRPO训练的RAG代理（Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.1-8B），得出三个关键发现：(i) 信号崩溃与LLM对声明的对数概率评分相关，其将超过97%的声明标记为中性，导致梯度归零，而校准后的MedNLI分类器则能避免此问题；(ii) 中等强度信号比强信号更优，强检查器会触发三阶段奖励作弊链——超短答案、搜索回避、语言崩溃，而中等信号本地分类器可使BERTScore比零样本基线提升12%，且无需GPT依赖；(iii) 信号强度依赖于策略，同一检查器在不同策略下可能表现为中等或强信号，且不触发作弊终点。这些发现为使用验证器作为奖励的RL系统提供了关键边界条件。
On the Communication Complexity of Decentralized Stochastic Bilevel Optimization
Authors: Yihan Zhang, My T. Thai, Jie Wu, Hongchang Gao
First: 2023-11-19T14:56:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:06:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic bilevel optimization finds widespread applications in machine learning, including meta-learning, hyperparameter optimization, and neural architecture search. To extend stochastic bilevel optimization to distributed data, several decentralized stochastic bilevel optimization algorithms have been developed. However, existing methods often suffer from slow convergence rates and high communication costs in heterogeneous settings, limiting their applicability to real-world tasks. To address these issues, we propose two novel decentralized stochastic bilevel gradient descent algorithms based on \textit{simultaneous} and \textit{alternating} update strategies. Our algorithms can achieve faster convergence rates and lower communication costs than existing methods. Importantly, our convergence analyses do not rely on strong assumptions regarding heterogeneity. More importantly, our theoretical analyses clearly disclose how the computation and communication regarding the Hessian-inverse-vector product under the heterogeneous setting affects the convergence rate. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time such favorable theoretical results have been achieved with mild assumptions in the heterogeneous setting. Furthermore, we demonstrate how to establish the convergence rate for the alternating update strategy when combined with the variance-reduced gradient. Finally, experimental results confirm the efficacy of our algorithms.

标题：去中心化随机双层优化的通信复杂度研究

Motivated by the need to efficiently solve stochastic bilevel optimization problems across decentralized, heterogeneous data, this paper proposes two novel decentralized algorithms using simultaneous and alternating update strategies. The methods aim to reduce communication overhead and accelerate convergence without requiring strong assumptions about data heterogeneity. Theoretical analysis reveals how Hessian-inverse-vector computations under heterogeneity impact convergence, and the alternating strategy is shown to converge when paired with variance-reduced gradients. Experiments validate that the proposed algorithms achieve faster convergence and lower communication costs compared to existing decentralized methods.
为解决分布式异构数据环境下随机双层优化收敛慢、通信成本高的问题，本文提出两种基于同步与交替更新策略的新型分布式算法。该方法在理论分析中无需强异构假设，明确揭示了Hessian逆向量乘法在异构环境中的计算如何影响收敛速度，并首次在弱假设下实现更优理论收敛率。实验结果证实，所提算法在实际场景中收敛更快、通信开销更低。
Efficient and Scalable Neural Symbolic Search for Knowledge Graph Complex Query Answering
Authors: Weizhi Fei, Zihao Wang, hang Yin, Shukai Zhao, Wei Zhang, Yangqiu Song
First: 2025-05-13T01:24:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:05:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Complex Query Answering (CQA) is a crucial reasoning task over Knowledge Graphs (KGs), which aims to answer first-order logical queries from incomplete KGs. While existing neural-symbolic methods achieve strong performance, they face significant complexity bottlenecks: quadratic data complexity scaling with the number of entities, and NP-hard query complexity for cyclic queries. Consequently, these approaches struggle to scale effectively to large knowledge graphs and complex queries. To address these limitations, we propose an efficient and scalable symbolic search method comprising two key components: (1) constraint strategies that drastically reduce the variable search domain, lowering data complexity; and (2) a local search algorithm that approximately solves NP-hard cyclic queries. Experiments on various CQA benchmarks demonstrate that, for tree-form queries, our method achieves 97% relative MRR with a 10$\times$ speedup using only 10% of the search space. Furthermore, it demonstrates robust performance on complex cyclic queries and large-scale KGs, effectively alleviating efficiency and scalability challenges. Our code is provided in https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/NLISA_KDD2026.

Uncertainty-Guided Appearance-Motion Association Network for Out-of-Distribution Action Detection
Authors: Xiang Fang, Arvind Easwaran, Blaise Genest
First: 2024-09-16T02:53:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:05:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted by MIPR 2024
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection targets to detect and reject test samples with semantic shifts, to prevent models trained on in-distribution (ID) dataset from producing unreliable predictions. Existing works only extract the appearance features on image datasets, and cannot handle dynamic multimedia scenarios with much motion information. Therefore, we target a more realistic and challenging OOD detection task: OOD action detection (ODAD). Given an untrimmed video, ODAD first classifies the ID actions and recognizes the OOD actions, and then localizes ID and OOD actions. To this end, in this paper, we propose a novel Uncertainty-Guided Appearance-Motion Association Network (UAAN), which explores both appearance features and motion contexts to reason spatial-temporal inter-object interaction for ODAD.Firstly, we design separate appearance and motion branches to extract corresponding appearance-oriented and motion-aspect object representations. In each branch, we construct a spatial-temporal graph to reason appearance-guided and motion-driven inter-object interaction. Then, we design an appearance-motion attention module to fuse the appearance and motion features for final action detection. Experimental results on two challenging datasets show that UAAN beats state-of-the-art methods by a significant margin, illustrating its effectiveness.

标题：基于不确定性的外观-运动关联网络用于分布外动作检测
分布外（OOD）检测旨在检测并拒绝语义发生偏移的测试样本，以防止在分布内（ID）数据集上训练的模型产生不可靠的预测。现有方法仅从图像数据集中提取外观特征，无法处理包含大量运动信息的动态多媒体场景。因此，我们针对更具现实性和挑战性的OOD检测任务——分布外动作检测（ODAD）：给定一个未剪辑的视频，ODAD首先分类ID动作并识别OOD动作，然后定位ID和OOD动作。为此，本文提出了一种新颖的不确定性引导的外观-运动关联网络（UAAN），该网络同时探索外观特征和运动上下文，以推理时空对象间交互关系用于ODAD。首先，我们设计独立的外观和运动分支，分别提取面向外观和运动方面的对象表示。在每个分支中，我们构建时空图以推理外观引导和运动驱动的对象间交互。然后，我们设计外观-运动注意力模块，融合外观和运动特征以实现最终的动作检测。在两个具有挑战性的数据集上的实验结果表明，UAAN显著优于现有最先进方法，证明了其有效性。
