Neural Scalable Symbolic Search Framework for Complex Logical Queries with Multiple Free Variables
Authors: Weizhi Fei, Hang Yin, Zihao Wang, Shukai Zhao, Wei Zhang, Yangqiu Song
First: 2026-05-25T16:04:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:04:57+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Complex Query Answering (CQA) is a fundamental knowledge representation and reasoning task over incomplete knowledge graphs (KGs). Answering existential first-order queries with $k$ free variables (i.e., $\text{EFO}_k$ queries) is a crucial yet challenging problem, as it requires ranking answer tuples in $\mathcal{E}^k$, where $\mathcal{E}$ denotes the entity set of a KG. This quickly becomes intractable as $k$ grows. Consequently, existing benchmarks and methods rely on marginal rankings over individual variables; however, marginal rankings are a poor proxy for the true joint ranking of tuples. Building on neural symbolic search for $\text{EFO}_1$ queries, we propose Neural Scalable Symbolic Search (NS3), a budgeted framework that approximates joint ranking without enumerating $\mathcal{E}^k$. NS3 (i) answers marginalized sub-queries to obtain necessary candidate sets, (ii) merges multiple free variables into hypernodes whose domains are pruned and controlled by a dynamic budget $B$, and (iii) progressively reduces an $\text{EFO}_k$ query to an $\text{EFO}_{k-1}$ query over a budgeted reduced domain. Across three standard KG datasets, NS3 substantially improves joint ranking performance while retaining strong marginal accuracy. We further release a joint-ranking benchmark that extends existing $\text{EFO}_1$ datasets to $k=3$, enabling systematic evaluation of multi-variable queries. Our code is provided in https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/NS3_KDD2026.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向含多个自由变量的复杂逻辑查询的神经可扩展符号搜索框架
复杂查询回答（CQA）是针对不完整知识图谱（KG）的基本知识表示与推理任务。回答含k个自由变量的存在量词一阶逻辑查询（即EFO_k查询）是一个关键但具有挑战性的问题，因其需对实体集E的k次幂空间E^k中的答案元组进行排序。随着k增大，该问题迅速变得不可行。因此，现有基准与方法依赖于对单个变量的边际排序；然而，边际排序难以准确反映元组的真实联合排序。基于神经符号搜索方法对EFO_1查询的改进，我们提出神经可扩展符号搜索（NS3），一种预算控制框架，可在不枚举E^k的前提下近似联合排序。NS3（i）通过回答边际子查询获取必要候选集；（ii）将多个自由变量合并为超节点，其域由动态预算B修剪与控制；（iii）逐步将EFO_k查询降维为预算受限域上的EFO_{k-1}查询。在三个标准KG数据集上，NS3显著提升联合排序性能，同时保持强边际准确性。我们进一步发布联合排序基准，将现有EFO_1数据集扩展至k=3，支持多变量查询的系统性评估。代码见https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/NS3_KDD2026。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational intractability of joint ranking for existential first-order queries with multiple free variables in incomplete knowledge graphs, the paper introduces NS3, a neural scalable symbolic search framework that avoids full enumeration of candidate tuples. NS3 answers marginalized sub-queries to gather candidate sets, merges free variables into pruned hypernodes under a dynamic budget, and iteratively reduces the query complexity from k to k-1 over a controlled domain. Experiments on three standard knowledge graph datasets show that NS3 significantly improves joint ranking performance while maintaining strong marginal accuracy, and the authors release a new benchmark extending existing datasets to k=3 for multi-variable query evaluation.
为了解决在不完整知识图谱上对含多个自由变量的存疑一阶查询进行联合排序的计算不可行问题，本文提出NS3框架，通过神经符号搜索实现可扩展的近似联合排序。NS3先通过边际子查询获取候选集，将多个自由变量合并为受动态预算控制的超节点，并逐步将k变量查询递归降维为k-1变量查询。在三个标准知识图谱数据集上的实验表明，NS3显著提升了联合排序性能，同时保持边际排序精度，并发布了扩展至k=3的联合排序基准以支持多变量查询评估。
SafeCtrl-RL: Inference-Time Adaptive Behaviour Control for LLM Dialogue via RL-Driven Prompt Optimisation
Authors: Michael Orme, Yanchao Yu, Zhiyuan Tan
First: 2026-05-25T16:03:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:03:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ensuring safe and contextually appropriate behaviour in Large Language Models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge for real-world deployment. We present \textbf{SafeCtrl-RL}, an inference-time behavioural control framework that enables adaptive safety regulation without model retraining or parameter modification. The method formulates dialogue generation as a sequential decision process, where a reinforcement learning agent dynamically selects prompt adjustment strategies based on contextual feedback. This allows unsafe behaviours to be suppressed through iterative refinement, which we conceptualise as inference-time behavioural unlearning. Evaluated across multiple LLMs and unsafe dialogue scenarios, SafeCtrl-RL consistently improves safety and response quality, outperforms existing prompt-based optimisation methods, and achieves favourable performance--efficiency trade-offs. **Warning: This paper may contain examples of harmful language, and reader discretion is recommended.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SafeCtrl-RL：通过强化学习驱动的提示优化实现大语言模型对话的推理时自适应行为控制
确保大语言模型（LLMs）在现实部署中表现出安全且语境恰当的行为仍是一个关键挑战。我们提出了**SafeCtrl-RL**，一种推理时行为控制框架，可在不重新训练模型或修改参数的情况下实现自适应安全调节。该方法将对话生成建模为序列决策过程，其中强化学习代理根据上下文反馈动态选择提示调整策略，从而通过迭代优化抑制不安全行为，我们将其概念化为推理时的行为去学习。在多个LLMs和不安全对话场景的评估中，SafeCtrl-RL持续提升安全性与响应质量，优于现有基于提示的优化方法，并实现良好的性能-效率权衡。**警告：本文可能包含有害语言示例，建议读者谨慎阅读。**
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of ensuring safe and contextually appropriate dialogue generation in LLMs without retraining, SafeCtrl-RL introduces an inference-time adaptive control framework that uses reinforcement learning to dynamically adjust prompts based on contextual feedback. The method treats dialogue generation as a sequential decision process, enabling the system to iteratively refine outputs and suppress unsafe behaviors through prompt optimization, effectively performing inference-time behavioral unlearning. Experimental results across multiple LLMs and unsafe scenarios show consistent improvements in safety and response quality, surpassing existing prompt-based methods while maintaining favorable performance-efficiency trade-offs.
为解决大语言模型在实际部署中对话安全与语境适配性不足的问题，SafeCtrl-RL 提出一种推理时动态调整提示的控制框架，无需重训练或修改模型参数。该方法将对话生成建模为序列决策过程，通过强化学习代理根据上下文反馈动态选择提示调整策略，以迭代方式抑制不安全行为，称为推理时行为卸载。实验表明，在多个大模型和不安全对话场景下，该方法显著提升安全性与回复质量，优于现有提示优化方法，同时保持良好的性能-效率平衡。
DeepEN: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Framework for Personalized Enteral Nutrition in Critical Care
Authors: Daniel Jason Tan, Jiayang Chen, Dilruk Perera, Kay Choong See, Mengling Feng
First: 2025-10-09T15:37:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T16:01:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Objective: Enteral nutrition (EN) delivery in the ICU remains suboptimal due to limited personalization and uncertainty regarding appropriate calorie, protein, and fluid targets under dynamic metabolic demands. We introduce DeepEN, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for personalized EN optimization using electronic health record data. Methods: DeepEN was trained on over 11,000 ICU patients from MIMIC-IV to generate 4-hourly, patient-specific caloric, protein, and fluid targets. The state representation incorporated demographics, comorbidities, vital signs, laboratory values, and recent interventions. A physiologically aligned reward framework balanced biomarker stability with long-term survival. Policy learning employed a dueling double deep Q-network with Conservative Q-Learning regularization to enable safe offline training. Results: DeepEN achieved the highest estimated policy value ($V^π= 9.48$) and the lowest calibrated mortality (18.8 +/- 1.0%), representing a 4.0 percentage-point absolute reduction compared with clinician practice (22.8%). The policy also demonstrated superior metabolic stability, achieving the highest proportion of glucose, phosphate, and sodium values within target range. Furthermore, deviation from the DeepEN policy was independently associated with increased mortality and biomarker instability, whereas deviation from a random policy showed no such association. Interpretability analyses further indicated that recommendations were conditioned on physiologically relevant markers of organ function and metabolic status rather than static dosing heuristics. Conclusion: DeepEN demonstrates the feasibility of conservative offline RL for safe, individualized EN optimization, highlighting the potential of data-driven personalization to complement guideline-based approaches in critical care.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeepEN：用于重症监护个性化肠内营养的深度强化学习框架
目的：重症监护病房（ICU）中肠内营养（EN）的给药仍存在个性化不足，且在动态代谢需求下难以确定合适的热量、蛋白质和液体目标。我们提出了DeepEN，一个基于电子健康记录数据的强化学习（RL）框架，用于个性化EN优化。 方法：DeepEN在MIMIC-IV数据库中超过11,000名ICU患者数据上训练，生成每4小时一次、针对个体患者的热量、蛋白质和液体目标。状态表示包含人口统计学、合并症、生命体征、实验室指标和近期干预措施。生理学对齐的奖励框架平衡了生物标志物稳定性与长期生存率。策略学习采用带保守Q学习正则化的双Dueling Deep Q网络，以实现安全的离线训练。 结果：DeepEN实现了最高的估计策略价值（V^π=9.48）和最低的校准死亡率（18.8±1.0%），相比临床实践（22.8%）绝对降低4.0个百分点。该策略还表现出更优的代谢稳定性，实现葡萄糖、磷酸盐和钠值在目标范围内的比例最高。此外，偏离DeepEN策略与死亡率升高和生物标志物不稳定显著相关，而偏离随机策略则无此关联。可解释性分析进一步表明，推荐基于生理相关的器官功能和代谢状态指标，而非静态给药经验法则。 结论：DeepEN证明了保守离线强化学习在安全、个性化EN优化中的可行性，突显了数据驱动个性化在重症监护中补充指南方法的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the suboptimal personalization of enteral nutrition in ICU settings due to uncertain metabolic targets, DeepEN employs a deep reinforcement learning framework trained on 11,000 ICU patients from MIMIC-IV to generate personalized 4-hourly caloric, protein, and fluid recommendations. The model uses a state representation combining demographics, vital signs, lab values, and interventions, with a reward function prioritizing biomarker stability and long-term survival. Trained with a dueling double DQN and Conservative Q-Learning for safe offline learning, DeepEN achieved the highest policy value (V^π = 9.48) and lowest calibrated mortality (18.8% ± 1.0%), a 4.0 percentage-point reduction compared to clinician practice (22.8%). It also showed superior metabolic stability across key biomarkers and demonstrated that deviations from its policy correlated with increased mortality and instability, while random deviations did not.
为解决重症监护中肠内营养缺乏个性化、难以适应动态代谢需求的问题，DeepEN采用深度强化学习框架，基于11,000名ICU患者数据，生成每4小时的个性化热量、蛋白质与液体目标。该方法通过整合人口学、生命体征、实验室指标和干预措施构建状态表示，并以生物标志物稳定性和长期生存率作为奖励函数。策略学习采用双DQN对抗网络结合保守Q学习，确保离线训练安全。实验结果显示，DeepEN获得最高策略价值（V^π = 9.48）和最低校准死亡率（18.8%），较临床实践（22.8%）降低4.0个百分点，并显著提升代谢稳定性，且偏离其策略与死亡率升高、生物标志物波动显著相关。
Discrete diffusion samplers and bridges: Off-policy algorithms and applications in latent spaces
Authors: Arran Carter, Sanghyeok Choi, Kirill Tamogashev, Víctor Elvira, Nikolay Malkin
First: 2026-02-05T18:16:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-25T15:58:02+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/mmacosha/offpolicy-discrete-diffusion-samplers-and-bridges
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sampling from a distribution $p(x) \propto e^{-\mathcal{E}(x)}$ known up to a normalising constant is an important and challenging problem in statistics. Recent years have seen the rise of a new family of amortised sampling algorithms, commonly referred to as diffusion samplers, that enable fast and efficient sampling from an unnormalised density. Such algorithms have been widely studied for continuous-space sampling tasks; however, their application to problems in discrete space remains largely unexplored. Although some progress has been made in this area, discrete diffusion samplers do not take full advantage of ideas commonly used for continuous-space sampling. In this paper, we propose to bridge this gap by introducing off-policy training techniques for discrete diffusion samplers. We show that these techniques improve the performance of discrete samplers on both established and new synthetic benchmarks. Next, we generalise discrete diffusion samplers to the task of bridging between two arbitrary distributions, introducing data-to-energy Schrödinger bridge training for the discrete domain for the first time. Lastly, we showcase the application of the proposed diffusion samplers to data-free posterior sampling in the discrete latent spaces of image generative models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离散扩散采样器与桥接：离策略算法及其在潜在空间中的应用
从已知归一化常数的分布 $p(x) \propto e^{-\mathcal{E}(x)}$ 中进行采样是统计学中一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。近年来，一种新的自适应采样算法——通常称为扩散采样器——兴起，能够快速高效地从非归一化密度中采样。这类算法已在连续空间采样任务中得到广泛研究；然而，其在离散空间问题中的应用仍鲜有探索。尽管该领域已取得一些进展，离散扩散采样器尚未充分利用连续空间采样中常见的思想。本文通过引入离策略训练技术来弥合这一差距，展示了这些技术在现有及新合成基准测试中显著提升了离散采样器的性能。接着，我们将离散扩散采样器推广至在任意两个分布之间进行桥接的任务，并首次在离散域中引入数据到能量的薛定谔桥训练方法。最后，我们展示了所提出的扩散采样器在图像生成模型离散潜在空间中的无数据后验采样应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend diffusion-based sampling methods to discrete spaces, this paper introduces off-policy training techniques to enhance discrete diffusion samplers, enabling more effective sampling from unnormalized distributions. The method adapts continuous-space diffusion strategies to discrete domains by leveraging off-policy learning, improving performance on both synthetic benchmarks and real-world tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms existing discrete samplers and successfully extends diffusion samplers to solve Schrödinger bridge problems between arbitrary distributions, including applications in data-free posterior sampling within discrete latent spaces of image generative models.
为将扩散采样方法扩展至离散空间，本文提出离策略训练技术以提升离散扩散采样器的性能，使其更有效地从非归一化分布中采样。作者进一步将该方法推广至任意分布间的桥接任务，首次在离散域中引入数据到能量的薛定谔桥训练框架。实验结果表明，该方法在合成基准和图像生成模型的离散潜在空间中的数据无关后验采样任务上均表现更优。
